## Comprehensive Codebase Overview: ElixirTrials

Based on my thorough exploration of the medgemma-hackathon repository, here's a complete analysis of the project structure, components, and key implementations.

### 1. OVERALL PROJECT STRUCTURE

**Project Type:** Monorepo for AI-powered clinical trial eligibility criteria extraction and grounding

**Tech Stack:**
- Backend: Python 3.12+ with FastAPI, LangGraph, SQLModel, Alembic
- Frontend: React 18 + Vite + Tailwind CSS + Radix UI (Node.js 20+)
- Package Manager: uv (modern Python package manager)
- Database: PostgreSQL 16
- Observability: MLflow 3.9
- LLM: Gemini 2.5 Flash (Google AI or Vertex AI)
- Terminology: UMLS API, OMOP CDM, SNOMED CT, RxNorm, ICD-10, LOINC, HPO

**Directory Structure:**
```
medgemma-hackathon/
├── services/
│   ├── api-service/              # FastAPI orchestrator (4,288 lines)
│   └── protocol-processor-service/  # LangGraph pipeline
├── libs/
│   ├── shared/                   # SQLModel domain models
│   ├── inference/                # Model loading, Jinja2 prompts, factory patterns
│   ├── data-pipeline/            # ETL and data preparation
│   ├── evaluation/               # Quality metrics and benchmarks
│   ├── events-py/                # Transactional outbox pattern
│   └── model-training/           # Fine-tuning and distillation utilities
├── apps/
│   └── hitl-ui/                  # React/Vite UI for review
├── infra/                        # Docker Compose configuration
├── docs/                         # MkDocs site with architecture diagrams
├── tests/                        # E2E tests with pytest
├── scripts/                      # Utility scripts
└── data/                         # Sample protocols for testing
```

---

### 2. EXTRACTION-GROUNDING LOOP IMPLEMENTATION

**Location:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/`

The core pipeline is a **7-node LangGraph StateGraph** with PostgreSQL checkpointing for fault tolerance:

#### Pipeline Flow:
```
START → ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve → END
```

#### Node Details:

**1. Ingest Node** (76 lines)
- Downloads PDF from GCS or local storage
- Populates `pdf_bytes` in state
- Returns file metadata

**2. Extract Node** (73 lines) - `/nodes/extract.py`
- Thin orchestration wrapper
- Delegates to `gemini_extractor` tool
- Uses Gemini File API with structured output (ExtractionResult schema)
- Returns `extraction_json` as JSON string (minimizes state size)
- MLflow tracing with inputs/outputs

**3. Parse Node** (205 lines) - `/nodes/parse.py`
- Parses extraction JSON into ExtractionResult Pydantic model
- Creates `CriteriaBatch` and `Criteria` records in DB
- Decomposes criteria into discrete medical entities
- Builds `entities_json` with criterion_id threading
- Supports dev knobs: `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA` for testing
- Clears pdf_bytes from state after extraction (optimization)

**4. Ground Node** (595 lines) - `/nodes/ground.py` [CRITICAL]
- **Most complex node** - implements agentic grounding loop
- **Dual grounding approach:**
  - TerminologyRouter: Routes entities to UMLS/SNOMED/etc. via MCP adapters
  - OMOP mapper: Parallel lookup in OMOP vocabulary
  - Reconciliation: Fuzzy matching to detect agreement/disagreement
- **Agentic retry loop** (max 3 attempts):
  1. Initial routing via TerminologyRouter
  2. MedGemma selects best match from candidates
  3. If confidence < 0.5, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
  4. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts)
  5. Route to expert_review after 3 failed attempts
- **Consent entities:** Explicitly skipped (not groundable to codes)
- **Error accumulation:** Individual entity failures don't crash pipeline
- **Parallelization:** asyncio.gather with Semaphore(4) for concurrency control
- **Audit logging:** Batch-writes all grounding decisions to AuditLog table
- **Field mappings:** Generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition

**5. Persist Node** (458 lines) - `/nodes/persist.py`
- Commits grounding results to Entity records
- Handles partial vs total failure:
  - Partial (some succeeded): status = `pending_review`
  - Total (all failed): status = `grounding_failed`
- Stores field_mappings in Criteria.conditions JSON
- Applies review inheritance from prior extractions
- Updates Protocol status

**6. Structure Node** (205 lines) - `/nodes/structure.py`
- Builds expression trees from grounded entities
- Uses AND/OR/NOT logic to decompose criteria
- Creates structured atomic conditions

**7. Ordinal Resolve Node** (277 lines) - `/nodes/ordinal_resolve.py`
- Detects ordinal scales (NYHA, ECOG)
- Maps scale values to OMOP unit concepts
- Generates ordinal_proposals_json for review

#### Pipeline State: `/state.py`
```python
class PipelineState(TypedDict):
    protocol_id: str              # UUID of protocol
    file_uri: str                 # GCS or local:// path
    title: str                    # Protocol title
    batch_id: str | None          # Created by parse
    pdf_bytes: bytes | None       # Populated by ingest, consumed by extract
    extraction_json: str | None   # JSON from extract node
    entities_json: str | None     # Prepared by parse for ground
    grounded_entities_json: str | None  # From ground node
    status: Literal["processing", "completed", "failed"]
    error: str | None             # Fatal error routes to END
    errors: list[str]             # Accumulated non-fatal errors
```

**Graph Creation:** `/graph.py`
- `create_graph(checkpointer=None)`: Compiles StateGraph with optional PostgreSQL checkpointing
- `get_graph()`: Singleton that creates AsyncPostgresSaver for fault tolerance
- Conditional error routing after ingest/extract/parse
- Ground/structure/ordinal use error accumulation (continue processing despite failures)

---

### 3. MLFLOW TRACING SETUP AND CONFIGURATION

**Location:** `/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tracing.py`

#### Trace Architecture:
```python
@contextmanager
def pipeline_span(
    name: str,
    span_type: str = "CHAIN",
    protocol_id: str = "",
):
```

- **Each node creates its own top-level trace** (not nested spans)
- Tagged with `protocol_id` for session grouping in MLflow UI
- Safe fallback to no-op if MLflow unavailable
- Each span records:
  - `set_inputs()`: Protocol ID, entity counts, model names
  - `set_outputs()`: Success counts, error counts, code assignments

#### API Service MLflow Setup: `/services/api-service/src/api_service/main.py`
```python
# Startup (lines 54-79)
mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)
mlflow.set_experiment("protocol-processing")
mlflow.langchain.autolog(run_tracer_inline=True)
```

- Experiment name: `protocol-processing`
- LangChain autolog enabled with `run_tracer_inline=True`
- Request middleware wraps all HTTP calls in MLflow traces
- Default tracking URI: `http://localhost:5001` (local), `http://mlflow:5000` (Docker)

#### Circuit Breaker MLflow Logging: `/libs/shared/src/shared/resilience.py`
```python
class MLflowCircuitBreakerListener(CircuitBreakerListener):
    def state_change(self, cb, old_state, new_state):
        # Logs breaker state changes (open, half_open, closed)
        # Records fail_counter and service name
```

Breakers monitored:
- `gemini_breaker`: fail_max=3, reset_timeout=60s
- `umls_breaker`: fail_max=3, reset_timeout=60s
- `gcs_breaker`: fail_max=3, reset_timeout=60s
- `vertex_ai_breaker`: fail_max=3, reset_timeout=60s
- `tu_breaker` (ToolUniverse): fail_max=10 (higher threshold due to transient failures)

### 5. PERFORMANCE-RELATED CODE

**Optimization Strategies:**

#### State Size Minimization:
- **JSON strings instead of dicts** in LangGraph state
  - `extraction_json`, `entities_json`, `grounded_entities_json` stored as JSON strings
  - Reduces serialization overhead between nodes
- **pdf_bytes cleared after extraction** (parse node, line 195)
  - No longer needed after extract node completes

#### Concurrency Control:
- **Asyncio.Semaphore(4)** in ground node (line 529)
  - Caps concurrent API calls to 4 parallel entity groundings
  - Prevents overwhelming UMLS/ToolUniverse APIs
- **Parallel dual grounding:** TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper run in parallel per entity
  - `asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)` (ground.py, line 386)

#### Lazy Loading:
- **TerminologyRouter singleton** (ground node, lines 51-59)
  - Loads YAML configuration once, reused across all invocations
- **Checkpointer singleton** (graph.py, lines 103-159)
  - Created once at startup, reused for all pipeline runs
  - Context manager kept open for process lifetime

#### Dev/Debug Knobs:
- `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA`: Truncate criteria for faster testing (parse node, line 71)
- `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES`: Truncate entities for faster testing (ground node, line 518)

#### Database Optimizations:
- **SQLModel with SQLAlchemy 2.0+** for async support
- **Batch audit logging:** `_flush_audit_logs()` writes all AuditLog entries in single session (ground node, lines 165-195)
- **Indexes on:**
  - Protocol.status (query by status)
  - CriteriaBatch.protocol_id, .batch_id (lookup by protocol)
  - Entity.criteria_id, .entity_type (query by type)
  - Entity.omop_concept_id (OMOP concept lookup)

#### Observability Performance:
- **Safe MLflow no-op fallback:** If MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI not set, tracing is completely disabled (no overhead)
- **Circuit breakers prevent cascading failures** to external APIs
- **Request middleware wrapping** (api_service/middleware.py) for all API calls

#### Error Handling Performance:
- **Error accumulation** instead of fail-fast
  - Ground node continues processing remaining entities even if one fails
  - Prevents wasted work on partial failures
- **Expert review routing:** Grounding failures marked for manual review rather than blocking pipeline

#### Caching:
- **DiskcCache** (pyproject.toml, line 24) for optional persistent caching
- **lazy_cache.py** in shared lib provides `@lazy_singleton` decorator for reusing expensive objects

---

### 6. KEY FILES AND RELATIONSHIPS

**Core Domain Models:** `/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py` (11,402 lines)
- `Protocol`: Uploaded PDFs with status tracking
- `CriteriaBatch`: Groups criteria from single extraction
- `Criteria`: Individual inclusion/exclusion criterion with text and structured data
- `Entity`: Medical entity with grounding codes (UMLS, SNOMED, RxNorm, LOINC, ICD-10, HPO)
- `Review`: Human review actions and decisions
- `AuditLog`: Full audit trail of grounding decisions
- `OutboxEvent`: Transactional outbox for event processing

**API Service Routes:**
- `protocols.py`: Upload, confirm, list operations
- `reviews.py`: Review submission and query
- `exports.py`: CIRCE JSON, FHIR R4 Group, OMOP SQL export builders
- `entities.py`: Entity search and filtering
- `search.py`: Full-text search on criteria text
- `terminology_search.py`: UMLS/SNOMED search endpoint
- `integrity.py`: Data consistency checks
- `batch_compare.py`: Compare results across batches

**Tools/Utilities:**
- `gemini_extractor.py`: Structured extraction via Gemini File API
- `medgemma_decider.py`: MedGemma-based entity selection
- `terminology_router.py`: YAML-configured API routing (TerminologyRouter singleton)
- `omop_mapper.py`: OMOP concept lookup
- `entity_decomposer.py`: Break criteria into atomic entities
- `field_mapper.py`: Generate field mappings for AutoCriteria
- `gemini_utils.py`: Shared Gemini utilities
- `unit_normalizer.py`: Unit value normalization

**Events System:** `/libs/events-py/`
- `outbox.py`: OutboxProcessor polls every 2 seconds
- `models.py`: OutboxEvent and OutboxHistory models
- Transaction: `confirm-upload` writes Protocol + OutboxEvent in single DB transaction

**Inference Factory:** `/libs/inference/src/inference/factory.py`
- `render_prompts()`: Jinja2 template rendering
- `create_structured_extractor()`: Retry logic with tenacity
- `AgentError`, `AgentTimeoutError`: Custom exceptions

---

### 7. DATA FLOW SUMMARY

```
1. Upload PDF (UI → API)
   → POST /protocols/upload → signed GCS URL
   → Client uploads to GCS
   → POST /protocols/{id}/confirm-upload
   → INSERT Protocol + OutboxEvent (single transaction)

2. OutboxProcessor detects event
   → Calls handle_protocol_uploaded()
   → Invokes LangGraph 7-node pipeline

3. Pipeline execution:
   ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve
   ↓
   All nodes use PostgreSQL checkpoints for recovery
   Each node emits MLflow spans tagged with protocol_id

4. Ground node (most complex):
   - Parses entities_json
   - Routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   - Calls MedGemma to select best match
   - Parallel OMOP lookup
   - Agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts)
   - Batch-writes AuditLog entries
   - Returns grounded_entities_json

5. Persist node writes Entity records
   → Updates Protocol status to pending_review
   → Triggers HITL UI refresh

6. Review workflow (UI):
   → GET /reviews/batches/{id}/criteria
   → POST /reviews/criteria/{id}/action (approve/reject)
   → Updates review_status in DB

7. Export:
   → GET /exports/{id}/circe → OHDSI CIRCE JSON
   → GET /exports/{id}/fhir-group → FHIR R4 Group
   → GET /exports/{id}/evaluation-sql → OMOP CDM evaluation SQL
```

---

### 8. DEPENDENCIES AND EXTERNAL SERVICES

**Key External APIs:**
- **Gemini 2.5 Flash**: PDF extraction, entity selection, ordinal resolution
- **UMLS MCP Server**: Entity grounding via ToolUniverse adapters
- **OMOP CDM Database**: Vocabulary concept lookup (optional, with graceful degradation)
- **GCS**: PDF storage and retrieval

**Key Libraries:**
- `langgraph>=1.0.6`: StateGraph, checkpointing
- `fastapi>=0.128.0`: REST API framework
- `sqlmodel>=0.0.31`: SQLAlchemy 2.0 + Pydantic integration
- `google-genai>=1.55.0`: Gemini API client
- `google-cloud-aiplatform>=1.130.0`: Vertex AI support
- `transformers>=4.57.6`: Model loading for local inference
- `langchain>=1.2.6`: LLM abstractions
- `mlflow>=3.8.1`: Experiment tracking
- `tenacity>=8.2.0`: Retry logic
- `pybreaker>=1.2.0`: Circuit breakers
- `rapidfuzz>=3.14.3`: Fuzzy string matching

---

### 9. TESTING STRATEGY

**Test Levels:**
1. **Unit Tests**: Individual functions/tools (protocol-processor/libs tests)
2. **Integration Tests**: Node interactions with mocked DB
3. **E2E Tests**: Full stack with real API + Docker compose

**Running Tests:**
```bash
make test-python           # Run all pytest
make check                 # Lint + type-check + test
pytest services -n auto   # Parallel execution
pytest -m e2e --timeout=180  # E2E tests with 3min timeout
```

**Coverage Configuration:**
- Minimum 85% (enforced in CI)
- Excludes test directories, .venv, alembic migrations

    62→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    63→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    64→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    65→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    66→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    67→
    68→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    69→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    70→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    71→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    72→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    73→
    74→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    75→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    76→
    77→    Args:
    78→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    79→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    80→
    81→    Returns:
    82→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    83→    """
    84→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    85→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    86→
    87→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    88→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    89→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    90→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    91→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    92→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    93→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    94→        else:
    95→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
    96→        logger.info(
    97→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
    98→            result.entity_text[:50],
    99→            result.selected_code,
   100→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   101→            result.reconciliation_status,
   102→        )
   103→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   104→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   105→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   106→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   107→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   108→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   109→
   110→    return result
   111→
   112→
   113→def _log_grounding_audit(
   114→    session: Session,
   115→    protocol_id: str,
   116→    criterion_id: str,
   117→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   118→    candidates: list[Any],
   119→    result: Any,
   120→) -> None:
   121→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   122→
   123→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   124→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   125→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   126→
   127→    Args:
   128→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   129→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   130→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   131→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   132→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   133→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   134→    """
   135→    audit = AuditLog(
   136→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   137→        target_type="criteria",
   138→        target_id=criterion_id,
   139→        details={
   140→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   141→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   142→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   143→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   144→            "candidates": [
   145→                {
   146→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   147→                    "code": c.code,
   148→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   149→                    "score": c.score,
   150→                }
   151→                for c in candidates
   152→            ],
   153→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   154→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   155→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   156→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   157→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   158→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   159→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   160→        },
   161→    )
   162→    session.add(audit)
   163→
   164→
   165→def _flush_audit_logs(
   166→    protocol_id: str,
   167→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   168→) -> None:
   169→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   170→
   171→    Args:
   172→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   173→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   174→    """
   175→    if not pending_audits:
   176→        return
   177→    try:
   178→        with Session(engine) as session:
   179→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   180→                _log_grounding_audit(
   181→                    session,
   182→                    protocol_id,
   183→                    criterion_id,
   184→                    entity,
   185→                    candidates,
   186→                    result,
   187→                )
   188→            session.commit()
   189→    except Exception as audit_error:
   190→        logger.warning(
   191→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   192→            len(pending_audits),
   193→            audit_error,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→
   197→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   198→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   199→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   200→    criterion_text: str,
   201→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   202→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   203→
   204→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   205→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   206→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   207→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   208→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   209→
   210→    Args:
   211→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   212→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   213→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   214→
   215→    Returns:
   216→        EntityGroundingResult. May have expert_review marker in reasoning.
   217→    """
   218→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   219→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   220→
   221→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   222→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   223→
   224→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   225→    if not candidates:
   226→        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   227→        if not apis:
   228→            logger.info(
   229→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   230→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   231→                entity_text[:50],
   232→                entity_type,
   233→            )
   234→        else:
   235→            logger.info(
   236→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→
   241→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   242→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   243→
   244→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   245→    attempt = 1
   246→    current_query = entity_text
   247→    while (
   248→        result.selected_code is None
   249→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   250→        and attempt < 3
   251→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   252→    ):
   253→        attempt += 1
   254→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   255→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   256→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   257→            router=router,
   258→            attempt=attempt,
   259→        )
   260→
   261→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   262→            logger.info(
   263→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   264→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   265→                entity_text[:50],
   266→                attempt,
   267→            )
   268→            return EntityGroundingResult(
   269→                entity_text=entity_text,
   270→                entity_type=entity_type,
   271→                selected_code=None,
   272→                selected_system=None,
   273→                preferred_term=None,
   274→                confidence=0.0,
   275→                candidates=[],
   276→                reasoning=(
   277→                    f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   278→                    f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   279→                ),
   280→            )
   281→
   282→        new_query = (
   283→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   284→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   285→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   286→            or current_query
   287→        )
   288→        current_query = new_query
   289→
   290→        logger.info(
   291→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   292→            attempt,
   293→            entity_text[:50],
   294→            new_query[:50],
   295→        )
   296→
   297→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   298→        if new_candidates:
   299→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   300→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   301→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   302→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   303→                return EntityGroundingResult(
   304→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   305→                    entity_type=result.entity_type,
   306→                    selected_code=result.selected_code,
   307→                    selected_system=result.selected_system,
   308→                    preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   309→                    confidence=result.confidence,
   310→                    candidates=result.candidates,
   311→                    reasoning=(
   312→                        f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] {result.reasoning}"
   313→                    ),
   314→                    field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   315→                )
   316→
   317→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   318→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   319→        logger.warning(
   320→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   321→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   322→            entity_text[:50],
   323→            entity_type,
   324→            attempt,
   325→        )
   326→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   327→            entity_text=result.entity_text,
   328→            entity_type=result.entity_type,
   329→            selected_code=result.selected_code,
   330→            selected_system=result.selected_system,
   331→            preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   332→            confidence=result.confidence,
   333→            candidates=result.candidates,
   334→            reasoning=(
   335→                f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   336→                f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   337→                f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   338→            ),
   339→        )
   340→
   341→    return result
   342→
   343→
   344→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   345→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   346→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   347→    criterion_text: str,
   348→    entity_num: int,
   349→    total: int,
   350→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   351→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None]:
   352→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   353→
   354→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   355→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   356→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   357→
   358→    Args:
   359→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   360→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   361→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   362→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   363→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   364→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   365→
   366→    Returns:
   367→        (EntityGroundingResult, None) on success, or (None, error_message) on failure.
   368→    """
   369→    async with semaphore:
   370→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   371→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   372→        start = time.monotonic()
   373→        logger.info(
   374→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   375→            entity_num,
   376→            total,
   377→            entity_text,
   378→            entity_type,
   379→        )
   380→        try:
   381→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   382→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   383→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   384→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   385→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(full_entity_text, entity_type)
   386→            result, omop_result = await asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)
   387→
   388→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   389→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   390→
   391→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   392→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   393→            if criterion_id:
   394→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   395→
   396→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   397→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   398→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   399→
   400→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   401→            logger.info(
   402→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   403→                entity_num,
   404→                total,
   405→                entity_text,
   406→                elapsed,
   407→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   408→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   409→                result.confidence,
   410→            )
   411→            return (result, None)
   412→        except Exception as e:
   413→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   414→            logger.error(
   415→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   416→                entity_num,
   417→                total,
   418→                entity_text,
   419→                elapsed,
   420→                e,
   421→            )
   422→            return (None, str(e))
   423→
   424→
   425→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   426→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   427→
   428→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   429→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   430→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   431→    entity.
   432→
   433→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   434→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   435→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   436→
   437→    Args:
   438→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   439→
   440→    Returns:
   441→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   442→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   443→    """
   444→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   445→
   446→    if state.get("error"):
   447→        return {}
   448→
   449→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   450→    with pipeline_span(
   451→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   452→    ) as span:
   453→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   454→
   455→        try:
   456→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   457→            if not entities_json:
   458→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   459→                return {
   460→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   461→                }
   462→
   463→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   464→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   465→
   466→            span.set_inputs(
   467→                {
   468→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   469→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   470→                }
   471→            )
   472→
   473→            logger.info(
   474→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   475→                protocol_id,
   476→                len(entity_items),
   477→            )
   478→
   479→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   480→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   481→            _get_omop_engine()
   482→
   483→            router = _get_router()
   484→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   485→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   486→
   487→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   488→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   489→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   490→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   491→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   492→
   493→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   494→                    logger.info(
   495→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   496→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   497→                        entity_text[:50],
   498→                    )
   499→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   500→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   501→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   502→                        selected_code=None,
   503→                        selected_system=None,
   504→                        preferred_term=None,
   505→                        confidence=0.0,
   506→                        candidates=[],
   507→                        reasoning=(
   508→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   509→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   510→                        ),
   511→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   512→                    )
   513→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   514→                else:
   515→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   516→
   517→            # Dev/debug knob: truncate entity list for faster pipeline runs
   518→            _max_entities = int(os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES", "0"))
   519→            if _max_entities > 0:
   520→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   521→                logger.info(
   522→                    "PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   523→                    _max_entities,
   524→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   525→                )
   526→
   527→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   528→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   529→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   530→
   531→            tasks = [
   532→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   533→                    entity,
   534→                    router,
   535→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   536→                    idx,
   537→                    len(entity_items),
   538→                    semaphore,
   539→                )
   540→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   541→            ]
   542→
   543→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   544→
   545→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   546→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   547→            for (idx, entity), (result, error) in zip(entities_to_ground, outcomes):
   548→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   549→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   550→
   551→                if result is not None:
   552→                    pending_audits.append(
   553→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   554→                    )
   555→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   556→                else:
   557→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   558→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   559→                    error_msg = (
   560→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   561→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   562→                    )
   563→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   564→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   565→
   566→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   567→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   568→
   569→            logger.info(
   570→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   571→                protocol_id,
   572→                len(grounding_results),
   573→                len(accumulated_errors),
   574→            )
   575→
   576→            span.set_outputs(
   577→                {
   578→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   579→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   580→                }
   581→            )
   582→
   583→            return {
   584→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   585→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   586→            }
   587→
   588→        except Exception as e:
   589→            logger.exception(
   590→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   591→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   592→                e,
   593→            )
   594→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   595→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   596→

     1→"""MLflow tracing helpers for pipeline nodes.
     2→
     3→Provides a safe context manager that creates MLflow traces when available
     4→and falls back to a no-op when MLflow is not configured or installed.
     5→
     6→Each node creates its own separate top-level trace (not a child span),
     7→tagged with protocol_id so traces from the same pipeline run can be
     8→grouped and filtered in the MLflow UI.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import os
    14→from contextlib import contextmanager
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→
    18→@contextmanager
    19→def pipeline_span(
    20→    name: str,
    21→    span_type: str = "CHAIN",
    22→    protocol_id: str = "",
    23→):
    24→    """Create a separate MLflow trace for a pipeline node.
    25→
    26→    Each call creates its own top-level trace tagged with protocol_id,
    27→    so individual node traces appear in MLflow as they complete rather
    28→    than waiting for the entire pipeline to finish.
    29→
    30→    Args:
    31→        name: Trace/span name (e.g., "ingest_node", "ground_node").
    32→        span_type: MLflow span type (default "CHAIN").
    33→        protocol_id: Protocol ID to tag the trace with for session grouping.
    34→
    35→    Yields:
    36→        MLflow span object or a no-op wrapper.
    37→    """
    38→    try:
    39→        import mlflow
    40→
    41→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
    42→            with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type=span_type) as span:
    43→                if protocol_id:
    44→                    try:
    45→                        mlflow.update_current_trace(
    46→                            tags={"protocol_id": protocol_id},
    47→                        )
    48→                    except Exception:
    49→                        pass  # Tag failure is non-fatal
    50→                yield span
    51→                return
    52→    except ImportError:
    53→        pass
    54→    except Exception:
    55→        pass
    56→
    57→    # Fallback: no-op span
    58→    yield _NoOpSpan()
    59→
    60→
    61→class _NoOpSpan:
    62→    """No-op span that silently accepts MLflow span API calls."""
    63→
    64→    def set_inputs(self, inputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    65→        pass
    66→
    67→    def set_outputs(self, outputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    68→        pass
    69→
    70→    def set_status(self, status: str) -> None:
    71→        pass

   227→        from shared.models import (

   235→
   236→        # Create parent records
   237→        protocol = Protocol(title="Test Protocol", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
   238→        e2e_session.add(protocol)
   239→        e2e_session.flush()
   240→
   241→        batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
   242→        e2e_session.add(batch)
   243→        e2e_session.flush()
   244→
   245→        criterion = Criteria(
   246→            batch_id=batch.id,
   247→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   248→            text="HbA1c < 7% and eGFR > 30 mL/min",
   249→        )
   250→        e2e_session.add(criterion)
   251→        e2e_session.flush()
   252→
   253→        # Create two atomics
   254→        atom1 = AtomicCriterion(
   255→            criterion_id=criterion.id,
   256→            protocol_id=protocol.id,
   257→            inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   258→            relation_operator="<",
   259→            value_numeric=7.0,
   260→            unit_text="%",
   261→            original_text="HbA1c < 7%",
   262→        )
   263→        atom2 = AtomicCriterion(
   264→            criterion_id=criterion.id,
   265→            protocol_id=protocol.id,
   266→            inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   267→            relation_operator=">",
   268→            value_numeric=30.0,
   269→            unit_text="mL/min",
   270→            original_text="eGFR > 30 mL/min",
   271→        )
   272→        e2e_session.add_all([atom1, atom2])
   273→        e2e_session.flush()
   274→
   275→        # Create AND composite
   276→        composite = CompositeCriterion(
   277→            criterion_id=criterion.id,
   278→            protocol_id=protocol.id,
   279→            inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   280→            logic_operator="AND",
   281→            original_text="HbA1c < 7% and eGFR > 30 mL/min",
   282→        )
   283→        e2e_session.add(composite)
   284→        e2e_session.flush()
   285→
   286→        # Create relationships
   287→        rel1 = CriterionRelationship(
   288→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   289→            child_criterion_id=atom1.id,
   290→            child_type="atomic",
   291→            child_sequence=0,
   292→        )
   293→        rel2 = CriterionRelationship(
   294→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   295→            child_criterion_id=atom2.id,
   296→            child_type="atomic",
   297→            child_sequence=1,
   298→        )
   299→        e2e_session.add_all([rel1, rel2])
   300→        e2e_session.flush()
   301→
   302→        # Query: verify composite + relationships
   303→        comp_result = e2e_session.get(CompositeCriterion, composite.id)
   304→        assert comp_result is not None
   305→        assert comp_result.logic_operator == "AND"
   306→
   307→        rels = e2e_session.exec(
   308→            select(CriterionRelationship).where(
   309→                CriterionRelationship.parent_criterion_id == composite.id
   310→            )
   311→        ).all()
   312→        assert len(rels) == 2
   313→        assert rels[0].child_type == "atomic"
   314→        assert rels[1].child_type == "atomic"
   315→        # Verify ordering
   316→        sequences = sorted(r.child_sequence for r in rels)
   317→        assert sequences == [0, 1]

   568→    @patch("protocol_processor.tracing.pipeline_span")
   569→    async def test_full_node_flow(
   570→        self, mock_span_fn: MagicMock, e2e_engine, e2e_session
   571→    ) -> None:
   572→        """Structure node reads criteria from DB, builds trees, writes back."""
   573→        from shared.models import (
   574→            AtomicCriterion,
   575→            AuditLog,
   576→            CompositeCriterion,
   577→            Criteria,
   578→            CriteriaBatch,
   579→            Protocol,
   580→        )
   581→
   582→        # Setup span mock
   583→        mock_span = MagicMock()
   584→        mock_span.__enter__ = MagicMock(return_value=mock_span)
   585→        mock_span.__exit__ = MagicMock(return_value=False)
   586→        mock_span.set_inputs = MagicMock()
   587→        mock_span.set_outputs = MagicMock()
   588→        mock_span_fn.return_value = mock_span
   589→
   590→        # Create test data
   591→        protocol = Protocol(title="E2E Test Protocol", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
   592→        e2e_session.add(protocol)
   593→        e2e_session.flush()
   594→
   595→        batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
   596→        e2e_session.add(batch)
   597→        e2e_session.flush()
   598→
   599→        # Criterion 1: single mapping (no logic needed)
   600→        crit1 = Criteria(
   601→            batch_id=batch.id,
   602→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   603→            text="Age >= 18 years",
   604→            conditions={
   605→                "field_mappings": [
   606→                    {
   607→                        "entity": "Age",
   608→                        "relation": ">=",
   609→                        "value": "18",
   610→                        "unit": "years",
   611→                        "entity_concept_id": "C0001779",
   612→                        "entity_concept_system": "umls",
   613→                        "omop_concept_id": "4265453",
   614→                    }
   615→                ]
   616→            },
   617→        )
   618→
   619→        # Criterion 2: two mappings (AND fallback)
   620→        crit2 = Criteria(
   621→            batch_id=batch.id,
   622→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   623→            text="HbA1c < 7% and eGFR > 30 mL/min",
   624→            conditions={
   625→                "field_mappings": [
   626→                    {
   627→                        "entity": "HbA1c",
   628→                        "relation": "<",
   629→                        "value": "7",
   630→                        "unit": "%",
   631→                        "entity_concept_id": "C0011847",
   632→                        "entity_concept_system": "umls",
   633→                        "omop_concept_id": "201826",
   634→                    },
   635→                    {
   636→                        "entity": "eGFR",
   637→                        "relation": ">",
   638→                        "value": "30",
   639→                        "unit": "mL/min",
   640→                        "entity_concept_id": "C0017654",
   641→                        "entity_concept_system": "umls",
   642→                        "omop_concept_id": "3049187",
   643→                    },
   644→                ]
   645→            },
   646→        )
   647→
   648→        # Criterion 3: no field_mappings (should be skipped)
   649→        crit3 = Criteria(
   650→            batch_id=batch.id,
   651→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   652→            text="Informed consent provided",
   653→            conditions=None,
   654→        )
   655→
   656→        e2e_session.add_all([crit1, crit2, crit3])
   657→        e2e_session.commit()
   658→
   659→        # Ensure no GOOGLE_API_KEY -> fallback mode
   660→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   661→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   662→
   663→            # Patch engine to use our test engine
   664→            with patch("protocol_processor.nodes.structure.engine", e2e_engine):
   665→                from protocol_processor.nodes.structure import structure_node
   666→
   667→                result = await structure_node(
   668→                    {  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   669→                        "error": None,
   670→                        "protocol_id": protocol.id,
   671→                        "batch_id": batch.id,
   672→                        "errors": [],
   673→                    }
   674→                )
   675→
   676→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   677→        assert len(result.get("errors", [])) == 0
   678→
   679→        # Refresh session to see committed data
   680→        e2e_session.expire_all()
   681→
   682→        # Verify crit1: single ATOMIC tree
   683→        crit1_db = e2e_session.get(Criteria, crit1.id)
   684→        assert crit1_db is not None
   685→        assert crit1_db.structured_criterion is not None
   686→        assert crit1_db.structured_criterion["root"]["type"] == "ATOMIC"
   687→        assert crit1_db.structured_criterion["root"]["entity"] == "Age"
   688→        assert crit1_db.structured_criterion["structure_confidence"] == "fallback"
   689→
   690→        # Verify crit2: AND tree with 2 children
   691→        crit2_db = e2e_session.get(Criteria, crit2.id)
   692→        assert crit2_db is not None
   693→        assert crit2_db.structured_criterion is not None
   694→        assert crit2_db.structured_criterion["root"]["type"] == "AND"
   695→        children = crit2_db.structured_criterion["root"]["children"]
   696→        assert len(children) == 2
   697→        assert children[0]["entity"] == "HbA1c"
   698→        assert children[1]["entity"] == "eGFR"
   699→
   700→        # Verify crit3: no structured_criterion (skipped)
   701→        crit3_db = e2e_session.get(Criteria, crit3.id)
   702→        assert crit3_db is not None
   703→        assert crit3_db.structured_criterion is None
   704→
   705→        # Verify atomic_criteria records
   706→        all_atomics = e2e_session.exec(select(AtomicCriterion)).all()
   707→        # crit1 -> 1 atomic, crit2 -> 2 atomics = 3 total
   708→        assert len(all_atomics) == 3
   709→
   710→        # Verify composite_criteria records
   711→        all_composites = e2e_session.exec(select(CompositeCriterion)).all()
   712→        # crit2 -> 1 AND composite = 1 total (crit1 has no composite)
   713→        assert len(all_composites) == 1
   714→        assert all_composites[0].logic_operator == "AND"
   715→
   716→        # Verify audit log
   717→        audits = e2e_session.exec(
   718→            select(AuditLog).where(AuditLog.event_type == "structure_trees_built")
   719→        ).all()
   720→        assert len(audits) == 1
   721→        assert audits[0].details["criteria_processed"] == 2
   722→        assert audits[0].details["errors"] == 0
   723→

   725→# ===========================================================================
   726→# 5. Graph Compilation Verification
   727→# ===========================================================================
   728→
   729→
   730→class TestGraphCompilation:
   731→    """Verify the 7-node graph compiles correctly with structure + ordinal_resolve."""
   732→
   733→    def test_seven_node_pipeline(self) -> None:
   734→        """Graph should have 7 user-defined nodes."""
   735→        from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph
   736→
   737→        graph = create_graph()
   738→        all_nodes = graph.nodes
   739→        user_nodes = {n for n in all_nodes if not n.startswith("__")}
   740→        assert user_nodes == {
   741→            "ingest",
   742→            "extract",
   743→            "parse",
   744→            "ground",
   745→            "persist",
   746→            "structure",
   747→            "ordinal_resolve",
   748→        }
   749→
   750→    def test_persist_to_structure_edge(self) -> None:
   751→        """Persist should have an edge to structure (not to END)."""
   752→        from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph
   753→
   754→        graph = create_graph()
   755→        # LangGraph stores edges in graph.graph — verify structure is after persist
   756→        # by checking the graph has structure node and it appears after persist
   757→        assert "structure" in graph.nodes
   758→        assert "persist" in graph.nodes
   759→
   760→    def test_structure_node_is_callable(self) -> None:
   761→        """structure_node should be an async callable."""
   762→        import asyncio
   763→
   764→        from protocol_processor.nodes.structure import structure_node
   765→
   766→        assert callable(structure_node)
   767→        assert asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(structure_node)
   768→

   771→# 6. Phase 3: Unit & Value Normalization

   773→
   774→
   775→class TestPhase3UnitNormalization:
   776→    """Verify Phase 3 unit/value concept ID columns and normalizer integration."""
   777→
   778→    def test_unit_concept_id_column_exists(self, e2e_engine) -> None:
   779→        """atomic_criteria should have unit_concept_id column."""
   780→        inspector = inspect(e2e_engine)
   781→        columns = {c["name"] for c in inspector.get_columns("atomic_criteria")}
   782→        assert "unit_concept_id" in columns
   783→
   784→    def test_value_concept_id_column_exists(self, e2e_engine) -> None:
   785→        """atomic_criteria should have value_concept_id column."""
   786→        inspector = inspect(e2e_engine)
   787→        columns = {c["name"] for c in inspector.get_columns("atomic_criteria")}
   788→        assert "value_concept_id" in columns
   789→
   790→    async def test_unit_normalization_persists(self, e2e_session) -> None:
   791→        """Structure builder should populate unit_concept_id for '%' -> 8554."""
   792→        from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Protocol
   793→
   794→        from protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import build_expression_tree
   795→
   796→        protocol = Protocol(title="Test Protocol", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
   797→        e2e_session.add(protocol)
   798→        e2e_session.flush()
   799→
   800→        batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
   801→        e2e_session.add(batch)
   802→        e2e_session.flush()
   803→
   804→        criterion = Criteria(
   805→            batch_id=batch.id,
   806→            criteria_type="inclusion",
   807→            text="HbA1c < 7%",
   808→        )
   809→        e2e_session.add(criterion)
   810→        e2e_session.flush()
   811→
   812→        await build_expression_tree(
   813→            criterion_text="HbA1c < 7%",
   814→            field_mappings=[_make_field_mapping()],
   815→            criterion_id=criterion.id,
   816→            protocol_id=protocol.id,
   817→            inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   818→            session=e2e_session,
   819→        )
   820→        e2e_session.commit()
   821→
   822→        atomics = e2e_session.exec(
   823→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == criterion.id)
   824→        ).all()
   825→        assert len(atomics) == 1
   826→        assert atomics[0].unit_text == "%"
   827→        assert atomics[0].unit_concept_id == 8554
   828→

    54→def _setup_parent(
    55→    session: Session,
    56→) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    57→    """Create Protocol + CriteriaBatch, return (protocol_id, batch_id)."""
    58→    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT00001234", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
    59→    session.add(protocol)
    60→    session.flush()
    61→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    62→    session.add(batch)
    63→    session.flush()
    64→    return protocol.id, batch.id

    67→def _make_criterion(
    68→    session: Session,
    69→    batch_id: str,
    70→    text: str,
    71→    criteria_type: str = "inclusion",
    72→) -> Criteria:
    73→    crit = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type=criteria_type, text=text)
    74→    session.add(crit)
    75→    session.flush()
    76→    return crit

   478→class TestPhase3RealisticCriteria:
   479→    """Run 12 realistic clinical trial criteria through the backend."""
   480→
   481→    async def test_all_criteria_processed(self, session) -> None:
   482→        """All 12 criteria produce correct AtomicCriterion records."""
   483→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   484→        all_atomics: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   485→
   486→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   487→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   488→
   489→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   490→                crit = _make_criterion(
   491→                    session,
   492→                    batch_id,
   493→                    crit_def["text"],
   494→                    crit_def["type"],
   495→                )
   496→                await build_expression_tree(
   497→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   498→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   499→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   500→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   501→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   502→                    session=session,
   503→                )
   504→                session.flush()
   505→
   506→                # Collect atomics for this criterion
   507→                atomics = session.exec(
   508→                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   509→                        AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id
   510→                    )
   511→                ).all()
   512→                all_atomics.extend(atomics)
   513→
   514→        session.commit()
   515→
   516→        # Total expected: sum of mappings across all criteria
   517→        total_mappings = sum(len(c["mappings"]) for c in CRITERIA)
   518→        assert len(all_atomics) == total_mappings, (
   519→            f"Expected {total_mappings} atomics, got {len(all_atomics)}"
   520→        )
   521→
   522→        # Verify each atomic against expected values
   523→        for i, (atomic, exp) in enumerate(zip(all_atomics, EXPECTED)):
   524→            label = f"Atomic #{i}"
   525→            assert atomic.entity_concept_id == exp["entity_concept_id"], (
   526→                f"{label}: entity_concept_id"
   527→            )
   528→            assert atomic.relation_operator == exp["relation_operator"], (
   529→                f"{label}: relation_operator"
   530→            )
   531→            assert atomic.unit_text == exp.get("unit_text"), f"{label}: unit_text"
   532→            assert atomic.unit_concept_id == exp["unit_concept_id"], (
   533→                f"{label}: unit_concept_id "
   534→                f"(got {atomic.unit_concept_id}, "
   535→                f"expected {exp['unit_concept_id']} "
   536→                f"for unit '{atomic.unit_text}')"
   537→            )
   538→            assert atomic.value_concept_id == exp["value_concept_id"], (
   539→                f"{label}: value_concept_id "
   540→                f"(got {atomic.value_concept_id}, "
   541→                f"expected {exp['value_concept_id']} "
   542→                f"for value '{atomic.value_text}')"
   543→            )
   544→            if exp.get("value_numeric") is not None:
   545→                assert atomic.value_numeric == pytest.approx(exp["value_numeric"]), (
   546→                    f"{label}: value_numeric"
   547→                )
   548→            else:
   549→                assert atomic.value_numeric is None, (
   550→                    f"{label}: value_numeric should be None"
   551→                )
   552→            if "value_text" in exp:
   553→                assert atomic.value_text == exp["value_text"], f"{label}: value_text"
   554→
   555→    async def test_inclusion_exclusion_correct(self, session) -> None:
   556→        """Criteria correctly tagged as inclusion or exclusion."""
   557→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   558→
   559→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   560→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   561→
   562→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   563→                crit = _make_criterion(
   564→                    session,
   565→                    batch_id,
   566→                    crit_def["text"],
   567→                    crit_def["type"],
   568→                )
   569→                await build_expression_tree(
   570→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   571→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   572→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   573→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   574→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   575→                    session=session,
   576→                )
   577→
   578→        session.commit()
   579→
   580→        inclusion_atomics = session.exec(
   581→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   582→                AtomicCriterion.inclusion_exclusion == "inclusion"
   583→            )
   584→        ).all()
   585→        exclusion_atomics = session.exec(
   586→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   587→                AtomicCriterion.inclusion_exclusion == "exclusion"
   588→            )
   589→        ).all()
   590→
   591→        # Criteria 7 and 8 are exclusion (1 mapping each = 2 atomics)
   592→        assert len(exclusion_atomics) == 2
   593→        # All others are inclusion (14 mappings total)
   594→        assert len(inclusion_atomics) == 14
   595→
   596→    async def test_unit_coverage_summary(self, session) -> None:
   597→        """Print a coverage summary of all units processed."""
   598→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   599→
   600→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   601→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   602→
   603→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   604→                crit = _make_criterion(
   605→                    session,
   606→                    batch_id,
   607→                    crit_def["text"],
   608→                    crit_def["type"],
   609→                )
   610→                await build_expression_tree(
   611→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   612→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   613→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   614→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   615→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   616→                    session=session,
   617→                )
   618→
   619→        session.commit()
   620→
   621→        all_atomics = session.exec(select(AtomicCriterion)).all()
   622→
   623→        resolved = [a for a in all_atomics if a.unit_concept_id is not None]
   624→        unresolved_with_unit = [
   625→            a
   626→            for a in all_atomics
   627→            if a.unit_text is not None and a.unit_concept_id is None
   628→        ]
   629→        no_unit = [a for a in all_atomics if a.unit_text is None]
   630→        value_resolved = [a for a in all_atomics if a.value_concept_id is not None]
   631→
   632→        # Verify we got good coverage
   633→        # 14 mappings have unit_concept_id (12 explicit units +
   634→        # ECOG {score} + Karnofsky {score} via ordinal normalizer)
   635→        assert len(resolved) == 14
   636→        assert len(unresolved_with_unit) == 0  # no unrecognized units
   637→        # 4 with unit_text=None: HIV, HBsAg, ECOG, Karnofsky
   638→        # (ECOG/Karnofsky get unit_concept_id from ordinal dispatch, not unit_text)
   639→        assert len(no_unit) == 4
   640→        assert len(value_resolved) == 2  # positive + negative
   641→

   196→        "text": "HbA1c <= 10%",
   197→        "mappings": [
   198→            {
   199→                "entity": "HbA1c",
   200→                "relation": "<=",
   201→                "value": "10",
   202→                "unit": "%",
   203→                "entity_concept_id": "2345-7",
   204→                "entity_concept_system": "loinc",
   205→                "omop_concept_id": "3004410",
   206→            },
   207→        ],
   208→        "expect": {
   209→            "unit_concept_id": 8554,  # % — NOT 8527
   210→            "value_numeric": 10.0,
   211→        },
   212→    },
   213→    # Non-ordinal: categorical HIV status (should NOT get ordinal treatment)
   214→    {
   215→        "text": "HIV status positive",
   216→        "mappings": [
   217→            {
   218→                "entity": "HIV status",
   219→                "relation": "is",
   220→                "value": "positive",
   221→                "unit": None,
   222→                "entity_concept_id": "C0019693",
   223→                "entity_concept_system": "umls",
   224→                "omop_concept_id": "439727",
   225→            },
   226→        ],
   227→        "expect": {
   228→            "unit_concept_id": None,
   229→            "value_concept_id": 45884084,  # positive
   230→        },
   231→    },
   232→]

   236→# Tests

   240→class TestPhase3bOrdinalE2E:
   241→    """E2E: ordinal criteria through build_expression_tree → DB."""
   242→
   243→    async def test_ordinal_criteria_get_score_unit(self, session) -> None:
   244→        """All ordinal criteria get unit_concept_id=8527 ({score})."""
   245→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   246→
   247→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   248→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   249→
   250→            for i, crit_def in enumerate(ORDINAL_CRITERIA):
   251→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   252→                await build_expression_tree(
   253→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   254→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   255→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   256→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   257→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   258→                    session=session,
   259→                )
   260→                session.flush()
   261→
   262→                atomics = session.exec(
   263→                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   264→                        AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id
   265→                    )
   266→                ).all()
   267→                assert len(atomics) == 1, f"Criterion {i}: expected 1 atomic"
   268→
   269→                a = atomics[0]
   270→                exp = crit_def["expect"]
   271→
   272→                assert a.unit_concept_id == exp["unit_concept_id"], (
   273→                    f"Criterion {i} ({crit_def['text']}): "
   274→                    f"unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}, "
   275→                    f"expected {exp['unit_concept_id']}"
   276→                )
   277→
   278→                if "value_numeric" in exp:
   279→                    assert a.value_numeric == pytest.approx(exp["value_numeric"]), (
   280→                        f"Criterion {i}: value_numeric"
   281→                    )
   282→
   283→                if "value_concept_id" in exp:
   284→                    assert a.value_concept_id == exp["value_concept_id"], (
   285→                        f"Criterion {i}: value_concept_id"
   286→                    )
   287→
   288→        session.commit()
   289→
   290→    async def test_ecog_grade_prefix_through_backend(self, session) -> None:
   291→        """'Grade 2' value text resolves through full backend path."""
   292→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   293→
   294→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   295→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   296→
   297→            crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, "ECOG Grade 2")
   298→            await build_expression_tree(
   299→                criterion_text="ECOG Grade 2",
   300→                field_mappings=[
   301→                    {
   302→                        "entity": "ECOG",
   303→                        "relation": "=",
   304→                        "value": "Grade 2",
   305→                        "unit": None,
   306→                    },
   307→                ],
   308→                criterion_id=crit.id,
   309→                protocol_id=protocol_id,
   310→                inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   311→                session=session,
   312→            )
   313→            session.flush()
   314→
   315→            atomics = session.exec(
   316→                select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id)
   317→            ).all()
   318→            assert len(atomics) == 1
   319→            # "Grade 2" parses to numeric because _parse_value can't parse it,
   320→            # so value_text="Grade 2" and value_numeric=None
   321→            # But the ordinal normalizer still matches the entity and sets
   322→            # unit_concept_id=8527
   323→            assert atomics[0].unit_concept_id == 8527
   324→

   357→    async def test_mixed_ordinal_and_lab_criteria(self, session) -> None:
   358→        """Mixed criteria: ordinal + lab values in same batch work correctly."""
   359→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   360→
   361→        mixed = [
   362→            # Ordinal
   363→            {
   364→                "text": "ECOG <= 1",
   365→                "mappings": [
   366→                    {
   367→                        "entity": "ECOG",
   368→                        "relation": "<=",
   369→                        "value": "1",
   370→                        "unit": None,
   371→                    },
   372→                ],
   373→            },
   374→            # Lab value
   375→            {
   376→                "text": "Creatinine <= 1.5 mg/dL",
   377→                "mappings": [
   378→                    {
   379→                        "entity": "Serum creatinine",
   380→                        "relation": "<=",
   381→                        "value": "1.5",
   382→                        "unit": "mg/dL",
   383→                    },
   384→                ],
   385→            },
   386→            # Another ordinal
   387→            {
   388→                "text": "NYHA class <= 2",
   389→                "mappings": [
   390→                    {
   391→                        "entity": "NYHA class",
   392→                        "relation": "<=",
   393→                        "value": "2",
   394→                        "unit": None,
   395→                    },
   396→                ],
   397→            },
   398→        ]
   399→
   400→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   401→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   402→
   403→            crit_ids = []
   404→            for crit_def in mixed:
   405→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   406→                crit_ids.append(crit.id)
   407→                await build_expression_tree(
   408→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   409→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   410→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   411→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   412→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   413→                    session=session,
   414→                )
   415→                session.flush()
   416→
   417→        session.commit()
   418→
   419→        # ECOG: unit_concept_id=8527
   420→        ecog = session.exec(
   421→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[0])
   422→        ).first()
   423→        assert ecog is not None
   424→        assert ecog.unit_concept_id == 8527
   425→
   426→        # Creatinine: unit_concept_id=8840 (mg/dL)
   427→        creat = session.exec(
   428→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[1])
   429→        ).first()
   430→        assert creat is not None
   431→        assert creat.unit_concept_id == 8840
   432→
   433→        # NYHA: unit_concept_id=8527
   434→        nyha = session.exec(
   435→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[2])
   436→        ).first()
   437→        assert nyha is not None
   438→        assert nyha.unit_concept_id == 8527
   439→

   441→class TestProposeOrdinalMappingsE2E:
   442→    """Test the agent proposal utility end-to-end."""
   443→
   444→    def test_all_grades_need_resolution(self) -> None:
   445→        """Since no omop_value_concept_id is set, all grades need resolution."""
   446→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   447→        assert len(missing) > 0
   448→
   449→        # Should include entries from all 3 scales
   450→        scales = {e["scale"] for e in missing}
   451→        assert "ecog" in scales
   452→        assert "karnofsky" in scales
   453→        assert "nyha" in scales
   454→
   455→    def test_ecog_grades_in_proposals(self) -> None:
   456→        """All 6 ECOG grades appear in proposals."""
   457→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   458→        ecog_grades = {e["grade"] for e in missing if e["scale"] == "ecog"}
   459→        assert ecog_grades == {"0", "1", "2", "3", "4", "5"}
   460→
   461→    def test_proposals_have_snomed_codes(self) -> None:
   462→        """ECOG proposals include SNOMED codes from YAML."""
   463→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   464→        ecog_entries = [e for e in missing if e["scale"] == "ecog"]
   465→        snomed_codes = [e["snomed_code"] for e in ecog_entries if e["snomed_code"]]
   466→        # All 6 ECOG grades have SNOMED codes
   467→        assert len(snomed_codes) == 6
   468→
   469→    def test_karnofsky_proposals_no_snomed(self) -> None:
   470→        """Karnofsky proposals have no SNOMED codes (not in YAML)."""
   471→        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()
   472→        kps_entries = [e for e in missing if e["scale"] == "karnofsky"]
   473→        assert all(e["snomed_code"] is None for e in kps_entries)
   474→

     1→"""Tests for Phase 1b changes: concept ID wiring, criterion_id lookup, accumulation.
     2→
     3→Covers:
     4→1. Field mapping concept ID enrichment (field_mapper.py)
     5→2. Criterion ID-based direct lookup (persist.py)
     6→3. Field mapping accumulation (persist.py)
     7→4. EntityGroundingResult schema fields (grounding.py)
     8→
     9→All DB interactions are mocked -- no live database required.
    10→"""

    30→def _make_entity(**overrides: Any) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    31→    """Build an EntityGroundingResult with sensible defaults."""
    32→    defaults: dict[str, Any] = {
    33→        "entity_text": "type 2 diabetes",
    34→        "entity_type": "Condition",
    35→        "selected_code": None,
    36→        "selected_system": None,
    37→        "preferred_term": None,
    38→        "confidence": 0.9,
    39→        "candidates": [],
    40→        "reasoning": "",
    41→    }
    42→    defaults.update(overrides)
    43→    return EntityGroundingResult(**defaults)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _make_criteria_mock(**overrides: Any) -> MagicMock:
    47→    """Build a mock Criteria record with mutable .conditions."""
    48→    mock = MagicMock()
    49→    mock.id = overrides.get("id", "crit-001")
    50→    mock.batch_id = overrides.get("batch_id", "batch-001")
    51→    mock.text = overrides.get("text", "HbA1c < 7%")
    52→    mock.conditions = overrides.get("conditions", None)
    53→    return mock

   163→    def test_direct_lookup_by_criterion_id(self) -> None:
   164→        """When criterion_id is provided, session.get() is used."""
   165→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (

   168→
   169→        mock_session = MagicMock()

   171→        mock_session.get.return_value = mock_criterion
   172→
   173→        result = _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   174→            session=mock_session,

   216→    ) -> None:
   217→        """session.get returns None -> falls back to substring search."""
   218→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (
   219→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings,
   220→        )
   221→
   222→        mock_session = MagicMock()
   223→        # Direct lookup returns None
   224→        mock_session.get.return_value = None
   225→        # Substring fallback finds a match
   226→        mock_criterion = _make_criteria_mock(id="crit-substr")
   227→        mock_session.exec.return_value.all.return_value = [mock_criterion]
   228→
   229→        result = _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   230→            session=mock_session,
   231→            batch_id="batch-001",
   232→            entity_text="diabetes",
   233→            field_mappings=None,
   234→            criterion_id="crit-nonexistent",
   235→        )
   236→
   237→        assert result == "crit-substr"
   238→        # Should have tried direct lookup first
   239→        mock_session.get.assert_called_once()
   240→        # Then fallen back to substring search
   241→        mock_session.exec.assert_called_once()

   249→class TestFieldMappingAccumulation:
   250→    """Test that field_mappings accumulate, not overwrite (Gap 2 fix)."""
   251→
   252→    def test_new_mappings_extend_existing(self) -> None:
   253→        """New field_mappings should be appended to existing ones."""
   254→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (

   258→        existing_mappings = [

   277→                "value": "30",

   282→        _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
