```
     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    17→
    18→from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import (
    23→    EntityGroundingResult,
    24→    GroundingCandidate,
    25→)
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→_model_loader = None
    30→_PROMPTS_DIR = None
    31→
    32→
    33→def _get_prompts_dir():
    34→    from pathlib import Path
    35→
    36→    return Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    37→
    38→
    39→def _render_template(template_name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    40→    """Render a Jinja2 template from the prompts directory."""
    41→    from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    42→
    43→    prompts_dir = _get_prompts_dir()
    44→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(prompts_dir)), autoescape=False)
    45→    template = env.get_template(template_name)
    46→    return template.render(**kwargs)
    47→
    48→
    49→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    50→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    51→
    52→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    53→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    54→
    55→    Attributes:
    56→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    57→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    58→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    59→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    60→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    61→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    65→        default=False,
    66→        description=(
    67→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    68→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    69→        ),
    70→    )
    71→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    72→        default=False,
    73→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    74→    )
    75→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    76→        default=None,
    77→        description=(
    78→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    79→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    80→        ),
    81→    )
    82→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    83→        default=None,
    84→        description=(
    85→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    86→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    87→        ),
    88→    )
    89→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    92→    )
    93→    reasoning: str = Field(
    94→        default="",
    95→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
   100→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
   101→
   102→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
   106→            " Null if no good match."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
   113→        ),
   114→    )
   115→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   116→        default=None,
   117→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   118→    )
   119→    confidence: float = Field(
   120→        ge=0.0,
   121→        le=1.0,
   122→        description=(
   123→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   124→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   125→        ),
   126→    )
   127→    reasoning: str = Field(
   128→        default="",
   129→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   134→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   135→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   136→    if _model_loader is None:
   137→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   138→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   139→
   140→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   141→    return _model_loader()
   142→
   143→
   144→def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   145→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   149→
   150→    Returns:
   151→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   152→    """
   153→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   154→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   155→
   156→    if not google_api_key:
   157→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   158→
   159→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   160→        model=gemini_model_name,
   161→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   162→    )
   163→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   164→
   165→    prompt = (
   166→        "Extract the grounding decision from this medical terminology analysis."
   167→        " Return the selected code, system, preferred term, confidence,"
   168→        f" and reasoning.\n\n{raw_text}"
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   172→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   173→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   174→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   175→
   176→
   177→async def medgemma_decide(
   178→    entity: dict,
   179→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   180→    criterion_context: str,
   181→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   182→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   183→
   184→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   185→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   186→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   187→    appropriate.
   188→
   189→    Args:
   190→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   191→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   192→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   193→
   194→    Returns:
   195→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   196→    """
   197→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   198→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   199→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   200→
   201→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   202→    if not candidates:
   203→        logger.info(
   204→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   205→            " — returning empty grounding",
   206→            entity_text[:50],
   207→            entity_type,
   208→            criterion_id[:12],
   209→        )
   210→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   211→            entity_text=entity_text,
   212→            entity_type=entity_type,
   213→            selected_code=None,
   214→            selected_system=None,
   215→            preferred_term=None,
   216→            confidence=0.0,
   217→            candidates=[],
   218→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    try:
   222→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   223→
   224→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   225→        evaluate_prompt = _render_template(
   226→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   227→            entity_text=entity_text,
   228→            entity_type=entity_type,
   229→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   230→            candidates=candidates,
   231→        )
   232→
   233→        messages = [
   234→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   235→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   236→        ]
   237→        raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   238→        raw_text = raw_response.content
   239→
   240→        logger.debug(
   241→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   242→            entity_text[:30],
   243→            raw_text[:200],
   244→        )
   245→
   246→        decision = _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   247→
   248→        logger.info(
   249→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   250→            entity_text[:50],
   251→            decision.selected_code,
   252→            decision.selected_system,
   253→            decision.confidence,
   254→        )
   255→
   256→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   257→            entity_text=entity_text,
   258→            entity_type=entity_type,
   259→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   260→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   261→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   262→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   263→            candidates=candidates,
   264→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   265→        )
   266→
   267→    except Exception as e:
   268→        logger.error(
   269→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   270→            entity_text[:50],
   271→            e,
   272→            exc_info=True,
   273→        )
   274→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   275→            entity_text=entity_text,
   276→            entity_type=entity_type,
   277→            selected_code=None,
   278→            selected_system=None,
   279→            preferred_term=None,
   280→            confidence=0.0,
   281→            candidates=candidates,
   282→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   283→        )
   284→
   285→
   286→def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   287→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   288→
   289→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   290→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   291→    the output.
   292→
   293→    Args:
   294→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   295→
   296→    Returns:
   297→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   298→    """
   299→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   300→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   301→
   302→    if not google_api_key:
   303→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   304→
   305→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   306→        model=gemini_model_name,
   307→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   308→    )
   309→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   310→
   311→    prompt = (
   312→        "Extract the structured reasoning from this medical entity analysis. "
   313→        "Determine: (1) should_skip if entity is not a valid medical criterion, "
   314→        "(2) is_derived and derived_term if entity maps to a standard concept, "
   315→        "(3) rephrased_query if a better medical term would improve search. "
   316→        "You may also add a gemini_suggestion with your own reformulation if "
   317→        "you can improve on the analysis.\n\n"
   318→        f"{raw_text}"
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   322→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   323→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   324→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   325→
   326→
   327→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   328→    entity: dict,
   329→    criterion_context: str,
   330→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   331→    attempt: int = 1,
   332→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   333→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   334→
   335→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   336→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   337→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   338→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   339→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   340→
   341→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   342→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   343→
   344→    Args:
   345→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   346→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   347→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   348→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   349→
   350→    Returns:
   351→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   352→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   353→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   354→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   355→    """
   356→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   357→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   358→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   359→
   360→    logger.info(
   361→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   362→        attempt,
   363→        entity_text[:50],
   364→        entity_type,
   365→    )
   366→
   367→    try:
   368→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   369→
   370→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   371→        reasoning_prompt = _render_template(
   372→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   373→            entity_text=entity_text,
   374→            entity_type=entity_type,
   375→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   376→            previous_query=previous_query,
   377→            attempt=attempt,
   378→        )
   379→
   380→        messages = [
   381→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   382→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   383→        ]
   384→        raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   385→        raw_text = raw_response.content
   386→
   387→        logger.debug(
   388→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   389→            entity_text[:30],
   390→            raw_text[:300],
   391→        )
   392→
   393→        result = _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   394→
   395→        logger.info(
   396→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   397→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   398→            entity_text[:50],
   399→            result.should_skip,
   400→            result.is_derived,
   401→            result.derived_term,
   402→            result.rephrased_query,
   403→        )
   404→
   405→        return result
   406→
   407→    except Exception as e:
   408→        logger.error(
   409→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   410→            entity_text[:50],
   411→            e,
   412→            exc_info=True,
   413→        )
   414→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   415→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   416→            should_skip=False,
   417→            is_derived=False,
   418→            derived_term=None,
   419→            rephrased_query=None,
   420→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   421→        )
   422→
```

```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    30→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    31→
    32→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    33→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    34→    """
    35→
    36→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    37→    relation: str = Field(
    38→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'has', 'is')"
    39→    )
    40→    value: str = Field(
    41→        description=(
    42→            "The specific value or threshold (e.g. '7%', 'positive', 'confirmed')"
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    unit: str | None = Field(
    46→        default=None,
    47→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    52→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    53→
    54→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    55→        default_factory=list,
    56→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→async def generate_field_mappings(
    61→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    62→    criterion_text: str,
    63→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    64→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
    65→
    66→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
    67→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
    68→
    69→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
    70→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
    74→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value, unit.
    78→        Empty list if generation fails.
    79→    """
    80→    if not criterion_text:
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
    84→    if structured_llm is None:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
    89→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
    90→        code_context = ""
    91→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
    92→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
    93→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
    94→
    95→        prompt = (
    96→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
    97→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
    98→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
    99→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   100→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   101→            "Instructions:\n"
   102→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   103→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   104→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   105→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   106→            "- relation: the logical operator"
   107→            " (<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not)\n"
   108→            "- value: the specific threshold or value"
   109→            " (e.g. '7', 'positive', 'confirmed')\n"
   110→            "- unit: the unit if applicable (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')"
   111→            " or null\n"
   112→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   113→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   114→            " relation='has', value='confirmed'"
   115→        )
   116→
   117→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   118→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   119→
   120→        mappings = [
   121→            {
   122→                "entity": m.entity,
   123→                "relation": m.relation,
   124→                "value": m.value,
   125→                "unit": m.unit,
   126→                "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,
   127→                "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,
   128→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   129→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   130→            }
   131→            for m in response.mappings
   132→        ]
   133→
   134→        logger.info(
   135→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   136→            len(mappings),
   137→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   138→        )
   139→        return mappings
   140→
   141→    except Exception as e:
   142→        logger.warning(
   143→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   144→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   145→            e,
   146→            exc_info=True,
   147→        )
   148→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   149→        return []
   150→
```

```
     1→"""TerminologyRouter: entity-type-aware routing to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse.
     2→
     3→Routes entities to terminology APIs based on entity type using a YAML config.
     4→All 6 systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO) are accessed via the
     5→ToolUniverse SDK singleton in tooluniverse_client.py.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Entity type → API mapping stored in config file (YAML), not hardcoded.
     9→- All terminology systems accessed via ToolUniverse SDK (single dependency).
    10→- On API failure: retry transient errors; continue on permanent failures.
    11→- Consent entities explicitly skipped with logging.
    12→- Demographic entities routed normally (not skipped); MedGemma handles derived
    13→  entity mapping (age → birthDate concept, gender → SNOMED gender concepts).
    14→
    15→See config/routing.yaml for the entity type → API mapping.
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→import logging

    40→# diskcache TTL: 7 days in seconds
    41→_CACHE_TTL = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60
    42→
    43→

    45→    """Get or create the diskcache Cache instance.

    47→    Returns:
    48→        diskcache.Cache instance stored in user cache dir, or None if

    51→    try:

    66→

    71→    TerminologyRouter logs and skips these — retrying cannot fix them.
    72→    """

    74→

    78→    Loads entity type → API mapping from routing.yaml. For each entity,
    79→    queries all matching APIs via the ToolUniverse singleton client and
    80→    returns a list of GroundingCandidate objects. MedGemma then selects
```

```
    80→    returns a list of GroundingCandidate objects. MedGemma then selects
    81→    the best match downstream.
    82→
    83→    Implements error accumulation: individual API failures are logged and
    84→    skipped, not propagated. The entity is still grounded by remaining APIs.
    85→
    86→    Usage:
    87→        router = TerminologyRouter()
    88→        candidates = await router.route_entity("metformin", "Medication")
    89→        # candidates: [GroundingCandidate(source_api="rxnorm", ...), ...]
    90→
    91→    Attributes:
    92→        config: Parsed YAML config dict.
    93→        config_path: Path to the routing.yaml file used.
    94→    """
    95→
    96→    def __init__(self, config_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    97→        """Initialize TerminologyRouter with routing config.
    98→
    99→        Args:
   100→            config_path: Path to routing.yaml. Defaults to config/routing.yaml
   101→                relative to this package.
   102→        """
   103→        self.config_path = config_path or _DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
   104→        with open(self.config_path) as f:
   105→            self.config: dict = yaml.safe_load(f)
   106→
   107→    def get_apis_for_entity(self, entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
   108→        """Get list of API names to query for this entity type.
   109→
   110→        Returns an empty list for entity types with skip: true in routing config
   111→        (e.g. Consent) and for entity types not found in the routing config.
   112→        Both cases are logged.
   113→
   114→        Args:
   115→            entity_type: Entity type string (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
   116→
   117→        Returns:
   118→            List of API names to query, or empty list if entity should be skipped.
   119→        """
   120→        routing_rules: dict = self.config.get("routing_rules", {})
   121→        rule = routing_rules.get(entity_type)
   122→
   123→        if rule is None:
   124→            logger.warning(
   125→                "Unknown entity type '%s' — not in routing config. "
   126→                "Skipping grounding. Add to config/routing.yaml to enable.",
   127→                entity_type,
   128→            )
   129→            return []
   130→
   131→        # Explicit skip (e.g. Consent: {skip: true})
   132→        if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("skip"):
   133→            logger.info(
   134→                "Entity type '%s' is explicitly skipped in routing config "
   135→                "(no terminology grounding for this type).",
   136→                entity_type,
   137→            )
   138→            return []
   139→
   140→        if isinstance(rule, list):
   141→            return rule
   142→
   143→        # Unexpected rule format — treat as unknown
   144→        logger.warning(
   145→            "Unexpected routing rule format for '%s': %r. Skipping.",
   146→            entity_type,
   147→            rule,
   148→        )
   149→        return []
   150→
   151→    async def route_entity(
   152→        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str
   153→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   154→        """Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.
   155→
   156→        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.
   157→        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).
   158→        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates
   159→        and selects the best match.
   160→
   161→        Args:
   162→            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin").
   163→            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. "Medication").
   164→
   165→        Returns:
   166→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.
   167→            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.
   168→        """
   169→        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   170→
   171→        if not api_names:
   172→            return []
   173→
   174→        all_candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   175→
   176→        for api_name in api_names:
   177→            try:
   178→                candidates = await self._query_tooluniverse(api_name, entity_text)
   179→                all_candidates.extend(candidates)
   180→
   181→            except PermanentAPIError as e:
   182→                # Non-retriable: log and skip this API
   183→                logger.error(
   184→                    "Permanent API error from '%s' for entity '%s': %s",
   185→                    api_name,
   186→                    entity_text,
   187→                    e,
   188→                )
   189→            except Exception as e:
   190→                # Transient/unexpected: log and skip (tenacity already retried)
   191→                logger.error(
   192→                    "API '%s' failed for entity '%s' after retries: %s",
   193→                    api_name,
   194→                    entity_text,
   195→                    e,
   196→                )
   197→
   198→        return all_candidates
   199→
   200→    @retry(
   201→        retry=retry_if_exception_type(TransientAPIError),
   202→        stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   203→        wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   204→        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   205→        reraise=True,
   206→    )
   207→    async def _query_tooluniverse(
   208→        self, api_name: str, entity_text: str
   209→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   210→        """Query terminology system via ToolUniverse client wrapper.
   211→
   212→        Uses the singleton search_terminology function which handles tool
   213→        dispatch, response parsing, and in-memory TTL caching.
   214→
   215→        Retries on TransientAPIError with exponential backoff (3 attempts max).
   216→
   217→        Args:
   218→            api_name: Terminology system name (e.g. "umls", "icd10").
   219→            entity_text: Term to search.
   220→
   221→        Returns:
   222→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from ToolUniverse.
   223→
   224→        Raises:
   225→            TransientAPIError: On retriable errors (rate limit, timeout, 5xx).
   226→        """
   227→        from protocol_processor.tools.tooluniverse_client import search_terminology
   228→
   229→        candidates = await search_terminology(api_name, entity_text, max_results=10)
   230→        return candidates
   231→
```

```
     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import logging
    11→import os
    12→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    13→
    14→from pydantic import BaseModel
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
    19→
    20→
    21→def create_structured_llm(
    22→    output_schema: type[T],
    23→) -> Any | None:
    24→    """Create a Gemini LLM client with structured output.
    25→
    26→    Guards on GOOGLE_API_KEY. Uses late import of ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    27→    to avoid import errors when the package is not installed.
    28→
    29→    Args:
    30→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
    31→
    32→    Returns:
    33→        A structured LLM instance, or None if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set
    34→        or client creation fails.
    35→    """
    36→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    37→    if not google_api_key:
    38→        logger.warning("GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping Gemini call")
    39→        return None
    40→
    41→    try:
    42→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    43→
    44→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    45→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    46→            model=gemini_model_name,
    47→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    48→        )
    49→        return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    50→    except Exception as e:
    51→        logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
    52→        return None
    53→
    54→
    55→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
    56→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
    57→
    58→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
    59→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
    60→
    61→    Args:
    62→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
    63→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
    64→
    65→    Returns:
    66→        Validated model instance.
    67→    """
    68→    if isinstance(result, dict):
    69→        return model.model_validate(result)
    70→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
    71→
```

```
     1→"""ToolUniverse client wrapper for terminology system lookups.
     2→
     3→Provides a module-level singleton ToolUniverse instance with in-memory TTL
     4→caching for autocomplete and pipeline grounding. Exposes a single
     5→`search_terminology(system, query)` function for all 6 medical terminology
     6→systems: UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO.
     7→
     8→Architecture:
     9→- Singleton via @lru_cache(maxsize=1) on _get_tu()
    10→- Two-layer cache: exact-match TTLCache + prefix-aware autocomplete cache
    11→- Selective tool loading (5 categories, ~18 tools) rather than all 1495
    12→- Response parsing handles each system's unique return format
    13→- Retry on transient 502/503 errors (3 attempts, exponential backoff)
    14→- Circuit breaker trips after 10 consecutive failures (60s cooldown)
    15→- Sentence-length queries pre-processed to extract medical terms
    16→
    17→Caching strategy for autocomplete:
    18→  Typing "hyp" → "hype" → "hyper" used to make 3 API calls (different keys).
    19→  Now the prefix cache checks: if we already have results for "hyp" and the
    20→  new query "hype" starts with "hyp", we filter the cached "hyp" results
    21→  client-side by checking if preferred_term contains "hype". This avoids
    22→  redundant API calls for progressive typing.
    23→
    24→Per user decision: ToolUniverse SDK for all 6 terminology systems (UMLS,
    25→SNOMED, RxNorm, ICD-10, LOINC, HPO) — single SDK, single dependency.
    26→
    27→See 40-RESEARCH.md for tool names, response formats, and latency info.
    28→"""
    29→
    30→from __future__ import annotations
    31→
    32→import logging
    33→import re
    34→from functools import lru_cache
    35→from typing import Any
    36→
    37→import pybreaker
    38→from cachetools import TTLCache  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    39→from shared.resilience import tu_breaker
    40→from tenacity import (
    41→    before_sleep_log,
    42→    retry,
    43→    retry_if_exception_type,
    44→    stop_after_attempt,
    45→    wait_exponential,
    46→)
    47→from tooluniverse import ToolUniverse  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    48→
    49→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import GroundingCandidate
    50→
    51→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    52→
    53→
    54→class ToolUniverseError(Exception):
    55→    """ToolUniverse returned an error dict response."""
    56→
    57→    pass
    58→
    59→
    60→class ToolUniverseUnavailableError(ToolUniverseError):
    61→    """Transient 502/503 error — eligible for retry."""
    62→
    63→    pass
    64→
    65→
    66→# Sentence extraction thresholds
    67→_SENTENCE_WORD_THRESHOLD = 4
    68→_SENTENCE_CHAR_THRESHOLD = 40
    69→
    70→# Regex to strip clinical preamble from sentence-length queries before search.
    71→# Matches common patterns like "patients must have", "history of", "diagnosis of", etc.
    72→_PREAMBLE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    73→    r"^(?:patient(?:s)?\s+(?:must|should|may|with|who|have|has|had|are|is)\s+"
    74→    r"|(?:has|have|had|is|are|must|should|may)\s+(?:a|an|the|confirmed|known|documented|active|history\s+of)\s+"
    75→    r"|(?:history\s+of|presence\s+of|absence\s+of|diagnosis\s+of)\s+)",
    76→    re.IGNORECASE,
    77→)
    78→
    79→
    80→def _extract_term_from_sentence(query: str) -> str:
    81→    """Extract medical term from sentence-length query.
    82→
    83→    Sentence-length queries (>= 4 words or >= 40 chars) are pre-processed
    84→    to remove clinical preamble before searching. This prevents 422 errors
    85→    from APIs that don't accept full sentences as search terms.
    86→
    87→    Args:
    88→        query: Raw query string from user or pipeline.
    89→
    90→    Returns:
    91→        Extracted medical term, or original query if it's short.
    92→    """
    93→    stripped = query.strip()
    94→    word_count = len(stripped.split())
    95→    too_short = (
    96→        word_count < _SENTENCE_WORD_THRESHOLD
    97→        or len(stripped) < _SENTENCE_CHAR_THRESHOLD
    98→    )
    99→    if too_short:
   100→        return stripped
   101→    cleaned = _PREAMBLE_PATTERN.sub("", stripped).strip()
   102→    if cleaned and cleaned != stripped and len(cleaned) >= 3:
   103→        logger.debug("Extracted term '%s' from sentence '%s'", cleaned, stripped[:60])
   104→        return cleaned
   105→    # Fallback: take last 4 words as the medical term
   106→    words = stripped.split()
   107→    return " ".join(words[-min(4, len(words)) :])
   108→
   109→
   110→# Tool categories to load (subset of 1495 total tools — only medical terminology)
   111→_TOOL_CATEGORIES = ["umls", "icd", "loinc", "rxnorm", "hpo"]
   112→
   113→# In-memory result cache: (tool_name, normalized_query, max_results) → candidates
   114→# TTL = 300s (5 minutes) — appropriate for autocomplete endpoints
   115→_CACHE: TTLCache = TTLCache(maxsize=1000, ttl=300)
   116→
   117→# Tool name per system (verified 2026-02-17 with ToolUniverse 1.0.18)
   118→_SYSTEM_TOOL_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
   119→    "umls": "umls_search_concepts",
   120→    "snomed": "snomed_search_concepts",
   121→    "icd10": "ICD10_search_codes",
   122→    "loinc": "LOINC_search_tests",
   123→    "rxnorm": "RxNorm_get_drug_names",
   124→    "hpo": "HPO_search_terms",
   125→}
   126→
   127→
   128→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   129→def _get_tu() -> ToolUniverse:
   130→    """Get or create singleton ToolUniverse instance.
   131→
   132→    lru_cache(maxsize=1) ensures only one ToolUniverse is created per process.
   133→    load_tools() with selective categories takes ~0.002s vs ~0.3s for all tools.
   134→
   135→    Returns:
   136→        Initialized ToolUniverse instance with medical terminology tools loaded.
   137→    """
   138→    tu = ToolUniverse()
   139→    tu.load_tools(tool_type=_TOOL_CATEGORIES)
   140→    logger.info("ToolUniverse initialized (categories: %s)", _TOOL_CATEGORIES)
   141→    return tu
   142→
   143→
   144→def _check_prefix_cache(
   145→    tool_name: str,
   146→    normalized_query: str,
   147→    max_results: int,
   148→) -> list[GroundingCandidate] | None:
   149→    """Check if a shorter (prefix) query is cached and filter its results.
   150→
   151→    For autocomplete: if the user typed "hyp" and we cached results, then
   152→    typing "hype" can reuse those results by filtering on preferred_term.
   153→
   154→    We only reuse when the cached shorter query returned fewer results than
   155→    max_results (meaning the API exhausted all matches — a longer query
   156→    can only produce a subset) OR when we can filter the cached results
   157→    to find matches containing the longer query.
   158→
   159→    Args:
   160→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name (cache key component).
   161→        normalized_query: Current query, stripped and lowered.
   162→        max_results: Max results requested.
   163→
   164→    Returns:
   165→        Filtered candidate list if a prefix match was found, None otherwise.
   166→    """
   167→    for (cached_tool, cached_query, cached_max), cached_candidates in list(
   168→        _CACHE.items()
   169→    ):
   170→        if cached_tool != tool_name or cached_max != max_results:
   171→            continue
   172→        # Check if cached query is a prefix of the new query
   173→        starts_with = normalized_query.startswith(cached_query)
   174→        is_prefix = starts_with and cached_query != normalized_query
   175→        if not is_prefix:
   176→            continue
   177→
   178→        # Filter cached results: keep candidates whose preferred_term
   179→        # contains the longer query (case-insensitive)
   180→        filtered = [
   181→            c for c in cached_candidates if normalized_query in c.preferred_term.lower()
   182→        ]
   183→
   184→        # If the original shorter query returned fewer than max_results,
   185→        # the API had no more matches — any longer query is a strict subset.
   186→        # Safe to return the filtered list even if empty.
   187→        if len(cached_candidates) < max_results:
   188→            return filtered
   189→
   190→        # If the shorter query returned max_results (truncated), we can still
   191→        # return filtered results if we got some. But if filtered is empty,
   192→        # we can't be sure — there might be results the API would return
   193→        # for the longer query that weren't in the truncated shorter results.
   194→        if filtered:
   195→            return filtered
   196→
   197→        # Truncated + no filtered matches → must query the API
   198→        return None
   199→
   200→    return None
   201→
   202→
   203→async def search_terminology(
   204→    system: str,
   205→    query: str,
   206→    max_results: int = 10,
   207→    use_cache: bool = True,
   208→) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   209→    """Search a terminology system via ToolUniverse SDK.
   210→
   211→    Routes the query to the appropriate ToolUniverse tool for the given system,
   212→    parses the response into GroundingCandidate objects, and caches results.
   213→
   214→    Args:
   215→        system: Terminology system to search. One of: umls, snomed, icd10,
   216→            loinc, rxnorm, hpo.
   217→        query: Clinical search term (e.g. "hypertension", "metformin").
   218→        max_results: Maximum number of candidates to return.
   219→        use_cache: Whether to use in-memory TTL cache (default True).
   220→            Set False for pipeline runs that need fresh results.
   221→
   222→    Returns:
   223→        List of GroundingCandidate objects. Empty list on any failure
   224→        (missing API key, network error, unknown system, empty results).
   225→    """
   226→    tool_name = _SYSTEM_TOOL_MAP.get(system)
   227→    if not tool_name:
   228→        logger.warning("Unknown terminology system requested: '%s'", system)
   229→        return []
   230→
   231→    # Pre-process sentence-length queries to extract medical terms.
   232→    # This prevents 422 errors caused by submitting full sentences to term search APIs.
   233→    query = _extract_term_from_sentence(query)
   234→
   235→    normalized_query = query.strip().lower()
   236→    cache_key = (tool_name, normalized_query, max_results)
   237→
   238→    if use_cache:
   239→        # Layer 1: exact match
   240→        if cache_key in _CACHE:
   241→            logger.debug("Cache hit (exact) for %s '%s'", system, query)
   242→            return _CACHE[cache_key]
   243→
   244→        # Layer 2: prefix match — reuse results from a shorter query
   245→        prefix_result = _check_prefix_cache(tool_name, normalized_query, max_results)
   246→        if prefix_result is not None:
   247→            logger.debug("Cache hit (prefix) for %s '%s'", system, query)
   248→            # Store the filtered result under the exact key too
   249→            _CACHE[cache_key] = prefix_result
   250→            return prefix_result
   251→
   252→    tu = _get_tu()
   253→    try:
   254→        raw = await _call_tool_with_resilience(
   255→            tu, system, tool_name, query, max_results
   256→        )
   257→    except pybreaker.CircuitBreakerError:
   258→        logger.warning(
   259→            "Circuit breaker open for ToolUniverse — returning cached/empty for '%s'",
   260→            query,
   261→        )
   262→        # Return cached results if available (TTLCache may still have them)
   263→        if use_cache and cache_key in _CACHE:
   264→            return _CACHE[cache_key]
   265→        return []
   266→    except ToolUniverseError as e:
   267→        logger.warning("ToolUniverse error for '%s': %s", query, e)
   268→        return []
   269→    candidates = _parse_result(system, tool_name, query, raw)
   270→
   271→    if use_cache:
   272→        _CACHE[cache_key] = candidates
   273→
   274→    logger.debug(
   275→        "ToolUniverse %s for '%s': %d candidates",
   276→        tool_name,
   277→        query,
   278→        len(candidates),
   279→    )
   280→    return candidates
   281→
   282→
   283→async def _call_tool_raw(
   284→    tu: ToolUniverse,
   285→    system: str,
   286→    tool_name: str,
   287→    query: str,
   288→    max_results: int,
   289→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   290→    """Execute a single ToolUniverse tool call (raw, no retry/circuit breaker).
   291→
   292→    Constructs the correct argument dict per system's tool specification
   293→    and calls tu.run(). Returns {} on any exception.
   294→
   295→    Args:
   296→        tu: ToolUniverse singleton instance.
   297→        system: System identifier for argument construction.
   298→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name to call.
   299→        query: Search term.
   300→        max_results: Max results to request.
   301→
   302→    Returns:
   303→        Raw response dict from ToolUniverse, or empty dict on error.
   304→    """
   305→    args: dict[str, Any]
   306→    if system in ("umls", "snomed"):
   307→        args = {"query": query, "pageSize": min(max_results, 25)}
   308→    elif system == "icd10":
   309→        args = {"query": query, "limit": min(max_results, 100)}
   310→    elif system == "loinc":
   311→        args = {"terms": query, "max_results": min(max_results, 20)}
   312→    elif system == "rxnorm":
   313→        args = {"drug_name": query}
   314→    elif system == "hpo":
   315→        args = {"query": query, "max_results": min(max_results, 50)}
   316→    else:
   317→        logger.warning("No argument mapping for system '%s'", system)
   318→        return {}
   319→
   320→    try:
   321→        return await tu.run({"name": tool_name, "arguments": args})  # type: ignore[return-value]
   322→    except Exception:
   323→        logger.exception("ToolUniverse %s failed for query '%s'", tool_name, query)
   324→        return {}
   325→
   326→
   327→@tu_breaker
   328→@retry(
   329→    retry=retry_if_exception_type(ToolUniverseUnavailableError),
   330→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   331→    wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   332→    before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   333→    reraise=True,
   334→)
   335→async def _call_tool_with_resilience(
   336→    tu: ToolUniverse,
   337→    system: str,
   338→    tool_name: str,
   339→    query: str,
   340→    max_results: int,
   341→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   342→    """Call ToolUniverse with retry on transient errors and circuit breaker.
   343→
   344→    Decorator order: @tu_breaker (outer) wraps @retry (inner). This means
   345→    tenacity retries up to 3 times before counting as 1 circuit breaker failure.
   346→
   347→    ToolUniverse SDK catches HTTP exceptions internally and returns error dicts
   348→    instead of raising. This function detects those error dicts and converts
   349→    them to typed exceptions for retry/breaker interception.
   350→
   351→    Args:
   352→        tu: ToolUniverse singleton instance.
   353→        system: System identifier for argument construction.
   354→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name to call.
   355→        query: Search term (already extracted from sentence if needed).
   356→        max_results: Max results to request.
   357→
   358→    Returns:
   359→        Raw response dict from ToolUniverse.
   360→
   361→    Raises:
   362→        ToolUniverseUnavailableError: On 502/503/timeout — triggers retry.
   363→        ToolUniverseError: On other non-transient errors — no retry.
   364→    """
   365→    raw = await _call_tool_raw(tu, system, tool_name, query, max_results)
   366→
   367→    # Detect error dict: ToolUniverse catches HTTP exceptions internally
   368→    # and returns {"error": "..."} dicts instead of raising.
   369→    if isinstance(raw, dict) and "error" in raw:
   370→        error_msg = str(raw["error"])
   371→        transient_markers = ("502", "503", "Service Unavailable", "timeout", "Timeout")
   372→        if any(s in error_msg for s in transient_markers):
   373→            raise ToolUniverseUnavailableError(f"Transient error: {error_msg}")
   374→        raise ToolUniverseError(f"Non-transient error: {error_msg}")
   375→
   376→    return raw
   377→
   378→
   379→def _parse_result(  # noqa: C901
   380→    system: str,
   381→    tool_name: str,
   382→    query: str,
   383→    raw: dict[str, Any],
   384→) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   385→    """Parse ToolUniverse response into GroundingCandidate list.
   386→
   387→    Each system returns a different response format. This function handles
   388→    all formats with graceful degradation on unexpected shapes.
   389→
   390→    Args:
   391→        system: System identifier for format dispatch.
   392→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name (for error logging).
   393→        query: Original query (used as fallback for preferred_term).
   394→        raw: Raw ToolUniverse response dict.
   395→
   396→    Returns:
   397→        List of GroundingCandidate objects. Empty on error or no results.
   398→    """
   399→    if not raw:
   400→        return []
   401→
   402→    if "error" in raw:
   403→        logger.warning(
   404→            "ToolUniverse %s returned error: %s",
   405→            tool_name,
   406→            raw["error"],
   407→        )
   408→        return []
   409→
   410→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   411→
   412→    if system in ("umls", "snomed"):
   413→        # UMLSRESTTool: data.result.results[].{ui, name, rootSource}
   414→        # Note: snomed_search_concepts returns UMLS CUIs (not SNOMED codes)
   415→        # rootSource="SNOMEDCT_US" confirms SNOMED provenance.
   416→        results = raw.get("data", {}).get("result", {}).get("results", [])
   417→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   418→            code = r.get("ui", "")
   419→            if not code or code == "NONE":
   420→                continue
   421→            candidates.append(
   422→                GroundingCandidate(
   423→                    source_api=system,
   424→                    code=code,
   425→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   426→                    semantic_type=r.get("rootSource"),
   427→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   428→                )
   429→            )
   430→
   431→    elif system == "icd10":
   432→        # ICD10Tool: data.results[].{code, name}
   433→        results = raw.get("data", {}).get("results", [])
   434→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   435→            code = r.get("code", "")
   436→            if not code:
   437→                continue
   438→            candidates.append(
   439→                GroundingCandidate(
   440→                    source_api="icd10",
   441→                    code=code,
   442→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   443→                    semantic_type=None,
   444→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   445→                )
   446→            )
   447→
   448→    elif system == "loinc":
   449→        # LOINCTool: results[].{code/LOINC_NUM, LONG_COMMON_NAME}
   450→        results = raw.get("results", [])
   451→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   452→            code = r.get("code") or r.get("LOINC_NUM", "")
   453→            if not code:
   454→                continue
   455→            candidates.append(
   456→                GroundingCandidate(
   457→                    source_api="loinc",
   458→                    code=code,
   459→                    preferred_term=r.get("LONG_COMMON_NAME", query),
   460→                    semantic_type=None,
   461→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   462→                )
   463→            )
   464→
   465→    elif system == "rxnorm":
   466→        # RxNormTool: single result dict {rxcui, drug_name, names}
   467→        # Returns ONE best match, not a list.
   468→        # RxNorm focuses on specific drug names, not drug classes.
   469→        # Drug class terms (e.g., "beta-blockers", "ACE inhibitors") may return
   470→        # no RXCUI — the TerminologyRouter falls through to UMLS for these.
   471→        rxcui = raw.get("rxcui", "")
   472→        if rxcui:
   473→            candidates.append(
   474→                GroundingCandidate(
   475→                    source_api="rxnorm",
   476→                    code=rxcui,
   477→                    preferred_term=raw.get("drug_name", query),
   478→                    semantic_type=None,
   479→                    score=0.9,
   480→                )
   481→            )
   482→
   483→    elif system == "hpo":
   484→        # HPOTool: data[].{id, name, definition}
   485→        results = raw.get("data", [])
   486→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   487→            code = r.get("id", "")
   488→            if not code:
   489→                continue
   490→            candidates.append(
   491→                GroundingCandidate(
   492→                    source_api="hpo",
   493→                    code=code,
   494→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   495→                    semantic_type=None,
   496→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   497→                )
   498→            )
   499→
   500→    return candidates
   501→
```

```
     1→"""OMOP vocabulary mapper: look up standard OMOP concept_ids for medical entities.
     2→
     3→Queries Athena vocabulary tables (concept, concept_synonym) in a dedicated
     4→OMOP vocabulary database. Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable
     5→pointing to a Postgres instance with loaded Athena vocabulary data.
     6→
     7→This tool provides the OMOP grounding leg of the dual-grounding pipeline:
     8→- TerminologyRouter provides UMLS/SNOMED/RxNorm/ICD-10/LOINC/HPO codes
     9→- OmopMapper provides OMOP standard concept_ids for CIRCE export and CDM joins
    10→
    11→Connection: Uses a dedicated SQLAlchemy engine created from OMOP_VOCAB_URL.
    12→This is separate from the main app database (DATABASE_URL). If OMOP_VOCAB_URL
    13→is not set, lookup_omop_concept raises RuntimeError — callers must handle this
    14→explicitly rather than silently receiving empty results.
    15→
    16→All DB I/O is synchronous SQLAlchemy wrapped in run_in_executor for async
    17→compatibility.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations

    34→# Configuration
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE: float = 0.3
    38→"""Minimum fuzzy-match score to accept a candidate. Below this, return None."""
    39→
    40→OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES: int = 50
    41→"""Maximum number of rows to fetch from each SQL query (LIMIT clause)."""

    44→# Dedicated OMOP vocabulary engine (lazy singleton)
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→_omop_engine: Any = None
    48→
    49→
    50→def _get_omop_engine() -> Any:
    51→    """Return the OMOP vocabulary SQLAlchemy engine.
    52→
    53→    Creates a dedicated engine from OMOP_VOCAB_URL on first call.
    54→    Raises RuntimeError if the env var is not set — callers must
    55→    handle this rather than silently degrading.
    56→    """
    57→    global _omop_engine  # noqa: PLW0603
    58→    if _omop_engine is not None:
    59→        return _omop_engine
    60→
    61→    omop_url = os.getenv("OMOP_VOCAB_URL")
    62→    if not omop_url:
    63→        raise RuntimeError(
    64→            "OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable is not set. "
    65→            "Set it to the OMOP vocabulary Postgres connection string "
    66→            "(e.g. postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5433/omop_vocab) "
    67→            "or start the omop-vocab container: "
    68→            "docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml --profile omop up -d"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    _omop_engine = create_engine(omop_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    72→    logger.info("OMOP vocabulary engine created: %s", omop_url.split("@")[-1])
    73→    return _omop_engine
    74→

    77→# Entity type -> OMOP domain mapping
```

```
   143→def _score_candidates(
   144→    entity_text: str, candidates: list[dict[str, Any]]
   145→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   146→    """Score and sort candidates by fuzzy string similarity to *entity_text*.
   147→
   148→    Uses ``difflib.SequenceMatcher`` ratio as the base score, with a bonus
   149→    for exact case-insensitive substring containment.
   150→
   151→    Args:
   152→        entity_text: The original entity text to match against.
   153→        candidates: List of candidate dicts, each with at least a
   154→            ``"match_text"`` key containing the string to compare.
   155→
   156→    Returns:
   157→        The same candidate dicts augmented with a ``"score"`` key, sorted
   158→        in descending order of score.
   159→    """
   160→    entity_lower = entity_text.lower().strip()
   161→
   162→    for candidate in candidates:
   163→        match_text = candidate.get("match_text", "").lower().strip()
   164→
   165→        # Base score: SequenceMatcher ratio
   166→        base_score = SequenceMatcher(None, entity_lower, match_text).ratio()
   167→
   168→        # Bonus for exact substring containment (either direction)
   169→        bonus = 0.0
   170→        if entity_lower in match_text or match_text in entity_lower:
   171→            bonus = 0.15
   172→
   173→        # Bonus for exact match
   174→        if entity_lower == match_text:
   175→            bonus = 0.25
   176→
   177→        candidate["score"] = min(base_score + bonus, 1.0)
   178→
```

```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Find ground_node and pipeline traces - all of them\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT ti.request_id, ti.timestamp_ms, ti.execution_time_ms, ti.status,\n       GROUP_CONCAT(tt.key || '=' || tt.value, ' | ') as tags\nFROM trace_info ti\nLEFT JOIN trace_tags tt ON ti.request_id = tt.request_id\nWHERE tt.key = 'mlflow.traceName' AND (\n    tt.value LIKE '%ground%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%extract%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%parse%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%persist%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%ingest%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%structure%' OR\n    tt.value LIKE '%pipeline%'\n)\nGROUP BY ti.request_id\nORDER BY ti.execution_time_ms DESC\nLIMIT 50\n''')\nrows = cursor.fetchall()\nprint(f'Pipeline-related traces: {len(rows)}')\nfor row in rows:\n    rid, ts, exec_ms, status, tags = row\n    # extract the traceName tag\n    trace_name = ''\n    if tags:\n        for t in tags.split(' | '):\n            if 'traceName' in t:\n                trace_name = t.split('=', 1)[1]\n                break\n    print(f'  [{exec_ms:>8}ms] {trace_name} ({status})')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Find ground/extract/parse/persist pipeline traces sorted by execution time"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Get ALL traces, count and top slowest\ncursor.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trace_info')\ntotal = cursor.fetchone()[0]\nprint(f'Total traces in DB: {total}')\n\n# Get all trace names\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT tt.value as trace_name, COUNT(*) as count, \n       AVG(ti.execution_time_ms) as avg_ms, \n       MAX(ti.execution_time_ms) as max_ms,\n       MIN(ti.execution_time_ms) as min_ms\nFROM trace_info ti\nJOIN trace_tags tt ON ti.request_id = tt.request_id AND tt.key = 'mlflow.traceName'\nGROUP BY tt.value\nORDER BY avg_ms DESC\nLIMIT 40\n''')\nrows = cursor.fetchall()\nprint(f'\\nTrace name breakdown (sorted by avg duration):')\nfor row in rows:\n    name, count, avg_ms, max_ms, min_ms = row\n    print(f'  {name[:60]:<60} count={count:>4}  avg={avg_ms:>8.0f}ms  max={max_ms:>8}ms  min={min_ms:>8}ms')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Analyze all MLflow traces grouped by trace name with timing stats"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Analyze the 7 LangGraph traces with all spans\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT ti.request_id, ti.timestamp_ms, ti.execution_time_ms, ti.status,\n       GROUP_CONCAT(tt.key || '=' || tt.value, ' | ') as tags\nFROM trace_info ti\nJOIN trace_tags tt ON ti.request_id = tt.request_id\nWHERE ti.request_id IN (\n    SELECT ti2.request_id FROM trace_info ti2\n    JOIN trace_tags tt2 ON ti2.request_id = tt2.request_id\n    WHERE tt2.key = 'mlflow.traceName' AND tt2.value = 'LangGraph'\n)\nGROUP BY ti.request_id\nORDER BY ti.execution_time_ms DESC\n''')\nrows = cursor.fetchall()\nprint('LangGraph traces:')\nfor row in rows:\n    rid, ts, exec_ms, status, tags = row\n    print(f'  request_id={rid[:40]} exec={exec_ms:>10}ms ({exec_ms/1000:.1f}s) status={status}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Analyze all LangGraph trace durations"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Deep dive into spans for the longest LangGraph trace\ntrace_id = 'tr-c04285b129981bb9f1222244f9772240'\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT span_id, parent_span_id, name, type, status,\n       start_time_unix_nano, end_time_unix_nano, content\nFROM spans\nWHERE trace_id = ?\nORDER BY start_time_unix_nano ASC\n''', (trace_id,))\nrows = cursor.fetchall()\nprint(f'Spans for longest LangGraph trace ({trace_id[:20]}... 1214s):')\nprint(f'Total spans: {len(rows)}')\nprint()\nfor row in rows[:60]:\n    sid, psid, name, typ, status, start_ns, end_ns, content = row\n    duration_ms = (end_ns - start_ns) / 1_000_000 if (start_ns and end_ns) else 0\n    parent = psid[:12] if psid else 'ROOT'\n    content_preview = ''\n    if content:\n        try:\n            c = json.loads(content)\n            inputs = c.get('inputs', {})\n            outputs = c.get('outputs', {})\n            content_preview = str(inputs)[:80] if inputs else str(outputs)[:80]\n        except:\n            content_preview = str(content)[:80]\n    print(f'  [{duration_ms:>10.0f}ms] {name[:50]:<50} type={typ:<10} parent={parent}')\n    if content_preview:\n        print(f'               content={content_preview}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Analyze spans for the longest LangGraph trace"
}
```

id: toolu_01CDi9UGicdYCakyAFFAZz9Q
```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Analyze the longest LangGraph trace - group spans by type and summarize\ntrace_id = 'tr-c04285b129981bb9f1222244f9772240'\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT span_id, parent_span_id, name, type, status,\n       start_time_unix_nano, end_time_unix_nano, content\nFROM spans\nWHERE trace_id = ?\nORDER BY start_time_unix_nano ASC\n''', (trace_id,))\nrows = cursor.fetchall()\n\n# Categorize and sum up times\nnode_times = {}\nllm_times = []\nfor row in rows:\n    sid, psid, name, typ, status, start_ns, end_ns, content = row\n    dur_ms = (end_ns - start_ns) / 1_000_000 if (start_ns and end_ns) else 0\n    key = name if name not in ('RunnableSequence', 'ChatGoogleGenerativeAI') else name\n    if key not in node_times:\n        node_times[key] = {'count': 0, 'total_ms': 0, 'max_ms': 0}\n    node_times[key]['count'] += 1\n    node_times[key]['total_ms'] += dur_ms\n    node_times[key]['max_ms'] = max(node_times[key]['max_ms'], dur_ms)\n    if typ == 'CHAT_MODEL' or name == 'ChatGoogleGenerativeAI':\n        llm_times.append(dur_ms)\n\nprint('Span summary by name (sorted by total time):')\nfor name, stats in sorted(node_times.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]['total_ms']):\n    print(f'  {name[:50]:<50} count={stats[\\\"count\\\"]:>4}  total={stats[\\\"total_ms\\\"]/1000:>8.1f}s  max={stats[\\\"max_ms\\\"]/1000:>6.1f}s')\n\nprint()\nif llm_times:\n    import statistics\n    print(f'LLM calls: {len(llm_times)} calls')\n    print(f'  Total: {sum(llm_times)/1000:.1f}s')\n    print(f'  Avg: {statistics.mean(llm_times):.0f}ms')\n    print(f'  Max: {max(llm_times):.0f}ms')\n    print(f'  Min: {min(llm_times):.0f}ms')\n    print(f'  Median: {statistics.median(llm_times):.0f}ms')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Summarize span types and LLM call statistics for longest trace"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3, json\nconn = sqlite3.connect('.mlflow/mlflow.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Analyze all 7 LangGraph traces\ncursor.execute('''\nSELECT request_id, execution_time_ms FROM trace_info\nWHERE request_id IN (\n    SELECT ti2.request_id FROM trace_info ti2\n    JOIN trace_tags tt2 ON ti2.request_id = tt2.request_id\n    WHERE tt2.key = 'mlflow.traceName' AND tt2.value = 'LangGraph'\n)\nORDER BY execution_time_ms DESC\n''')\ntraces = cursor.fetchall()\n\nprint('All LangGraph traces - per-node breakdown:\\n')\nfor trace_id, total_ms in traces:\n    cursor.execute('''\n    SELECT name, \n           COUNT(*) as count,\n           SUM(end_time_unix_nano - start_time_unix_nano) / 1000000.0 as total_ms,\n           MAX(end_time_unix_nano - start_time_unix_nano) / 1000000.0 as max_ms\n    FROM spans\n    WHERE trace_id = ? \n      AND name NOT IN ('should_continue', 'PydanticOutputParser', 'LangGraph')\n      AND end_time_unix_nano > start_time_unix_nano\n    GROUP BY name\n    ORDER BY total_ms DESC\n    ''', (trace_id,))\n    spans = cursor.fetchall()\n    print(f'--- Trace {trace_id[:30]}... ({total_ms/1000:.1f}s total) ---')\n    for name, count, t_ms, max_ms in spans:\n        print(f'  {name[:40]:<40} count={count:>4} total={t_ms/1000:>7.1f}s max={max_ms/1000:>6.1f}s')\n    print()\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Per-node breakdown for all 7 LangGraph traces"
}
```

# Root Cause Report: Pipeline Slowness & Bottleneck Analysis

**Source:** MLflow trace DB (`.mlflow/mlflow.db`) — 7 LangGraph runs, 5,645 total traces analyzed

---

## TL;DR

Tests are slow because they run the full extraction-grounding loop against real APIs with no entity limiting. The ground node alone consumes **94–95% of total pipeline time**, driven by a combination of extremely high-latency MedGemma (Vertex AI Model Garden) calls, synchronous Gemini calls that serialize the async event loop, and a per-entity LLM count of 2–7 calls.

---

## Section 1: Observed Timing From MLflow Traces

| Trace | Total Wall Time | Ground Node | Extract | Parse | Ingest/Persist/Other |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Run 1 | **1214s (20min)** | 1147s (94%) | 60s | 6.4s | <1s |
| Run 2 | **956s (16min)** | 861s (90%) | 89s | 5.9s | <1s |
| Run 3 | **922s (15min)** | 858s (93%) | 52s | 12s | <1s |
| Run 4 | **228s** | 196s (86%) | 24s | 7.7s | <1s |
| Run 5 | **184s** | 159s (86%) | 22s | 3.4s | <1s |
| Run 6 | **181s** | 155s (86%) | 19s | 6.9s | <1s |
| Run 7 | **20s** | *(no ground node)* | 20s | 0ms | <1s |

**The ground node is the singular bottleneck**, consuming 86–94% of end-to-end pipeline time. The final three nodes (structure, ordinal_resolve, persist) together take under 1 second and are irrelevant to performance.

---

## Section 2: Root Cause Breakdown

### RC-1: Full Protocol Scale in Tests — No Entity Limiting

**The immediate cause of long test times.**

The `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES` env vars exist specifically for this (see `parse.py:71`, `ground.py:518`), but they are never set in the test suite. Real protocols produce 53–79 entities, each triggering multiple API calls.

**Fix:** Set `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=3` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` in `tests/conftest.py` or individual grounding test fixtures.

---

### RC-2: Synchronous Gemini Calls Serialize the asyncio Event Loop

**The most impactful architectural bug — negates all parallelism.**

The ground node runs entities with `asyncio.gather(*tasks)` behind a `Semaphore(4)`. This should produce 4× parallelism. Instead, effective concurrency is ~1× because of two synchronous blocking calls inside each async task:

**`medgemma_decider.py:171`** — `_structure_decision_with_gemini()`:
```python
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)  # sync HTTP call — blocks event loop
```

**`field_mapper.py:117`** — `generate_field_mappings()`:
```python
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)  # sync HTTP call — blocks event loop
```

**`medgemma_decider.py:321`** — `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()`:
```python
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)  # sync HTTP call — blocks event loop
```

When any of these `.invoke()` calls are executing HTTP I/O, the asyncio event loop is blocked — no other entity task can proceed regardless of how many semaphore slots are available. The `Semaphore(4)` cap is rendered meaningless: with 4 slots allocated but the event loop blocked, throughput is approximately sequential.

**Evidence:** In Run 1 (63 entities, 158 Gemini calls), the 158 `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` spans totaled **1065s cumulative API time**. With genuine parallelism this would complete in ~265s (at 4× concurrency); instead, the ground node took **1147s** — almost perfectly sequential.

---

### RC-3: MedGemma Vertex AI Model Garden Has Extreme Latency Variance

**The dominant per-call cost, with unacceptable tail latency.**

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Number of calls (Run 1) | 63 |
| Total cumulative time | 2245s |
| Average per call | ~25s |
| Median per call | 14s |
| **Maximum** | **308s (5+ minutes for a single call)** |
| Minimum | 1.3s |

A 308-second single call to `ModelGardenChatModel` indicates the Vertex AI endpoint is either cold-starting, queued behind other requests, or experiencing rate limiting. This variance makes the pipeline entirely unpredictable — the same 63-entity protocol can take anywhere from 180s to 1214s depending on Model Garden scheduler state.

---

### RC-4: Two-Model Architecture Multiplies LLM Calls Per Entity

**3–7 LLM calls per entity where 1–2 would suffice.**

The MedGemma-evaluate + Gemini-structure two-stage design means every entity requires, at minimum:

1. **TerminologyRouter** → ToolUniverse network call (fast, ~200ms)
2. **OMOP mapper** → SQL query in parallel (fast)
3. **MedGemma** `ModelGardenChatModel.ainvoke()` → evaluate candidates (~25s avg)
4. **Gemini** `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.invoke()` → structure the decision (~4s avg, **sync**)
5. **Gemini** `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.invoke()` → generate field mappings (~4s avg, **sync**)

With agentic retries (triggered when `confidence < 0.5`), entities get:
6. **MedGemma** `agentic_reasoning_loop` → 3-question reasoning (~25s)
7. **Gemini** `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.invoke()` → structure reasoning (**sync**)
8. **ToolUniverse** re-query with rephrased term
9. **MedGemma** re-evaluate
10. **Gemini** re-structure

In Run 1: 63 MedGemma + 158 Gemini = 221 calls for what appears to be ~63 entities. The ~32 excess Gemini calls (beyond the baseline 2/entity for decision + field mapping) indicate ~30% of entities required at least one agentic retry cycle.

---

### RC-5: Extract Node — 20–89s Per Protocol (Gemini File API)

**Secondary bottleneck, scale-dependent.**

PDF upload to Gemini File API + structured extraction takes 20–89s depending on protocol page count. This is inherent to vision-model PDF processing and acceptable for production but makes E2E tests slow. The `extract_node` span maps 1:1 to this.

---

### RC-6: Parse Node — Sequential Entity Decomposition (3–12s)

Entity decomposition in the parse node runs Gemini calls per criterion sequentially (no asyncio.gather). With 10–30 criteria per protocol, this adds up. This is the third bottleneck but not the primary one.

---

### RC-7: MLflow Tracing Architecture Obscures Per-Entity Data

**Limits visibility into per-entity breakdown within ground node.**

The current `pipeline_span()` in `tracing.py` creates **separate top-level traces** per node rather than child spans under a root trace. As a result:

- The 7 LangGraph traces (from LangChain autolog) capture total node times, but the custom `ground_node` trace (a parallel separate trace) doesn't appear with child spans for individual entities
- From the trace data, we cannot see which specific entities triggered retries, how long ToolUniverse took vs MedGemma, or which entity type is slowest
- The `pipeline_span()` in `ground_node` only records `entity_count` and `grounded_count`, not per-entity timing

---

## Section 3: Effort-Sorted Recommendations

### Immediate (tests)

| Fix | Impact | Effort |
|---|---|---|
| Set `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=3`, `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` in test conftest | Tests go from 3–20min → 30–60s | 5 min |
| Mock MedGemma + Gemini in integration tests; reserve real calls for `@pytest.mark.e2e` only | Same | 1 hour |

### High-Impact Code Changes

| Fix | Impact | Effort |
|---|---|---|
| Replace `.invoke()` with `.ainvoke()` in `_structure_decision_with_gemini()`, `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()`, and `generate_field_mappings()` | Restores actual 4× parallelism; ground node time drops ~4× | 2–3 hours |
| Add `run_in_executor` wrapper around any remaining sync Gemini calls | Same safety guarantee | 1 hour |

### Medium-Impact

| Fix | Impact | Effort |
|---|---|---|
| Cache MedGemma decisions by `(entity_text, entity_type, top_candidates_hash)` | Eliminates duplicate reasoning for same entities across protocols | 4–6 hours |
| Add per-entity timing to ground node's `pipeline_span` outputs (entity_text, attempt_count, llm_ms) | Enables entity-level performance debugging | 2 hours |
| Route field mapping through a cheaper/faster model (Flash vs MedGemma) | 60–80% reduction in field mapping latency | 1 hour |

### Investigate

- **MedGemma cold start**: Determine if there's a keep-warm or provisioned-throughput option on Vertex AI Model Garden; the 308s outlier is unacceptable
- **Agentic retry trigger rate**: Add logging/tracing for what % of entities hit the retry loop; if >30%, the threshold (`confidence < 0.5`) may be too aggressive

---

## Section 4: Summary Timeline (Ideal State After Fixes)

With `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` in tests and async Gemini calls:

| Node | Before | After (5 entities, async) |
|---|---|---|
| Ingest | 0.1s | 0.1s |
| Extract | 20–89s | 20–89s (unchanged) |
| Parse | 3–12s | 3–12s (unchanged) |
| **Ground** | **155–1148s** | **~30–60s (5 entities × 10s avg, 4× parallel)** |
| Persist/Structure | <1s | <1s |
| **Total test runtime** | **3–20 min** | **~1–2 min** |

Here's the plan summary:

---

**Phase 1 — Investigation**
Audit all sync `.invoke()` call sites in async paths (3 confirmed, may be more). Capture baseline test durations to have hard numbers for before/after. Confirm `setdefault` env-var injection is safe from `conftest.py`, verify `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output` has working `ainvoke`, and determine whether the 308s MedGemma outlier is cold-start or rate limiting.

**Phase 2 — Test Fixture Limits** *(zero prod changes)*
Set `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=3` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` in the test `conftest.py` via `setdefault` (so CI/E2E can override to 0). Add `@pytest.mark.slow` for full-pipeline runs. Expected: 80–90% test time reduction.

**Phase 3 — Async Gemini Calls** *(the highest-impact code change)*
Convert the three confirmed sync call sites — `_structure_decision_with_gemini`, `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini`, and the `invoke` inside `generate_field_mappings` — from `.invoke()` to `await .ainvoke()`. This restores genuine 4× parallelism within `Semaphore(4)`. Update the corresponding unit test mocks to use `AsyncMock`.

**Phase 4 — Per-Entity Tracing**
Add `avg_entity_ms`, `max_entity_ms`, and `retry_count` to the ground node's MLflow span output. Tag all 7 pipeline node traces with `protocol_id` so a single run is fully grouped in the MLflow UI. This eliminates the blind spot that made this investigation require log-grepping.

**Phase 5 — MedGemma Keep-Warm** *(conditional on Phase 1.5 findings)*
Only if Phase 1 confirms the 308s outlier is a cold-start issue: add a lightweight pre-flight `ainvoke` call to warm the Vertex AI Model Garden endpoint before the parallel entity batch runs.

  - Agentic retry trigger rate: Add logging/tracing for what % of entities hit the retry loop; if >30%, the threshold (confidence < 0.5) may be too aggressive. Let's do all the investigation in phase 1.

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was added:

**Phase 1.5 — MedGemma Cold-Start Investigation** now has concrete steps: read `model_garden.py` to determine if the endpoint is shared vs. dedicated and what `min_replica_count` is set to, then evaluate four ranked remedies with costs — provisioned throughput, dedicated endpoint with min 1 warm replica, a pre-flight warm-up call, and mocking MedGemma entirely in non-E2E tests. The output feeds whether Phase 5 is worth doing at all.

**Phase 1.6 — Agentic Retry Trigger Rate** is a new section. It uses the MLflow spans DB to compute retry rate per trace (from the MedGemma call count vs entity count ratio), then cross-references the AuditLog table to sample low-confidence entries and classify *why* they fell below 0.5 — genuinely ambiguous, ungroundable entities that slipped past the pre-filter, or the threshold being too tight. The output is a written recommendation on whether to raise the threshold, add entity-type pre-filters, or leave it as-is. This feeds Phase 5's scope.

The Phase 1 completion criteria checklist now requires both investigations to be finished before any implementation phases begin.
