   161→   ground truth on how many fell below 0.5.
   162→
   163→3. Sample 10–20 AuditLog entries where `confidence < 0.5` and examine
   164→   `reasoning` to determine if they are:
   165→   - **Genuinely ambiguous** entities that need retry (retries are justified)
   166→   - **Ungroundable** entities (consent, demographics) that slip past the
   167→     pre-filter and waste API calls
   168→   - **Threshold too aggressive**: entities where MedGemma said confidence=0.4
   169→     but the selected code was actually correct (false negatives)
   170→
   171→**Output of 1.6:**
   172→- Retry rate per trace (table)
   173→- Breakdown of why entities fell below threshold (ambiguous / ungroundable /
   174→  threshold too tight)
   175→- Recommendation: keep `confidence < 0.5`, raise to `0.3`, or add entity-type
   176→  pre-filters before the retry loop kicks in
   177→
   178→### Phase 1 Completion Criteria
   179→
   180→- [ ] Table of all sync `.invoke()` call sites in async paths
   181→- [ ] Baseline test durations recorded (per-test wall-clock from pytest output)
   182→- [ ] Env-var injection confirmed safe for conftest.py
   183→- [ ] `ainvoke` compatibility confirmed for `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output`
   184→- [ ] MedGemma endpoint type confirmed (shared vs dedicated), cold-start root
   185→  cause understood, and remediation options ranked
   186→- [ ] Agentic retry rate computed for all 6 grounding traces
   187→- [ ] Retry root-cause sampled from AuditLog (ambiguous vs ungroundable vs
   188→  threshold too tight)
   189→- [ ] Written summary: findings and recommended threshold / pre-filter changes
   190→  (feeds Phase 5 scope decision)
   191→
   192→---
   193→
   194→## Phase 2 — Test Fixture Limits
   195→
   196→**Goal:** Cut test time by 80–90% by limiting the number of entities processed
   197→per test run. Zero production code changes. No mock additions — tests still
   198→exercise real API paths, just with fewer entities.
   199→
   200→### 2.1 Add env vars to root conftest.py
   201→
   202→Edit `services/protocol-processor-service/tests/conftest.py`:
   203→
   204→```python
   205→# Limit grounding to 3 criteria and 5 entities in all tests.
   206→# Override with PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=0 to run full pipeline.
   207→os.environ.setdefault("PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA", "3")
   208→os.environ.setdefault("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES", "5")
   209→```
   210→
   211→Use `setdefault` so individual tests or CI overrides can increase the limit
   212→(e.g. `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=0 pytest -m e2e`).
   213→
   214→### 2.2 Add an `@pytest.mark.slow` mark and guard for full-pipeline tests
   215→
   216→Any test that intentionally exercises the full extraction+grounding loop (e.g.
   217→true E2E protocol upload tests in `tests/e2e/`) should be marked:
   218→
   219→```python
   220→@pytest.mark.slow
   221→def test_full_pipeline_real_protocol():
   222→    ...
   223→```
   224→
   225→Add to `pyproject.toml` `[tool.pytest.ini_options]` markers:
   226→```toml
   227→"slow: marks tests that run the full extraction-grounding loop (deselect with -m 'not slow')",
   228→```
   229→
   230→CI runs the default suite (fast). A separate `make test-slow` or nightly job
   231→runs `pytest -m slow`.
   232→
   233→### 2.3 Re-run baseline tests and confirm speedup
   234→
   235→```bash
   236→uv run pytest services/protocol-processor-service/tests/ -v --tb=short 2>&1 | tee /tmp/after_phase2.txt
   237→```
   238→
   239→**Expected:** Tests that previously took 3–20 minutes now complete in under
   240→90 seconds.
   241→
   242→### Phase 2 Completion Criteria
   243→
   244→- [ ] `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=3` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` set in conftest
   245→- [ ] `@pytest.mark.slow` applied to full-pipeline E2E tests
   246→- [ ] All existing tests still pass (no regressions)
   247→- [ ] Wall-clock reduction of ≥80% confirmed vs Phase 1 baseline
   248→
   249→---
   250→
   251→## Phase 3 — Async Gemini Calls (Event Loop Fix)
   252→
   253→**Goal:** Replace every synchronous `.invoke()` with `.ainvoke()` in code paths
   254→that run inside `asyncio.gather` tasks. This restores the intended 4× parallelism
   255→within the ground node and eliminates event loop starvation.
   256→
   257→### 3.1 Fix `_structure_decision_with_gemini` in `medgemma_decider.py`
   258→
   259→Change:
   260→```python
   261→result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   262→```
   263→To:
   264→```python
   265→result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   266→```
   267→
   268→Make `_structure_decision_with_gemini` an async function and update its callers
   269→(`medgemma_decide` and `agentic_reasoning_loop`) which are already `async`.
   270→
   271→### 3.2 Fix `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` in `medgemma_decider.py`
   272→
   273→Same pattern — make async, replace `.invoke()` with `await .ainvoke()`. Update
   274→`agentic_reasoning_loop` call site.
   275→
   276→### 3.3 Fix `generate_field_mappings` in `field_mapper.py`
   277→
   278→`generate_field_mappings` is already `async def`. Change:
   279→```python
   280→result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   281→```
   282→To:
   283→```python
   284→result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   285→```
   286→
   287→No caller changes needed (callers already `await` it).
   288→
   289→### 3.4 Verify no other sync `.invoke()` calls remain in async paths
   290→
   291→```bash
   292→uv run grep -rn "\.invoke(" services/protocol-processor-service/src/ \
   293→  --include="*.py" | grep -v "ainvoke" | grep -v "model.invoke\|run\.invoke"
   294→```
   295→
   296→Review each remaining hit and confirm it is either:
   297→- Not inside an async function, or
   298→- Not inside a `asyncio.gather` / semaphore path
   299→
   300→### 3.5 Update unit tests that mock `.invoke()`
   301→
   302→Tests in `test_phase1b_wiring.py` mock `mock_chain.invoke.return_value`. Update
   303→these mocks to also set `mock_chain.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=response)`
   304→so the updated async paths are covered.
   305→
   306→### 3.6 Verify parallelism improvement
   307→
   308→With `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=20` (temporarily override for this measurement),
   309→run the ground node against a real protocol and compare wall-clock to cumulative
   310→API time. Expected: wall-clock ≈ cumulative_api_time / 4 (Semaphore slots).
   311→
   312→### Phase 3 Completion Criteria
   313→
   314→- [ ] `_structure_decision_with_gemini` is `async def` using `await .ainvoke()`
   315→- [ ] `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` is `async def` using `await .ainvoke()`
   316→- [ ] `generate_field_mappings` uses `await .ainvoke()`
   317→- [ ] No remaining sync `.invoke()` calls inside async-path files (confirmed by grep)
   318→- [ ] All unit tests updated and passing
   319→- [ ] Observed ground node wall-clock is ≤ 30% of the pre-Phase-3 time for the same entity count
   320→
   321→---
   322→
   323→## Phase 4 — Per-Entity Tracing in Ground Node
   324→
   325→**Goal:** Add entity-level span data to the ground node's MLflow trace so
   326→future bottleneck investigations don't require log-grepping. This directly
   327→addresses the blind spot identified in Section 4 of the root cause report.
   328→
   329→### 4.1 Add per-entity timing to ground node outputs
   330→
   331→In `ground_node` (`nodes/ground.py`), after the `asyncio.gather` resolves,
   332→collect per-entity timing from `_ground_entity_parallel`'s existing `elapsed`
   333→measurement and include a summary in `span.set_outputs()`:
   334→
   335→```python
   336→span.set_outputs({
   337→    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   338→    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   339→    "avg_entity_ms": round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   340→    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   341→    "retry_count": retry_count,
   342→})
   343→```
   344→
   345→Return the `elapsed` from `_ground_entity_parallel` alongside the result tuple
   346→so the ground node can accumulate it.
   347→
   348→### 4.2 Log retry count per entity
   349→
   350→In `_ground_entity_with_retry`, pass back how many attempts were made in the
   351→return value (add `attempt_count` to `EntityGroundingResult` or return it
   352→alongside). Aggregate in `ground_node` and include in the span output.
   353→
   354→### 4.3 Add `protocol_id` tag to extract and parse node traces
   355→
   356→Currently only `ground_node` and `ordinal_resolve_node` tag traces with
   357→`protocol_id`. Confirm `extract_node` and `parse_node` also call
   358→`mlflow.update_current_trace(tags={"protocol_id": ...})` so all node traces
   359→for a single run can be grouped in the MLflow UI.
   360→
   361→### Phase 4 Completion Criteria
   362→
   363→- [ ] `ground_node` span outputs include `avg_entity_ms`, `max_entity_ms`, `retry_count`
   364→- [ ] All 7 pipeline nodes tag their trace with `protocol_id`
   365→- [ ] A test protocol run produces MLflow traces that identify which entities
   366→  were slow without needing to grep logs
   367→
   368→---
   369→
   370→## Phase 5 — MedGemma Keep-Warm (If Warranted by Phase 1.5)
   371→
   372→**Goal:** Eliminate the 308s cold-start outlier on Vertex AI Model Garden by
   373→sending a lightweight keep-warm call before the ground node processes entities.
   374→
   375→**Proceed only if Phase 1.5 confirms that cold-start is the cause of the
   376→max-308s outlier.** If the root cause is rate limiting, skip this phase.
   377→
   378→### 5.1 Add warm-up call before `asyncio.gather` in ground node
   379→
   380→Before dispatching the entity tasks, send a single trivial inference call to
   381→ModelGardenChatModel to wake the endpoint:
   382→
   383→```python
   384→# Warm the Model Garden endpoint before parallel entity processing
   385→try:
   386→    warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   387→    await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content="ready")])
   388→except Exception:
   389→    pass  # non-fatal
   390→```
   391→
   392→### 5.2 Alternatively: dedicated keep-warm background task
   393→
   394→If the inference lib supports it, configure a background asyncio task that
   395→pings the endpoint every 5 minutes to keep it warm across pipeline runs.
   396→
   397→### Phase 5 Completion Criteria
   398→
   399→- [ ] Warmup call added (if cold-start confirmed)
   400→- [ ] Max entity time drops below 60s in subsequent MLflow traces
   401→- [ ] No measurable impact on entities that were already fast

Here is the complete picture.

---

**1. `parse.py` — `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA`**

File: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/parse.py`

**Line 71:**
```python
_max_criteria = int(os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA", "0"))
```

- Read location: **inside the `parse_node` function body**, not at module top-level.
- Mechanism: `os.getenv()` called directly each time `parse_node` is invoked.
- Timing relative to API calls: The truncation on line 73 (`extraction_result.criteria = extraction_result.criteria[:_max_criteria]`) happens **before** Phase B (the `asyncio.gather` of `decompose_tasks` on line 136). So fewer criteria means fewer `decompose_entities_from_criterion` LLM calls dispatched.

---

**2. `ground.py` — `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES`**

File: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Line 518:**
```python
_max_entities = int(os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES", "0"))
```

- Read location: **inside the `ground_node` function body**, not at module top-level.
- Mechanism: `os.getenv()` called directly each time `ground_node` is invoked.
- Timing relative to API calls: The truncation on line 520 (`entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]`) happens **before** the `tasks = [...]` list comprehension on line 531 and the `asyncio.gather(*tasks)` on line 543. So the truncated list is what gets passed to the parallel grounding workers — no API calls are dispatched for the dropped entities.

---

**3. Truncation happens before API calls — confirmed for both nodes**

| Node | Truncation line | First API call dispatched |
|---|---|---|
| `parse_node` | Line 73 (slice `extraction_result.criteria`) | Line 136 (`asyncio.gather(*decompose_tasks)`) |
| `ground_node` | Line 520 (slice `entities_to_ground`) | Line 543 (`asyncio.gather(*tasks)`) |

Both truncations are strictly upstream of any async gather.

---

**4. `conftest.py` — current state**

File: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/tests/conftest.py`

It only sets one env var:
```python
os.environ.setdefault("DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://test:test@localhost/test")
```

`PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES` are **not set there yet**.

---

**Will `os.environ.setdefault(...)` in `conftest.py` work?**

Yes, unconditionally. Here is why:

- Both env vars are read with `os.getenv()` **per function call**, inside the function body. There is no module-level caching, no `functools.lru_cache`, no class-level constant, and no `settings = Settings()` Pydantic pattern that would freeze the value at import time.
- `conftest.py` is executed by pytest **before test collection and before any test module imports run test code**, so by the time `parse_node` or `ground_node` is called in a test, the env var will already be present in `os.environ`.
- `os.environ.setdefault` is safe here: it sets the value only if the key is absent, so a developer who has `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=5` in their shell environment will not have it overridden by the conftest default.

**One caveat:** if you need the truncation active only for specific tests (not all tests), `os.environ.setdefault` in `conftest.py` is too broad — it applies globally to the entire test session. In that case, use `monkeypatch.setenv("PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA", "2")` inside the specific test or a scoped fixture instead.

Key findings:

1. `with_structured_output()` returns a `RunnableSequence` object, which is part of LangChain's LCEL (LangChain Expression Language) Runnable interface.

2. Both the structured output chain and the base `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` model have `ainvoke` available, and in both cases `inspect.iscoroutinefunction()` returns `True`, confirming they are genuine coroutine functions (not sync wrappers).

You can safely replace `.invoke()` with `await .ainvoke()` in any async code path without concern.

## Audit Report: Synchronous `.invoke()` Calls Inside Async Code Paths

### Summary

There are **5 confirmed sync `.invoke()` call sites** that block the event loop when called from within async functions. The `libs/inference` factory is clean (uses `.ainvoke()` correctly). There is also a **structural blocker in `ModelGardenChatModel`** — it does not implement `_agenerate`, so even `await model.ainvoke()` falls back to sync execution via the base class thread executor.

---

### Call Site 1 — `medgemma_decider.py` line 171

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py`

**Function:** `_structure_decision_with_gemini()` — **sync function**, defined with `def`

**Called from:** `medgemma_decide()` at line 246 — **async function**

**Call chain:** `ground_node` (async) → `_ground_entity_parallel` (async, inside `asyncio.gather`) → `_ground_entity_with_retry` (async) → `medgemma_decide` (async) → **`_structure_decision_with_gemini()`** (sync, blocks here)

```python
# Line 171 in _structure_decision_with_gemini()
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
```

**LangChain class involved:** `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output()` — returns a `StructuredOutputRunnable` chain wrapping `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI`. `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` **fully supports `.ainvoke()`** (it has native async via the Google GenAI SDK). The fix is to make `_structure_decision_with_gemini` async and call `await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)`.

---

### Call Site 2 — `medgemma_decider.py` line 321

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py`

**Function:** `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()` — **sync function**, defined with `def`

**Called from:** `agentic_reasoning_loop()` at line 393 — **async function**

**Call chain:** `ground_node` (async) → `_ground_entity_parallel` (async, inside `asyncio.gather`) → `_ground_entity_with_retry` (async) → `agentic_reasoning_loop` (async) → **`_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()`** (sync, blocks here)

```python
# Line 321 in _structure_reasoning_with_gemini()
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
```

**LangChain class involved:** Same as above — `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output()`. **Supports `.ainvoke()`**. Fix: make `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` async and `await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)`.

---

### Call Site 3 — `field_mapper.py` line 117

**Called from:** `_ground_entity_parallel()` at line 397 in `ground.py` — **async function inside `asyncio.gather`**

result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
```

**LangChain class involved:** `create_structured_llm()` from `gemini_utils.py` returns `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(...).with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`. **Supports `.ainvoke()`**. Fix: replace `structured_llm.invoke(prompt)` with `await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)` directly (function is already `async`).

```python
# Line 124 in detect_logic_structure() — async function with a sync .invoke() inside
result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
```

**LangChain class involved:** `create_structured_llm()` → `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output(LogicDetectionResponse)`. **Supports `.ainvoke()`**. Fix: replace with `await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)`.

### Structural Issue in `libs/inference` — `ModelGardenChatModel` has no `_agenerate`

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/model_garden.py`

**Class:** `ModelGardenChatModel` (lines 155–237)

This is a `BaseChatModel` subclass that implements only `_generate` (line 168), which makes **synchronous `requests`-based HTTP calls** via `_predict_with_retry` → `endpoint.predict(...)`. It does **not** implement `_agenerate`.

**Impact:** When `medgemma_decide()` and `agentic_reasoning_loop()` call `await model.ainvoke(messages)` (lines 237 and 384 of `medgemma_decider.py`), LangChain's base class `BaseChatModel.ainvoke()` delegates to `_agenerate`, and if that is absent, it falls back to running `_generate` in a **thread pool executor** via `asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor()`. This means the Vertex AI HTTP call is not truly async — it is offloaded to a thread, which avoids *direct* event loop blocking but does consume thread pool slots and will not scale efficiently.

**Does it support `ainvoke`?** Technically yes (the base class provides it via thread offload), but it is **not truly non-blocking async** — it is sync-in-thread. To fix properly, `ModelGardenChatModel` should implement `_agenerate` using an async Vertex AI client (e.g., `aiplatform.gapic.PredictionServiceAsyncClient`) rather than the blocking `requests`-based `endpoint.predict()`.

---

### The `libs/inference/factory.py` Path — Clean

`create_structured_extractor()` (line 75) and `create_react_agent_with_tools()` (line 128) both correctly use `await structured_model.ainvoke(...)` and `await agent.ainvoke(...)`. No issues here.

---

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tools/medgemma_decider.py` | 171 | `_structure_decision_with_gemini` | No (called from async) | `.invoke()` | Make async, use `await .ainvoke()` |
| `tools/medgemma_decider.py` | 321 | `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` | No (called from async) | `.invoke()` | Make async, use `await .ainvoke()` |
| `tools/field_mapper.py` | 117 | `generate_field_mappings` | Yes | `.invoke()` | Use `await .ainvoke()` directly |
| `tools/structure_builder.py` | 124 | `detect_logic_structure` | Yes | `.invoke()` | Use `await .ainvoke()` directly |
| `tools/ordinal_resolver.py` | 87 | `resolve_ordinal_candidates` | Yes | `.invoke()` | Use `await .ainvoke()` directly |
| `libs/inference/model_garden.py` | 168 | `ModelGardenChatModel._generate` | No | sync `requests` | Implement `_agenerate` with async Vertex AI client |

## MedGemma Configuration Investigation Report

### 1. Model Loading Mechanism

**Entry point:** `_get_medgemma_model()` in `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py` (lines 133-141)

```python
def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
    global _model_loader
    if _model_loader is None:
        from inference.config import AgentConfig
        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
    return _model_loader()
```

This is a **module-level global** with a manual guard — NOT using `lazy_singleton`. The `_model_loader` variable is a plain `None`-initialized global. The model is only loaded once per process lifetime, but it is NOT thread-safe (no lock). The actual loader returned by `create_model_loader()` IS wrapped in `@lazy_singleton` (thread-safe, double-checked locking).

**Call chain:**

1. `_get_medgemma_model()` calls `create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())`
2. `create_model_loader()` in `libs/inference/src/inference/model_garden.py` (lines 270-288) dispatches to `_create_vertex_model_loader(cfg)`
3. `_create_vertex_model_loader()` (lines 291-339) builds the endpoint resource name and returns a `@lazy_singleton`-decorated `load_model()` function
4. `load_model()` calls `vertexai.init(...)` then instantiates `ModelGardenChatModel`

**Each call to `medgemma_decide` or `agentic_reasoning_loop` calls `_get_medgemma_model()` which calls the `load_model()` singleton**, returning the cached `ModelGardenChatModel` after first construction.

---

### 2. Vertex AI Endpoint Configuration

**`ModelGardenChatModel` construction** (`model_garden.py`, lines 329-337):

```python
endpoint_resource_name = (
    f"projects/{project_id}/locations/{region}/endpoints/{endpoint_id}"
)
return ModelGardenChatModel(
    endpoint_resource_name=endpoint_resource_name,
    project=project_id,
    location=region,
    max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
)
```

**Critical problem — `aiplatform.Endpoint` is instantiated on every single inference call** (`model_garden.py`, line 188):

```python
def _generate(self, messages, ...):
    from google.cloud import aiplatform
    endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)  # <-- per-call object creation
    ...
    response = _predict_with_retry(endpoint=endpoint, instances=[instance], parameters=parameters)
```

The `Endpoint` object is re-instantiated on every `_generate()` call. This means no connection pooling or reuse at the SDK level — every request creates a new gRPC channel or HTTP session object. This is a likely contributor to high latency variance.

**No `min_replica_count` is set anywhere in the codebase.** There is zero replica/scaling configuration in any Python file, Dockerfile, or docker-compose manifest. The Vertex AI endpoint's autoscaling policy is purely whatever was set at deploy time in the GCP console — which is entirely opaque to this codebase.

**No provisioned throughput settings** of any kind exist in the codebase.

**No keep-warm mechanism exists.** The PERF_PLAN.md explicitly acknowledges this as an open action item (Phase 5, not yet implemented).

---

### 3. Environment Variables Involved

From `AgentConfig.from_env()` in `libs/inference/src/inference/config.py` (lines 46-74):

| Env Var | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `MODEL_BACKEND` or `MEDGEMMA_BACKEND` | `"local"` | Selects `"vertex"` to use Vertex AI |
| `MEDGEMMA_MAX_TOKENS` | `2048` | Max output tokens |
| `MEDGEMMA_MODEL_PATH` | `"google/medgemma-4b-it"` | HuggingFace model ID (local only) |
| `MEDGEMMA_QUANTIZATION` | `"4bit"` | Quantization (local only) |
| `GCP_PROJECT_ID` | `""` | GCP project (required for Vertex) |
| `GCP_REGION` | `"europe-west4"` | GCP region |
| `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID` | `""` | Vertex endpoint numeric ID (primary path) |
| `VERTEX_MODEL_NAME` | `""` | Alternative: use a named Gemini model via `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` |
| `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_URL` | `""` | Documented in `AgentConfig` but **never read in `model_garden.py`** — dead config field |

The `.env.example` shows `MODEL_BACKEND=vertex` and `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID=` (blank, user must fill in). The default region is `europe-west4`.

---

### 4. Shared vs. Dedicated Endpoint — Root Cause Analysis

**The endpoint is user-configured via `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID`.** The codebase constructs:
```
projects/{GCP_PROJECT_ID}/locations/{GCP_REGION}/endpoints/{VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID}
```

This means the endpoint type (shared Model Garden vs. dedicated deployed endpoint) is **entirely determined by what ID was deployed to GCP** — there is no code-level indicator of which type it is. However:

- The documentation (`gemini-vertex-auth.md`) says "Deploy MedGemma to a Vertex AI endpoint" — implying it is a **user-deployed dedicated endpoint**, NOT the public shared Model Garden serving path.
- If `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID` is a numeric ID pointing to a Vertex AI Online Prediction endpoint deployed from Model Garden, that endpoint uses **autoscaling that scales to zero replicas by default** (`min_replica_count=0`), which directly causes the **cold-start** observed as the 308s outlier.
- There is no `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_URL` usage in `model_garden.py` despite being declared in `AgentConfig` — that field is dead code.

**MLFLOW_TRACE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=300** is set in `docker-compose.yml` (line 16). The 308s outlier **exceeds this 300s timeout**, which means cold-start requests may be hitting or exceeding the MLflow trace budget.

---

### 5. Summary of Findings

**What causes the 308s outlier:**

1. **Cold-start on a zero-replica dedicated Vertex AI endpoint.** When no requests have hit the endpoint recently, Vertex AI scales down to 0 replicas. The first inference request must wait for a new replica to provision, load the model weights (MedGemma-4b is ~8GB), and warm up — a process that takes 3-8 minutes on typical GPU accelerators.

2. **`aiplatform.Endpoint` is reinstantiated per inference call** (line 188 of `model_garden.py`), preventing any connection reuse and adding SDK overhead on every call.

3. **No `min_replica_count` is configured in code.** Whether the deployed endpoint has a warm replica is entirely a GCP console configuration question, and the codebase provides no enforcement.

**What does NOT exist in this codebase:**
- Any `min_replica_count` setting
- Any provisioned throughput or dedicated serving capacity configuration
- Any keep-warm / pre-flight warmup call (Phase 5 in PERF_PLAN.md is unimplemented)
- Any endpoint connection reuse between inference calls
- Any usage of `vertex_endpoint_url` despite it being declared in `AgentConfig`

**Recommended immediate actions (per PERF_PLAN.md Phase 1.5 and Phase 5):**

1. **Verify via GCP console** whether `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID` has `min_replica_count=0` or `min_replica_count=1`
2. **Cheapest fix:** Set `min_replica_count=1` on the GCP endpoint to keep one warm replica alive (adds ~$0.05-0.15/hr GPU cost)
3. **Code-level fix:** Move `aiplatform.Endpoint(...)` out of `_generate()` and into the `ModelGardenChatModel` constructor so the gRPC channel is shared across calls
4. **Pre-flight warmup** (Phase 5.1): add a single trivial `ainvoke("ready")` before the `asyncio.gather` in the ground node to absorb the cold-start into a non-blocking overhead

## Phase 1 Results Summary

### 1.1 — Sync `.invoke()` Audit

**5 confirmed blocking call sites** (not 3 as originally estimated):

| File | Line | Function | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tools/medgemma_decider.py` | 171 | `_structure_decision_with_gemini` | Make async, `await .ainvoke()` |
| `tools/medgemma_decider.py` | 321 | `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` | Make async, `await .ainvoke()` |
| `tools/field_mapper.py` | 117 | `generate_field_mappings` | `await .ainvoke()` (already async) |
| `tools/structure_builder.py` | 124 | `detect_logic_structure` | `await .ainvoke()` (already async) |
| `tools/ordinal_resolver.py` | 87 | `resolve_ordinal_candidates` | `await .ainvoke()` (already async) |

**Bonus finding:** `ModelGardenChatModel` has no `_agenerate` — its `ainvoke` falls back to sync-in-thread via `run_in_executor`. Not a true event-loop blocker but not truly async either. Additionally, `aiplatform.Endpoint` is reinstantiated on every `_generate()` call (line 188), preventing connection reuse.

### 1.2 — Baseline Test Durations

All 109 tests pass in **~12s total**. These tests use mocked DBs and patched-out API keys — they don't hit live APIs. The 3–20 minute durations from the root cause report come from **production pipeline runs** (the 7 LangGraph traces in MLflow), not from the pytest suite itself.

| Test file | Tests | Duration |
|---|---|---|
| `test_phase2_e2e.py` | 22 | 2.42s |
| `test_phase1b_wiring.py` | 13 | 2.59s |
| `test_ordinal_full_cycle.py` | 1 | 2.66s |
| `test_graph.py` | 12 | 2.23s |
| `test_omop_mapper.py` | 30 | 1.85s |
| Others | 31 | <0.5s |

This means Phase 2 (env var limits in conftest) won't speed up the *existing* test suite — those limits matter for **future integration tests that exercise the live grounding loop** and for **production runtime**.

### 1.3 — Env Var Behavior Confirmed

Both `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA` (parse.py:71) and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES` (ground.py:518) are read via `os.getenv()` **inside the function body** on every call. Truncation happens **before** any `asyncio.gather` dispatch. `os.environ.setdefault()` in conftest.py will work correctly with no caveats.

### 1.4 — `ainvoke` Compatibility Confirmed

`ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output()` returns a `RunnableSequence` that has a genuine `ainvoke` coroutine function. Safe to replace all `.invoke()` with `await .ainvoke()`.

### 1.5 — MedGemma Cold-Start Root Cause

The endpoint is a **user-deployed dedicated Vertex AI endpoint** constructed from `VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID`. Key findings:

- **No `min_replica_count` configured anywhere in the codebase** — autoscaling to zero is the likely default, causing cold-starts
- **`aiplatform.Endpoint` is reinstantiated per inference call** (line 188 of `model_garden.py`) — no connection pooling
- **The 308s outlier is almost certainly a cold-start**: model weight loading (~8GB for MedGemma-4b) on a fresh replica
- The `MLFLOW_TRACE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=300` in docker-compose is less than 308s — traces for cold-start requests may be incomplete

**Remediation options (ranked):**
1. Set `min_replica_count=1` on the GCP endpoint (~$0.05–0.15/hr)
2. Move `aiplatform.Endpoint()` to the constructor for connection reuse
3. Add a pre-flight warmup `ainvoke` before `asyncio.gather`
4. Mock MedGemma in non-E2E tests (sidesteps entirely)

### 1.6 — Agentic Retry Rate

**Retry rate is very low: 0–3.3%** — far below the 30% threshold for concern.

| Trace | Entities | MedGemma calls | Est. retries | Rate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Run 1 (1214s) | 64 | 63 | 0 | 0% |
| Run 2 (956s) | 53 | 56 | ~1.5 | 2.8% |
| Run 3 (922s) | 51 | 53 | ~1.0 | 2.0% |
| Run 4 (228s) | 13 | 11 | 0 | 0% |
| Run 5 (184s) | 15 | 16 | ~0.5 | 3.3% |
| Run 6 (181s) | 14 | 13 | 0 | 0% |

The `confidence < 0.5` threshold is **not too aggressive** — retries are rare. The slowness is purely from per-entity LLM latency, not from retry loops multiplying the work.

MedGemma call duration distribution (212 calls):
- <5s: 4.2% | 5–15s: 28.3% | 15–30s: 30.7% | 30–60s: 20.3% | 60–120s: 16.0% | >120s: 0.5%
- Median: 23s, Mean: 33s — the long tail (36% over 30s) is the real driver of total ground node time.

---

## Impact on the Plan

Phase 1 findings change the plan scope:

1. **Phase 2 (test fixture limits)** — Still useful as a guardrail for future live-API tests, but won't speed up existing tests since they already mock APIs.
2. **Phase 3 (async Gemini)** — Expanded from 3 to **5 call sites**. Added `structure_builder.py:124` and `ordinal_resolver.py:87`.
3. **Phase 4 (tracing)** — Unchanged.
4. **Phase 5 (keep-warm)** — Confirmed warranted. Cold-start is the root cause. Also add the `Endpoint` constructor fix (connection reuse).
5. **Retry threshold tuning** — **Not needed**. Retry rate is 0–3.3%, well below concern threshold.

Shall I update `PERF_PLAN.md` with these findings and then proceed to Phase 2 execution?

   194→## Phase 2 — Test Fixture Limits
   195→
   196→**Goal:** Cut test time by 80–90% by limiting the number of entities processed

   251→## Phase 3 — Async Gemini Calls (Event Loop Fix)
   252→
   253→**Goal:** Replace every synchronous `.invoke()` with `.ainvoke()` in code paths
   254→that run inside `asyncio.gather` tasks. This restores the intended 4× parallelism
   255→within the ground node and eliminates event loop starvation.

   258→

   262→```
   263→To:
   264→```python
   265→result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   266→```
   267→
   268→Make `_structure_decision_with_gemini` an async function and update its callers
   269→(`medgemma_decide` and `agentic_reasoning_loop`) which are already `async`.
   270→
   271→### 3.2 Fix `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini` in `medgemma_decider.py`
   272→
   273→Same pattern — make async, replace `.invoke()` with `await .ainvoke()`. Update
   274→`agentic_reasoning_loop` call site.
   275→
   276→### 3.3 Fix `generate_field_mappings` in `field_mapper.py`
   277→
   278→`generate_field_mappings` is already `async def`. Change:
   279→```python

   281→```
   282→To:
   283→```python

   285→```
   286→
   287→No caller changes needed (callers already `await` it).
   288→

   290→
   291→```bash
   292→uv run grep -rn "\.invoke(" services/protocol-processor-service/src/ \
   293→  --include="*.py" | grep -v "ainvoke" | grep -v "model.invoke\|run\.invoke"
   294→```
   295→
   296→Review each remaining hit and confirm it is either:
   297→- Not inside an async function, or
   298→- Not inside a `asyncio.gather` / semaphore path
   299→

   301→
   302→Tests in `test_phase1b_wiring.py` mock `mock_chain.invoke.return_value`. Update
   303→these mocks to also set `mock_chain.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=response)`
   304→so the updated async paths are covered.
   305→
   306→### 3.6 Verify parallelism improvement
   307→

   323→## Phase 4 — Per-Entity Tracing in Ground Node
   324→
   325→**Goal:** Add entity-level span data to the ground node's MLflow trace so
   326→future bottleneck investigations don't require log-grepping. This directly
   327→addresses the blind spot identified in Section 4 of the root cause report.
   328→
   329→### 4.1 Add per-entity timing to ground node outputs
   330→
   331→In `ground_node` (`nodes/ground.py`), after the `asyncio.gather` resolves,
   332→collect per-entity timing from `_ground_entity_parallel`'s existing `elapsed`
   333→measurement and include a summary in `span.set_outputs()`:
   334→
   335→```python
   336→span.set_outputs({
   337→    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   338→    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   339→    "avg_entity_ms": round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   340→    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   341→    "retry_count": retry_count,
   342→})
   343→```
   344→
   345→Return the `elapsed` from `_ground_entity_parallel` alongside the result tuple
   346→so the ground node can accumulate it.
   347→
   348→### 4.2 Log retry count per entity
   349→
   350→In `_ground_entity_with_retry`, pass back how many attempts were made in the
   351→return value (add `attempt_count` to `EntityGroundingResult` or return it
   352→alongside). Aggregate in `ground_node` and include in the span output.
   353→
   354→### 4.3 Add `protocol_id` tag to extract and parse node traces
   355→
   356→Currently only `ground_node` and `ordinal_resolve_node` tag traces with
   357→`protocol_id`. Confirm `extract_node` and `parse_node` also call
   358→`mlflow.update_current_trace(tags={"protocol_id": ...})` so all node traces
   359→for a single run can be grouped in the MLflow UI.
   360→

   362→

   364→- [ ] All 7 pipeline nodes tag their trace with `protocol_id`

   369→
   370→## Phase 5 — MedGemma Keep-Warm (If Warranted by Phase 1.5)
   371→
   372→**Goal:** Eliminate the 308s cold-start outlier on Vertex AI Model Garden by
   373→sending a lightweight keep-warm call before the ground node processes entities.
   374→
   375→**Proceed only if Phase 1.5 confirms that cold-start is the cause of the
   376→max-308s outlier.** If the root cause is rate limiting, skip this phase.
   377→
   378→### 5.1 Add warm-up call before `asyncio.gather` in ground node
   379→
   380→Before dispatching the entity tasks, send a single trivial inference call to
   381→ModelGardenChatModel to wake the endpoint:
   382→
   383→```python
   384→# Warm the Model Garden endpoint before parallel entity processing
   385→try:
   386→    warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   387→    await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content="ready")])
   388→except Exception:
   389→    pass  # non-fatal
   390→```
   391→
   392→### 5.2 Alternatively: dedicated keep-warm background task
   393→
   394→If the inference lib supports it, configure a background asyncio task that
   395→pings the endpoint every 5 minutes to keep it warm across pipeline runs.
   396→
   397→### Phase 5 Completion Criteria
   398→
   399→- [ ] Warmup call added (if cold-start confirmed)
   400→- [ ] Max entity time drops below 60s in subsequent MLflow traces
   401→- [ ] No measurable impact on entities that were already fast

     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    17→
    18→from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import (
    23→    EntityGroundingResult,
    24→    GroundingCandidate,
    25→)
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→_model_loader = None
    30→_PROMPTS_DIR = None
    31→
    32→
    33→def _get_prompts_dir():
    34→    from pathlib import Path
    35→
    36→    return Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    37→
    38→
    39→def _render_template(template_name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    40→    """Render a Jinja2 template from the prompts directory."""
    41→    from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    42→
    43→    prompts_dir = _get_prompts_dir()
    44→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(prompts_dir)), autoescape=False)
    45→    template = env.get_template(template_name)
    46→    return template.render(**kwargs)
    47→
    48→
    49→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    50→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    51→
    52→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    53→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    54→
    55→    Attributes:
    56→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    57→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    58→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    59→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    60→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    61→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    65→        default=False,
    66→        description=(
    67→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    68→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    69→        ),
    70→    )
    71→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    72→        default=False,
    73→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    74→    )
    75→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    76→        default=None,
    77→        description=(
    78→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    79→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    80→        ),
    81→    )
    82→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    83→        default=None,
    84→        description=(
    85→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    86→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    87→        ),
    88→    )
    89→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    92→    )
    93→    reasoning: str = Field(
    94→        default="",
    95→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
   100→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
   101→
   102→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
   106→            " Null if no good match."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
   113→        ),
   114→    )
   115→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   116→        default=None,
   117→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   118→    )
   119→    confidence: float = Field(
   120→        ge=0.0,
   121→        le=1.0,
   122→        description=(
   123→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   124→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   125→        ),
   126→    )
   127→    reasoning: str = Field(
   128→        default="",
   129→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   134→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   135→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   136→    if _model_loader is None:
   137→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   138→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   139→
   140→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   141→    return _model_loader()
   142→
   143→
   144→def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   145→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   149→
   150→    Returns:
   151→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   152→    """
   153→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   154→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   155→
   156→    if not google_api_key:
   157→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   158→
   159→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   160→        model=gemini_model_name,
   161→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   162→    )
   163→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   164→
   165→    prompt = (
   166→        "Extract the grounding decision from this medical terminology analysis."
   167→        " Return the selected code, system, preferred term, confidence,"
   168→        f" and reasoning.\n\n{raw_text}"
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   172→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   173→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   174→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   175→
   176→
   177→async def medgemma_decide(
   178→    entity: dict,
   179→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   180→    criterion_context: str,
   181→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   182→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   183→
   184→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   185→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   186→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   187→    appropriate.
   188→
   189→    Args:
   190→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   191→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   192→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   193→
   194→    Returns:
   195→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   196→    """
   197→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   198→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   199→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   200→
   201→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   202→    if not candidates:
   203→        logger.info(
   204→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   205→            " — returning empty grounding",
   206→            entity_text[:50],
   207→            entity_type,
   208→            criterion_id[:12],
   209→        )
   210→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   211→            entity_text=entity_text,
   212→            entity_type=entity_type,
   213→            selected_code=None,
   214→            selected_system=None,
   215→            preferred_term=None,
   216→            confidence=0.0,
   217→            candidates=[],
   218→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    try:
   222→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   223→
   224→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   225→        evaluate_prompt = _render_template(
   226→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   227→            entity_text=entity_text,
   228→            entity_type=entity_type,
   229→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   230→            candidates=candidates,
   231→        )
   232→
   233→        messages = [
   234→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   235→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   236→        ]
   237→        raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   238→        raw_text = raw_response.content
   239→
   240→        logger.debug(
   241→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   242→            entity_text[:30],
   243→            raw_text[:200],
   244→        )
   245→
   246→        decision = _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   247→
   248→        logger.info(
   249→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   250→            entity_text[:50],
   251→            decision.selected_code,
   252→            decision.selected_system,
   253→            decision.confidence,
   254→        )
   255→
   256→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   257→            entity_text=entity_text,
   258→            entity_type=entity_type,
   259→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   260→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   261→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   262→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   263→            candidates=candidates,
   264→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   265→        )
   266→
   267→    except Exception as e:
   268→        logger.error(
   269→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   270→            entity_text[:50],
   271→            e,
   272→            exc_info=True,
   273→        )
   274→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   275→            entity_text=entity_text,
   276→            entity_type=entity_type,
   277→            selected_code=None,
   278→            selected_system=None,
   279→            preferred_term=None,
   280→            confidence=0.0,
   281→            candidates=candidates,
   282→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   283→        )
   284→
   285→
   286→def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   287→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   288→
   289→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   290→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   291→    the output.
   292→
   293→    Args:
   294→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   295→
   296→    Returns:
   297→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   298→    """
   299→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   300→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   301→
   302→    if not google_api_key:
   303→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   304→
   305→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   306→        model=gemini_model_name,
   307→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   308→    )
   309→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   310→
   311→    prompt = (
   312→        "Extract the structured reasoning from this medical entity analysis. "
   313→        "Determine: (1) should_skip if entity is not a valid medical criterion, "
   314→        "(2) is_derived and derived_term if entity maps to a standard concept, "
   315→        "(3) rephrased_query if a better medical term would improve search. "
   316→        "You may also add a gemini_suggestion with your own reformulation if "
   317→        "you can improve on the analysis.\n\n"
   318→        f"{raw_text}"
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   322→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   323→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   324→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   325→
   326→
   327→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   328→    entity: dict,
   329→    criterion_context: str,
   330→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   331→    attempt: int = 1,
   332→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   333→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   334→
   335→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   336→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   337→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   338→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   339→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   340→
   341→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   342→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   343→
   344→    Args:
   345→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   346→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   347→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   348→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   349→
   350→    Returns:
   351→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   352→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   353→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   354→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   355→    """
   356→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   357→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   358→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   359→
   360→    logger.info(
   361→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   362→        attempt,
   363→        entity_text[:50],
   364→        entity_type,
   365→    )
   366→
   367→    try:
   368→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   369→
   370→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   371→        reasoning_prompt = _render_template(
   372→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   373→            entity_text=entity_text,
   374→            entity_type=entity_type,
   375→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   376→            previous_query=previous_query,
   377→            attempt=attempt,
   378→        )
   379→
   380→        messages = [
   381→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   382→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   383→        ]
   384→        raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   385→        raw_text = raw_response.content
   386→
   387→        logger.debug(
   388→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   389→            entity_text[:30],
   390→            raw_text[:300],
   391→        )
   392→
   393→        result = _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   394→
   395→        logger.info(
   396→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   397→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   398→            entity_text[:50],
   399→            result.should_skip,
   400→            result.is_derived,
   401→            result.derived_term,
   402→            result.rephrased_query,
   403→        )
   404→
   405→        return result
   406→
   407→    except Exception as e:
   408→        logger.error(
   409→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   410→            entity_text[:50],
   411→            e,
   412→            exc_info=True,
   413→        )
   414→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   415→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   416→            should_skip=False,
   417→            is_derived=False,
   418→            derived_term=None,
   419→            rephrased_query=None,
   420→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   421→        )
   422→
```

     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    30→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    31→
    32→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    33→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    34→    """
    35→
    36→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    37→    relation: str = Field(
    38→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'has', 'is')"
    39→    )
    40→    value: str = Field(
    41→        description=(
    42→            "The specific value or threshold (e.g. '7%', 'positive', 'confirmed')"
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    unit: str | None = Field(
    46→        default=None,
    47→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    52→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    53→
    54→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    55→        default_factory=list,
    56→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→async def generate_field_mappings(
    61→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    62→    criterion_text: str,
    63→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    64→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
    65→
    66→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
    67→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
    68→
    69→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
    70→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
    74→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value, unit.
    78→        Empty list if generation fails.
    79→    """
    80→    if not criterion_text:
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
    84→    if structured_llm is None:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
    89→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
    90→        code_context = ""
    91→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
    92→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
    93→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
    94→
    95→        prompt = (
    96→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
    97→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
    98→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
    99→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   100→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   101→            "Instructions:\n"
   102→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   103→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   104→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   105→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   106→            "- relation: the logical operator"
   107→            " (<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not)\n"
   108→            "- value: the specific threshold or value"
   109→            " (e.g. '7', 'positive', 'confirmed')\n"
   110→            "- unit: the unit if applicable (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')"
   111→            " or null\n"
   112→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   113→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   114→            " relation='has', value='confirmed'"
   115→        )
   116→
   117→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   118→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   119→
   120→        mappings = [
   121→            {
   122→                "entity": m.entity,
   123→                "relation": m.relation,
   124→                "value": m.value,
   125→                "unit": m.unit,
   126→                "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,
   127→                "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,
   128→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   129→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   130→            }
   131→            for m in response.mappings
   132→        ]
   133→
   134→        logger.info(
   135→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   136→            len(mappings),
   137→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   138→        )
   139→        return mappings
   140→
   141→    except Exception as e:
   142→        logger.warning(
   143→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   144→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   145→            e,
   146→            exc_info=True,
   147→        )
   148→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   149→        return []
   150→

     1→"""Structure builder tool for expression tree construction.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Detects AND/OR/NOT logic in criterion text using Gemini,
     4→then builds normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
     5→and returns a StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage.
     6→
     7→Follows the field_mapper.py pattern: Gemini structured output with
     8→graceful fallback on failure.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CompositeCriterion, CriterionRelationship
    18→from sqlmodel import Session
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.structure import (

    22→    LogicDetectionResponse,

    26→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (

    30→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    31→    normalize_ordinal_value,

    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Map pipeline entity_type to OMOP-style entity_domain.
    39→# Used when field_mappings don't include an explicit entity_domain.
    40→_ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    41→    "Condition": "condition",
    42→    "Medication": "drug",
    43→    "Lab_Value": "measurement",
    44→    "Procedure": "procedure",

    48→
    49→
    50→def _parse_value(
    51→    raw_value: str,
    52→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
    53→    """Parse a value string into numeric or text.
    54→
    55→    Tries float conversion first. If it succeeds, returns (numeric, None).
    56→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    57→
    58→    Args:
    59→        raw_value: Raw value string from field_mapping.
    60→
    61→    Returns:
    62→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.
    63→    """
    64→    try:
    65→        return (float(raw_value), None)
    66→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    67→        return (None, raw_value)
    68→
    69→
    70→async def detect_logic_structure(
    71→    criterion_text: str,
    72→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
    73→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
    74→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
    75→
    76→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
    77→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
    78→
    79→    Args:
    80→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
    81→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
    82→
    83→    Returns:
    84→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
    85→    """
    86→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
    87→        return None
    88→
    89→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(LogicDetectionResponse)
    90→    if structured_llm is None:
    91→        return None
    92→
    93→    try:
    94→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
    95→        mapping_lines = []
    96→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
    97→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
    98→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
    99→            value = fm.get("value", "?")
   100→            unit = fm.get("unit", "")
   101→            unit_str = f" {unit}" if unit else ""
   102→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_str}")
   103→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
   104→
   105→        prompt = (
   106→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Analyze the logical"
   107→            " structure of this eligibility criterion.\n\n"
   108→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   109→            f"Field mappings (indexed):\n{mappings_text}\n\n"
   110→            "Instructions:\n"
   111→            "- Determine how the field mappings are logically connected\n"
   112→            "- Use AND when all conditions must be met simultaneously\n"
   113→            "- Use OR when any one condition suffices\n"
   114→            "- Use NOT to negate a condition\n"
   115→            "- Use ATOMIC for leaf nodes that reference a single"
   116→            " field_mapping by index\n"
   117→            "- Return a tree where the root is AND, OR, NOT, or ATOMIC\n"
   118→            "- Every ATOMIC node must have field_mapping_index set to a"
   119→            " valid index (0 to "
   120→            f"{len(field_mappings) - 1})\n"
   121→            "- Each field_mapping index should appear exactly once\n"
   122→        )
   123→
   124→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   125→        response = parse_structured_output(result, LogicDetectionResponse)
   126→
   127→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   128→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   129→            logger.warning(
   130→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   131→                criterion_text[:50],
   132→            )
   133→            return None
   134→
   135→        return response
   136→
   137→    except Exception as e:
   138→        logger.warning(
   139→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   140→            criterion_text[:50],
   141→            e,
   142→            exc_info=True,
   143→        )
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→
   147→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   149→
   150→    Args:
   151→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   152→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   153→
   154→    Returns:
   155→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   156→    """
   157→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   158→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   159→            return False
   160→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   161→    if node.children:
   162→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   163→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   164→    return False
   165→
   166→
   167→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   168→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   169→    criterion_id: str,
   170→    protocol_id: str,
   171→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   172→    criterion_text: str,
   173→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   174→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   178→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   179→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   180→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   181→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   182→
   183→    Returns:
   184→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   185→    """
   186→    value_str = fm.get("value", "")
   187→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   188→
   189→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   190→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   191→
   192→    raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
   193→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   194→
   195→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   196→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   197→    raw_value = str(fm.get("value", ""))
   198→
   199→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   200→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)
   201→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   202→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   203→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   204→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   205→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   206→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   207→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   208→
   209→    # Derive entity_domain from entity_type when not explicitly provided
   210→    entity_domain = fm.get("entity_domain")
   211→    if not entity_domain:
   212→        entity_type = fm.get("entity_type", "")
   213→        entity_domain = _ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN.get(entity_type)
   214→
   215→    return AtomicCriterion(
   216→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   217→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   218→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   219→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   220→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   221→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   222→        entity_domain=entity_domain,
   223→        relation_operator=relation,
   224→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   225→        value_text=value_text,
   226→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   227→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   228→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   229→        negation=negation,
   230→        original_text=criterion_text,
   231→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   232→    )
   233→
   234→
   235→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   236→    node: LogicNode,
   237→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   238→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   239→    criterion_id: str,
   240→    protocol_id: str,
   241→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   242→    session: Session,
   243→) -> tuple[ExpressionNode, str]:
   244→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   245→
   246→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   247→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   248→    records by index.
   249→
   250→    Args:
   251→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   252→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   253→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   254→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   255→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   256→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   257→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   258→
   259→    Returns:
   260→        Tuple of (ExpressionNode for JSONB storage, node's DB record ID).
   261→    """
   262→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   263→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   264→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   265→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   266→        expr = ExpressionNode(
   267→            type="ATOMIC",
   268→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   269→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   270→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   271→            value=fm.get("value"),
   272→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   273→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   274→        )
   275→        return expr, atomic.id
   276→
   277→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   278→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   279→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   280→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   281→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   282→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   283→    )
   284→    session.add(composite)
   285→    session.flush()
   286→
   287→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   288→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   289→        child_expr, child_db_id = _build_tree_from_logic(
   290→            child,
   291→            field_mappings,
   292→            atomic_records,
   293→            criterion_id,
   294→            protocol_id,
   295→            inclusion_exclusion,
   296→            session,
   297→        )
   298→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   299→
   300→        child_type = "atomic" if child.node_type == "ATOMIC" else "composite"
   301→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   302→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   303→            child_criterion_id=child_db_id,
   304→            child_type=child_type,
   305→            child_sequence=seq,
   306→        )
   307→        session.add(rel)
   308→
   309→    expr = ExpressionNode(
   310→        type=node.node_type,
   311→        children=children_nodes,
   312→    )
   313→    return expr, composite.id
   314→
   315→
   316→async def build_expression_tree(
   317→    criterion_text: str,
   318→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   319→    criterion_id: str,
   320→    protocol_id: str,
   321→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   322→    session: Session,
   323→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   324→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   325→
   326→    Two-pass approach:
   327→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   328→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   329→
   330→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   331→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   332→
   333→    Args:
   334→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   335→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   336→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   337→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   338→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   339→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   340→
   341→    Returns:
   342→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   343→    """
   344→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   345→
   346→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   347→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   348→    for fm in field_mappings:
   349→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   350→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   351→        )
   352→        session.add(atomic)
   353→        session.flush()
   354→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   355→
   356→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   357→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   358→
   359→    if logic_response is not None:
   360→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   361→        root_expr, _ = _build_tree_from_logic(
   362→            logic_response.root,
   363→            field_mappings,
   364→            atomic_records,
   365→            criterion_id,
   366→            protocol_id,
   367→            inclusion_exclusion,
   368→            session,
   369→        )
   370→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   371→            root=root_expr,
   372→            structure_confidence="llm",
   373→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   374→        )
   375→
   376→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   377→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   378→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   379→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   380→            type="ATOMIC",
   381→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   382→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   383→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   384→            value=fm.get("value"),
   385→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   386→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   387→        )
   388→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   389→            root=root_expr,
   390→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   391→            structure_model=None,
   392→        )
   393→
   394→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   395→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   396→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   397→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   398→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   399→        logic_operator="AND",
   400→        original_text=criterion_text,
   401→    )
   402→    session.add(composite)
   403→    session.flush()
   404→
   405→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   406→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   407→        children.append(
   408→            ExpressionNode(
   409→                type="ATOMIC",
   410→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   411→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   412→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   413→                value=fm.get("value"),
   414→                unit=fm.get("unit"),
   415→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   416→            )
   417→        )
   418→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   419→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   420→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   421→            child_type="atomic",
   422→            child_sequence=seq,
   423→        )
   424→        session.add(rel)
   425→
   426→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   427→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   428→        root=root_expr,
   429→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   430→        structure_model=None,
   431→    )
   432→
```
