     1→"""Ordinal scale resolution tool using Gemini structured output.
     2→
     3→Identifies unrecognized clinical ordinal scoring systems (Child-Pugh, GCS,
     4→APACHE II, MELD, mRS, SOFA, etc.) and proposes YAML config entries for
     5→human review.
     6→
     7→Follows the detect_logic_structure() pattern from structure_builder.py:
     8→- Guard on GOOGLE_API_KEY
     9→- Late import of ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    10→- with_structured_output() for typed LLM response
    11→- Return None on any failure (graceful degradation)
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→import logging
    17→from typing import Any
    18→
    19→from protocol_processor.schemas.ordinal import (
    20→    OrdinalResolutionResponse,
    21→    OrdinalScaleProposal,
    22→)
    23→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    24→    create_structured_llm,
    25→    parse_structured_output,
    26→)
    27→
    28→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    29→
    30→
    31→async def resolve_ordinal_candidates(
    32→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]],
    33→) -> OrdinalResolutionResponse | None:
    34→    """Send candidate entities to Gemini for ordinal scale identification.
    35→
    36→    Args:
    37→        candidates: List of dicts with keys 'entity_text', 'value_numeric',
    38→            'relation_operator' from AtomicCriterion records.
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        OrdinalResolutionResponse with proposals, or None on failure/skip.
    42→    """
    43→    if not candidates:
    44→        return None
    45→
    46→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(OrdinalResolutionResponse)
    47→    if structured_llm is None:
    48→        return None
    49→
    50→    try:
    51→        # Build indexed entity list for the prompt
    52→        entity_lines = []
    53→        for i, c in enumerate(candidates):
    54→            entity = c.get("entity_text", "?")
    55→            value = c.get("value_numeric", "?")
    56→            relation = c.get("relation_operator", "?")
    57→            entity_lines.append(
    58→                f'  [{i}] entity="{entity}" relation="{relation}" value={value}'
    59→            )
    60→        entities_text = "\n".join(entity_lines)
    61→
    62→        prompt = (
    63→            "You are a clinical terminology expert specializing in medical scoring "
    64→            "systems and ordinal scales.\n\n"
    65→            "For each entity below, determine if it is a clinical ordinal scoring "
    66→            "system (e.g. ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE II, MELD, "
    67→            "mRS, SOFA, etc.).\n\n"
    68→            "An ordinal scoring system has these characteristics:\n"
    69→            "- Uses discrete numeric grades/scores (not continuous measurements)\n"
    70→            "- Each grade has a specific clinical meaning\n"
    71→            "- The unit is conceptually {score}, not a physical unit\n"
    72→            "- Common in clinical trials for performance status, organ function, "
    73→            "disease severity\n\n"
    74→            "NOT ordinal scales:\n"
    75→            "- Lab values with physical units (mg/dL, g/L, IU/mL)\n"
    76→            "- Continuous measurements (weight, height, BMI)\n"
    77→            "- Binary yes/no conditions (HIV status, pregnancy)\n"
    78→            "- Age, duration, or time-based criteria\n\n"
    79→            f"Entities to evaluate:\n{entities_text}\n\n"
    80→            "For each entity that IS an ordinal scale, propose:\n"
    81→            "- scale_name: snake_case identifier for YAML config\n"
    82→            "- entity_aliases: alternative names clinicians might use\n"
    83→            "- loinc_code: LOINC code if known\n"
    84→            "- values: list of grades with descriptions\n"
    85→        )
    86→
    87→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
    88→        response = parse_structured_output(result, OrdinalResolutionResponse)
    89→
    90→        # Filter to confirmed ordinals with sufficient confidence
    91→        confirmed: list[OrdinalScaleProposal] = [
    92→            p for p in response.proposals if p.is_ordinal_scale and p.confidence >= 0.7
    93→        ]
    94→
    95→        return OrdinalResolutionResponse(proposals=confirmed)
    96→
    97→    except Exception as e:
    98→        logger.warning(
    99→            "Ordinal resolution failed: %s",
   100→            e,
   101→            exc_info=True,
   102→        )
   103→        return None
   104→

   249→class TestFieldMappingAccumulation:

   252→    def test_new_mappings_extend_existing(self) -> None:
   253→        """New field_mappings should be appended to existing ones."""
   254→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (

   264→            }

   271→        mock_session.get.return_value = mock_criterion
   272→

   275→                "entity": "eGFR",
   276→                "relation": ">",
   277→                "value": "30",

   279→            }

   282→        _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   283→            session=mock_session,

   285→            entity_text="eGFR",
   286→            field_mappings=new_mappings,
   287→            criterion_id="crit-accum",
   288→        )
   289→
   290→        final = mock_criterion.conditions["field_mappings"]
   291→        assert len(final) == 2
   292→        entities = [m["entity"] for m in final]
   293→        assert "HbA1c" in entities
   294→        assert "eGFR" in entities

    62→def _setup_parent(session: Session) -> tuple[str, str]:
    63→    """Create Protocol + CriteriaBatch, return (protocol_id, batch_id)."""
    64→    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT-ORDINAL-TEST", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
    65→    session.add(protocol)
    66→    session.flush()
    67→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    68→    session.add(batch)
    69→    session.flush()
    70→    return protocol.id, batch.id
    71→
    72→
    73→def _make_criterion(
    74→    session: Session, batch_id: str, text: str, criteria_type: str = "inclusion"
    75→) -> Criteria:
    76→    crit = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type=criteria_type, text=text)
    77→    session.add(crit)
    78→    session.flush()
    79→    return crit
    80→
    81→
    82→def _make_atomic(
    83→    session: Session,
    84→    criterion_id: str,
    85→    protocol_id: str,
    86→    *,
    87→    original_text: str,
    88→    relation_operator: str = "<=",
    89→    value_numeric: float | None = None,
    90→    value_text: str | None = None,
    91→    unit_text: str | None = None,
    92→    unit_concept_id: int | None = None,
    93→) -> AtomicCriterion:
    94→    atomic = AtomicCriterion(
    95→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
    96→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
    97→        inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
    98→        relation_operator=relation_operator,
    99→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   100→        value_text=value_text,
   101→        unit_text=unit_text,
   102→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   103→        original_text=original_text,
   104→    )
   105→    session.add(atomic)
   106→    session.flush()
   107→    return atomic
   108→

   269→class TestOrdinalResolveNodeE2E:

   272→    async def test_child_pugh_gets_resolved(self, engine, session) -> None:
   273→        """Child-Pugh criterion with mocked LLM → unit_concept_id updated to 8527."""
   274→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   275→        crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, "Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)")
   276→        atomic = _make_atomic(
   277→            session,
   278→            crit.id,
   279→            protocol_id,
   280→            original_text="Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)",
   281→            relation_operator="<=",
   282→            value_numeric=6.0,
   283→            unit_text=None,
   284→            unit_concept_id=None,
   285→        )
   286→        session.commit()
   287→
   288→        mock_response = _build_mock_response(
   289→            [
   290→                {
   291→                    "entity_text": "Child-Pugh score",
   292→                    "is_ordinal_scale": True,
   293→                    "confidence": 0.95,
   294→                    "scale_name": "child_pugh",
   295→                    "entity_aliases": ["Child-Pugh"],
   296→                    "loinc_code": "75622-1",
   297→                },
   298→            ]
   299→        )
   300→
   301→        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node
   302→
   303→        state = {
   304→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   305→            "batch_id": batch_id,
   306→            "error": None,
   307→            "errors": [],
   308→        }
   309→
   310→        with (
   311→            patch("protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine", engine),
   312→            patch("langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI") as mock_cls,
   313→            patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"}),
   314→        ):
   315→            mock_model = mock_cls.return_value
   316→            mock_structured = mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value
   317→            mock_structured.invoke.return_value = mock_response
   318→
   319→            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   320→
   321→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   322→
   323→        # Verify the AtomicCriterion was updated
   324→        session.expire_all()
   325→        updated = session.get(AtomicCriterion, atomic.id)
   326→        assert updated is not None
   327→        assert updated.unit_concept_id == 8527

   329→    async def test_known_scales_not_rechecked(self, engine, session) -> None:
   330→        """ECOG (already 8527 from YAML) is NOT sent to LLM."""
   331→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   332→        crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, "ECOG <= 2")

   339→            value_numeric=2.0,
   340→            unit_text=None,
   341→            unit_concept_id=8527,  # Already resolved by YAML
   342→        )
   343→        session.commit()
   344→
   345→        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node
   346→
   347→        state = {
   348→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   349→            "batch_id": batch_id,
   350→            "error": None,
   351→            "errors": [],
   352→        }
   353→
   354→        mock_resolve = AsyncMock(return_value=None)

   356→            patch("protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine", engine),

   360→            ),
   361→        ):
   362→            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   363→
   364→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   365→        # resolve_ordinal_candidates should NOT be called (no candidates)
   366→        mock_resolve.assert_not_called()

   368→    async def test_mixed_batch(self, engine, session) -> None:

   431→        with (

   463→        """AuditLog entry with proposals is created."""

     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import time
    27→from typing import Any
    28→
    29→from api_service.storage import engine
    30→from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    31→from shared.models import AuditLog
    32→from sqlmodel import Session
    33→
    34→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    35→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    36→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    38→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    39→    medgemma_decide,
    40→)
    41→from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
    42→    OmopLookupResult,
    43→    _get_omop_engine,
    44→    lookup_omop_concept,
    45→)
    46→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    47→
    48→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    49→
    50→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    51→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    52→
    53→
    54→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    55→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    56→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    57→    if _router is None:
    58→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    59→    return _router
    60→
    61→
    62→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    63→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    64→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    65→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    66→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    67→
    68→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    69→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    70→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    71→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    72→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    73→
    74→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    75→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    76→
    77→    Args:
    78→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    79→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    80→
    81→    Returns:
    82→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    83→    """
    84→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    85→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    86→
    87→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    88→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    89→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    90→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    91→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    92→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    93→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    94→        else:
    95→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
    96→        logger.info(
    97→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
    98→            result.entity_text[:50],
    99→            result.selected_code,
   100→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   101→            result.reconciliation_status,
   102→        )
   103→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   104→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   105→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   106→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   107→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   108→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   109→
   110→    return result
   111→
   112→
   113→def _log_grounding_audit(
   114→    session: Session,
   115→    protocol_id: str,
   116→    criterion_id: str,
   117→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   118→    candidates: list[Any],
   119→    result: Any,
   120→) -> None:
   121→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   122→
   123→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   124→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   125→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   126→
   127→    Args:
   128→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   129→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   130→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   131→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   132→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   133→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   134→    """
   135→    audit = AuditLog(
   136→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   137→        target_type="criteria",
   138→        target_id=criterion_id,
   139→        details={
   140→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   141→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   142→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   143→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   144→            "candidates": [
   145→                {
   146→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   147→                    "code": c.code,
   148→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   149→                    "score": c.score,
   150→                }
   151→                for c in candidates
   152→            ],
   153→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   154→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   155→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   156→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   157→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   158→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   159→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   160→        },
   161→    )
   162→    session.add(audit)
   163→
   164→
   165→def _flush_audit_logs(
   166→    protocol_id: str,
   167→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   168→) -> None:
   169→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   170→
   171→    Args:
   172→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   173→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   174→    """
   175→    if not pending_audits:
   176→        return
   177→    try:
   178→        with Session(engine) as session:
   179→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   180→                _log_grounding_audit(
   181→                    session,
   182→                    protocol_id,
   183→                    criterion_id,
   184→                    entity,
   185→                    candidates,
   186→                    result,
   187→                )
   188→            session.commit()
   189→    except Exception as audit_error:
   190→        logger.warning(
   191→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   192→            len(pending_audits),
   193→            audit_error,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→
   197→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   198→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   199→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   200→    criterion_text: str,
   201→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   202→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   203→
   204→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   205→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   206→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   207→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   208→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   209→
   210→    Args:
   211→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   212→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   213→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   214→
   215→    Returns:
   216→        EntityGroundingResult. May have expert_review marker in reasoning.
   217→    """
   218→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   219→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   220→
   221→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   222→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   223→
   224→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   225→    if not candidates:
   226→        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   227→        if not apis:
   228→            logger.info(
   229→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   230→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   231→                entity_text[:50],
   232→                entity_type,
   233→            )
   234→        else:
   235→            logger.info(
   236→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→
   241→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   242→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   243→
   244→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   245→    attempt = 1
   246→    current_query = entity_text
   247→    while (
   248→        result.selected_code is None
   249→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   250→        and attempt < 3
   251→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   252→    ):
   253→        attempt += 1
   254→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   255→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   256→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   257→            router=router,
   258→            attempt=attempt,
   259→        )
   260→
   261→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   262→            logger.info(
   263→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   264→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   265→                entity_text[:50],
   266→                attempt,
   267→            )
   268→            return EntityGroundingResult(
   269→                entity_text=entity_text,
   270→                entity_type=entity_type,
   271→                selected_code=None,
   272→                selected_system=None,
   273→                preferred_term=None,
   274→                confidence=0.0,
   275→                candidates=[],
   276→                reasoning=(
   277→                    f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   278→                    f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   279→                ),
   280→            )
   281→
   282→        new_query = (
   283→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   284→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   285→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   286→            or current_query
   287→        )
   288→        current_query = new_query
   289→
   290→        logger.info(
   291→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   292→            attempt,
   293→            entity_text[:50],
   294→            new_query[:50],
   295→        )
   296→
   297→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   298→        if new_candidates:
   299→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   300→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   301→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   302→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   303→                return EntityGroundingResult(
   304→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   305→                    entity_type=result.entity_type,
   306→                    selected_code=result.selected_code,
   307→                    selected_system=result.selected_system,
   308→                    preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   309→                    confidence=result.confidence,
   310→                    candidates=result.candidates,
   311→                    reasoning=(
   312→                        f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] {result.reasoning}"
   313→                    ),
   314→                    field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   315→                )
   316→
   317→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   318→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   319→        logger.warning(
   320→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   321→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   322→            entity_text[:50],
   323→            entity_type,
   324→            attempt,
   325→        )
   326→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   327→            entity_text=result.entity_text,
   328→            entity_type=result.entity_type,
   329→            selected_code=result.selected_code,
   330→            selected_system=result.selected_system,
   331→            preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   332→            confidence=result.confidence,
   333→            candidates=result.candidates,
   334→            reasoning=(
   335→                f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   336→                f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   337→                f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   338→            ),
   339→        )
   340→
   341→    return result
   342→
   343→
   344→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   345→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   346→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   347→    criterion_text: str,
   348→    entity_num: int,
   349→    total: int,
   350→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   351→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None]:
   352→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   353→
   354→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   355→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   356→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   357→
   358→    Args:
   359→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   360→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   361→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   362→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   363→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   364→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   365→
   366→    Returns:
   367→        (EntityGroundingResult, None) on success, or (None, error_message) on failure.
   368→    """
   369→    async with semaphore:
   370→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   371→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   372→        start = time.monotonic()
   373→        logger.info(
   374→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   375→            entity_num,
   376→            total,
   377→            entity_text,
   378→            entity_type,
   379→        )
   380→        try:
   381→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   382→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   383→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   384→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   385→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(full_entity_text, entity_type)
   386→            result, omop_result = await asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)
   387→
   388→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   389→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   390→
   391→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   392→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   393→            if criterion_id:
   394→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   395→
   396→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   397→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   398→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   399→
   400→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   401→            logger.info(
   402→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   403→                entity_num,
   404→                total,
   405→                entity_text,
   406→                elapsed,
   407→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   408→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   409→                result.confidence,
   410→            )
   411→            return (result, None)
   412→        except Exception as e:
   413→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   414→            logger.error(
   415→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   416→                entity_num,
   417→                total,
   418→                entity_text,
   419→                elapsed,
   420→                e,
   421→            )
   422→            return (None, str(e))
   423→
   424→
   425→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   426→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   427→
   428→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   429→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   430→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   431→    entity.
   432→
   433→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   434→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   435→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   436→
   437→    Args:
   438→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   439→
   440→    Returns:
   441→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   442→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   443→    """
   444→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   445→
   446→    if state.get("error"):
   447→        return {}
   448→
   449→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   450→    with pipeline_span(
   451→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   452→    ) as span:
   453→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   454→
   455→        try:
   456→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   457→            if not entities_json:
   458→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   459→                return {
   460→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   461→                }
   462→
   463→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   464→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   465→
   466→            span.set_inputs(
   467→                {
   468→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   469→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   470→                }
   471→            )
   472→
   473→            logger.info(
   474→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   475→                protocol_id,
   476→                len(entity_items),
   477→            )
   478→
   479→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   480→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   481→            _get_omop_engine()
   482→
   483→            router = _get_router()
   484→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   485→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   486→
   487→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   488→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   489→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   490→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   491→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   492→
   493→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   494→                    logger.info(
   495→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   496→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   497→                        entity_text[:50],
   498→                    )
   499→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   500→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   501→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   502→                        selected_code=None,
   503→                        selected_system=None,
   504→                        preferred_term=None,
   505→                        confidence=0.0,
   506→                        candidates=[],
   507→                        reasoning=(
   508→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   509→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   510→                        ),
   511→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   512→                    )
   513→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   514→                else:
   515→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   516→
   517→            # Dev/debug knob: truncate entity list for faster pipeline runs
   518→            _max_entities = int(os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES", "0"))
   519→            if _max_entities > 0:
   520→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   521→                logger.info(
   522→                    "PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   523→                    _max_entities,
   524→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   525→                )
   526→
   527→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   528→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   529→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   530→
   531→            tasks = [
   532→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   533→                    entity,
   534→                    router,
   535→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   536→                    idx,
   537→                    len(entity_items),
   538→                    semaphore,
   539→                )
   540→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   541→            ]
   542→
   543→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   544→
   545→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   546→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   547→            for (idx, entity), (result, error) in zip(entities_to_ground, outcomes):
   548→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   549→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   550→
   551→                if result is not None:
   552→                    pending_audits.append(
   553→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   554→                    )
   555→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   556→                else:
   557→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   558→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   559→                    error_msg = (
   560→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   561→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   562→                    )
   563→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   564→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   565→
   566→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   567→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   568→
   569→            logger.info(
   570→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   571→                protocol_id,
   572→                len(grounding_results),
   573→                len(accumulated_errors),
   574→            )
   575→
   576→            span.set_outputs(
   577→                {
   578→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   579→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   580→                }
   581→            )
   582→
   583→            return {
   584→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   585→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   586→            }
   587→
   588→        except Exception as e:
   589→            logger.exception(
   590→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   591→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   592→                e,
   593→            )
   594→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   595→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   596→
```

```
     1→"""MLflow tracing helpers for pipeline nodes.
     2→
     3→Provides a safe context manager that creates MLflow traces when available
     4→and falls back to a no-op when MLflow is not configured or installed.
     5→
     6→Each node creates its own separate top-level trace (not a child span),
     7→tagged with protocol_id so traces from the same pipeline run can be
     8→grouped and filtered in the MLflow UI.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import os
    14→from contextlib import contextmanager
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→
    18→@contextmanager
    19→def pipeline_span(
    20→    name: str,
    21→    span_type: str = "CHAIN",
    22→    protocol_id: str = "",
    23→):
    24→    """Create a separate MLflow trace for a pipeline node.
    25→
    26→    Each call creates its own top-level trace tagged with protocol_id,
    27→    so individual node traces appear in MLflow as they complete rather
    28→    than waiting for the entire pipeline to finish.
    29→
    30→    Args:
    31→        name: Trace/span name (e.g., "ingest_node", "ground_node").
    32→        span_type: MLflow span type (default "CHAIN").
    33→        protocol_id: Protocol ID to tag the trace with for session grouping.
    34→
    35→    Yields:
    36→        MLflow span object or a no-op wrapper.
    37→    """
    38→    try:
    39→        import mlflow
    40→
    41→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
    42→            with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type=span_type) as span:
    43→                if protocol_id:
    44→                    try:
    45→                        mlflow.update_current_trace(
    46→                            tags={"protocol_id": protocol_id},
    47→                        )
    48→                    except Exception:
    49→                        pass  # Tag failure is non-fatal
    50→                yield span
    51→                return
    52→    except ImportError:
    53→        pass
    54→    except Exception:
    55→        pass
    56→
    57→    # Fallback: no-op span
    58→    yield _NoOpSpan()
    59→
    60→
    61→class _NoOpSpan:
    62→    """No-op span that silently accepts MLflow span API calls."""
    63→
    64→    def set_inputs(self, inputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    65→        pass
    66→
    67→    def set_outputs(self, outputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    68→        pass
    69→
    70→    def set_status(self, status: str) -> None:
    71→        pass
    72→

     1→"""Vertex AI Model Garden integration for MedGemma.
     2→
     3→Provides ModelGardenChatModel (a LangChain BaseChatModel wrapper for
     4→Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints) and a factory function for creating
     5→lazy model loaders.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import logging
    11→import time
    12→from typing import Any, Callable
    13→
    14→import requests  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    15→from langchain_core.callbacks.manager import CallbackManagerForLLMRun
    16→from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
    17→from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, BaseMessage
    18→from langchain_core.outputs import ChatGeneration, ChatResult
    19→from pydantic import Field
    20→from shared.lazy_cache import lazy_singleton
    21→from tenacity import (
    22→    retry,
    23→    retry_if_exception,
    24→    stop_after_attempt,
    25→    wait_exponential,
    26→)
    27→
    28→from inference.config import AgentConfig
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _build_gemma_prompt(messages: list[Any]) -> str:
    34→    """Build Gemma chat template prompt from messages.
    35→
    36→    Args:
    37→        messages: List of LangChain messages.
    38→
    39→    Returns:
    40→        Formatted prompt string.
    41→    """
    42→    prompt_parts = []
    43→    for msg in messages:
    44→        role = "user"
    45→        if msg.type == "ai":
    46→            role = "model"
    47→
    48→        content = msg.content
    49→        if not isinstance(content, str):
    50→            content = str(content)
    51→
    52→        if role == "user":
    53→            content = f"### Instruction:\n{content}"
    54→
    55→        prompt_parts.append(f"<start_of_turn>{role}\n{content}<end_of_turn>")
    56→
    57→    return "\n".join(prompt_parts) + "\n<start_of_turn>model\n"
    58→
    59→
    60→def _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text: str, full_prompt: str) -> str:
    61→    """Strip echoed prompts and end-of-turn markers from response.
    62→
    63→    Note: This is now minimal because Gemini handles JSON structuring.
    64→    MedGemma's output goes to Gemini, so thinking tokens and other artifacts
    65→    are handled by Gemini's structured output parser.
    66→    """
    67→    # Strip echoed prompt if present
    68→    if text.startswith(full_prompt):
    69→        text = text[len(full_prompt) :].strip()
    70→
    71→    # Strip trailing end-of-turn marker
    72→    if text.endswith("<end_of_turn>"):
    73→        text = text[: -len("<end_of_turn>")].strip()
    74→
    75→    return text
    76→
    77→
    78→def _is_retryable_error(exception: BaseException) -> bool:
    79→    """Check if an exception is retryable (transient error).
    80→
    81→    Args:
    82→        exception: The exception to check.
    83→
    84→    Returns:
    85→        True if the exception is retryable, False otherwise.
    86→    """
    87→    # Retry on network/connection errors
    88→    if isinstance(
    89→        exception,
    90→        (
    91→            requests.exceptions.ConnectionError,
    92→            requests.exceptions.Timeout,
    93→            requests.exceptions.ChunkedEncodingError,
    94→        ),
    95→    ):
    96→        return True
    97→
    98→    # Retry on Google API transient server errors
    99→    try:
   100→        from google.api_core import exceptions as google_exceptions
   101→
   102→        if isinstance(
   103→            exception,
   104→            (
   105→                google_exceptions.ServiceUnavailable,
   106→                google_exceptions.InternalServerError,
   107→                google_exceptions.DeadlineExceeded,
   108→                google_exceptions.ResourceExhausted,
   109→            ),
   110→        ):
   111→            return True
   112→
   113→        # Do NOT retry on client errors
   114→        if isinstance(
   115→            exception,
   116→            (
   117→                google_exceptions.PermissionDenied,
   118→                google_exceptions.Unauthenticated,
   119→                google_exceptions.InvalidArgument,
   120→                google_exceptions.NotFound,
   121→                google_exceptions.AlreadyExists,
   122→                google_exceptions.FailedPrecondition,
   123→                google_exceptions.OutOfRange,
   124→            ),
   125→        ):
   126→            return False
   127→    except ImportError:
   128→        pass
   129→
   130→    return False
   131→
   132→
   133→@retry(
   134→    retry=retry_if_exception(_is_retryable_error),
   135→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   136→    wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=1, max=10),
   137→    reraise=True,
   138→)
   139→def _predict_with_retry(
   140→    endpoint: Any, instances: list[dict[str, Any]], parameters: dict[str, Any]
   141→) -> Any:
   142→    """Call Vertex AI endpoint.predict with retry on transient errors.
   143→
   144→    Args:
   145→        endpoint: Vertex AI Endpoint object.
   146→        instances: List of prediction instances.
   147→        parameters: Prediction parameters.
   148→
   149→    Returns:
   150→        Prediction response from the endpoint.
   151→    """
   152→    return endpoint.predict(instances=instances, parameters=parameters)
   153→
   154→
   155→class ModelGardenChatModel(BaseChatModel):
   156→    """LangChain ChatModel wrapper for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.
   157→
   158→    Wraps a Vertex AI endpoint that serves MedGemma (or other Gemma-family
   159→    models) using the Gemma chat template for prompt formatting and
   160→    exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.
   161→    """
   162→
   163→    endpoint_resource_name: str
   164→    project: str
   165→    location: str
   166→    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=8192)
   167→
   168→    def _generate(
   169→        self,
   170→        messages: list[BaseMessage],
   171→        stop: list[str] | None = None,
   172→        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,
   173→        **kwargs: Any,
   174→    ) -> ChatResult:
   175→        """Generate a chat response from the Model Garden endpoint.
   176→
   177→        Args:
   178→            messages: List of LangChain messages.
   179→            stop: Optional stop sequences (unused by Model Garden).
   180→            run_manager: Optional callback manager.
   181→            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).
   182→
   183→        Returns:
   184→            ChatResult containing the model's response.
   185→        """
   186→        from google.cloud import aiplatform
   187→
   188→        endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)
   189→
   190→        full_prompt = _build_gemma_prompt(messages)
   191→
   192→        instance = {
   193→            "prompt": full_prompt,
   194→            "max_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   195→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   196→            "top_p": 0.95,
   197→            "top_k": 40,
   198→        }
   199→        parameters = {
   200→            "max_output_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   201→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   202→        }
   203→
   204→        start_time = time.time()
   205→        try:
   206→            logger.debug(
   207→                "Calling Vertex AI endpoint.predict for endpoint: %s",
   208→                self.endpoint_resource_name,
   209→            )
   210→            response = _predict_with_retry(
   211→                endpoint=endpoint, instances=[instance], parameters=parameters
   212→            )
   213→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   214→            logger.debug(
   215→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict succeeded in %.2f seconds",
   216→                duration,
   217→            )
   218→        except Exception as e:
   219→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   220→            logger.warning(
   221→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict failed after %.2f seconds: %s",
   222→                duration,
   223→                e,
   224→                exc_info=True,
   225→            )
   226→            raise
   227→
   228→        text = response.predictions[0]
   229→        text = _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text, full_prompt)
   230→
   231→        message = AIMessage(content=text)
   232→        generation = ChatGeneration(message=message)
   233→        return ChatResult(generations=[generation])
   234→
   235→    @property
   236→    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
   237→        return "vertex_model_garden"
   238→
   239→
   240→def _validate_vertex_config(cfg: AgentConfig) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
   241→    """Validate Vertex AI configuration fields.
   242→
   243→    Args:
   244→        cfg: AgentConfig instance.
   245→
   246→    Returns:
   247→        Tuple of (project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name).
   248→
   249→    Raises:
   250→        ValueError: If required configuration is missing.
   251→    """
   252→    project_id = (cfg.gcp_project_id or "").strip()
   253→    region = (cfg.gcp_region or "").strip()
   254→    endpoint_id = (cfg.vertex_endpoint_id or "").strip()
   255→    vertex_model_name = (cfg.vertex_model_name or "").strip()
   256→
   257→    if not project_id:
   258→        raise ValueError("GCP_PROJECT_ID is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   259→    if not region:
   260→        raise ValueError("GCP_REGION is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   261→    if not endpoint_id and not vertex_model_name:
   262→        raise ValueError(
   263→            "VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID or VERTEX_MODEL_NAME is required when "
   264→            "MODEL_BACKEND=vertex"
   265→        )
   266→
   267→    return project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name
   268→
   269→
   270→def create_model_loader(config: AgentConfig | None = None) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   271→    """Create a lazy MedGemma model loader for the configured backend.
   272→
   273→    Args:
   274→        config: Agent configuration. Defaults to ``AgentConfig.from_env()``.
   275→
   276→    Returns:
   277→        Callable that loads and returns a LangChain chat model when invoked.
   278→
   279→    Raises:
   280→        ValueError: If required Vertex configuration is missing.
   281→        NotImplementedError: If backend is "local" (not yet ported).
   282→    """
   283→    cfg = config or AgentConfig.from_env()
   284→
   285→    if cfg.backend == "vertex":
   286→        return _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg)
   287→
   288→    raise NotImplementedError("Local MedGemma loading not yet ported")
   289→
   290→
   291→def _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   292→    """Create a lazy Vertex AI model loader.
   293→
   294→    Args:
   295→        cfg: Validated AgentConfig with backend=="vertex".
   296→
   297→    Returns:
   298→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   299→    """
   300→    project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name = _validate_vertex_config(cfg)
   301→
   302→    @lazy_singleton
   303→    def load_model() -> Any:
   304→        try:
   305→            import vertexai
   306→        except ImportError as exc:
   307→            raise ImportError(
   308→                "Vertex AI backend requires google-cloud-aiplatform installed."
   309→            ) from exc
   310→
   311→        vertexai.init(project=project_id, location=region)
   312→
   313→        if vertex_model_name:
   314→            try:
   315→                from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   316→            except ImportError as exc:
   317→                raise ImportError(
   318→                    "Vertex AI backend requires langchain-google-genai installed."
   319→                ) from exc
   320→
   321→            return ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   322→                model=vertex_model_name,
   323→                project=project_id,
   324→                location=region,
   325→                vertexai=True,
   326→                max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   327→            )
   328→
   329→        endpoint_resource_name = (
   330→            f"projects/{project_id}/locations/{region}/endpoints/{endpoint_id}"
   331→        )
   332→        return ModelGardenChatModel(
   333→            endpoint_resource_name=endpoint_resource_name,
   334→            project=project_id,
   335→            location=region,
   336→            max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   337→        )
   338→
   339→    return load_model
   340→
```

  "old_string": "class ModelGardenChatModel(BaseChatModel):\n    \"\"\"LangChain ChatModel wrapper for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.\n\n    Wraps a Vertex AI endpoint that serves MedGemma (or other Gemma-family\n    models) using the Gemma chat template for prompt formatting and\n    exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    endpoint_resource_name: str\n    project: str\n    location: str\n    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=8192)\n\n    def _generate(\n        self,\n        messages: list[BaseMessage],\n        stop: list[str] | None = None,\n        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,\n        **kwargs: Any,\n    ) -> ChatResult:\n        \"\"\"Generate a chat response from the Model Garden endpoint.\n\n        Args:\n            messages: List of LangChain messages.\n            stop: Optional stop sequences (unused by Model Garden).\n            run_manager: Optional callback manager.\n            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).\n\n        Returns:\n            ChatResult containing the model's response.\n        \"\"\"\n        from google.cloud import aiplatform\n\n        endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)",
  "new_string": "class ModelGardenChatModel(BaseChatModel):\n    \"\"\"LangChain ChatModel wrapper for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.\n\n    Wraps a Vertex AI endpoint that serves MedGemma (or other Gemma-family\n    models) using the Gemma chat template for prompt formatting and\n    exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.\n\n    The Endpoint object is instantiated once in the constructor and reused\n    across calls to enable gRPC channel reuse and avoid per-call overhead.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    endpoint_resource_name: str\n    project: str\n    location: str\n    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=8192)\n    _endpoint: Any = None\n\n    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize the Vertex AI Endpoint once for connection reuse.\"\"\"\n        from google.cloud import aiplatform\n\n        self._endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)\n\n    def _generate(\n        self,\n        messages: list[BaseMessage],\n        stop: list[str] | None = None,\n        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,\n        **kwargs: Any,\n    ) -> ChatResult:\n        \"\"\"Generate a chat response from the Model Garden endpoint.\n\n        Args:\n            messages: List of LangChain messages.\n            stop: Optional stop sequences (unused by Model Garden).\n            run_manager: Optional callback manager.\n            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).\n\n        Returns:\n            ChatResult containing the model's response.\n        \"\"\"\n        endpoint = self._endpoint"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py",
  "old_string": "            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore\n            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop\n            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)",
  "new_string": "            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before\n            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.\n            try:\n                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import _get_medgemma_model\n                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as _HM\n\n                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()\n                await warmup_model.ainvoke([_HM(content=\"ready\")])\n                logger.info(\"MedGemma warmup succeeded\")\n            except Exception as warmup_err:\n                logger.info(\"MedGemma warmup skipped: %s\", warmup_err)\n\n            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore\n            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop\n            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)"
}

All 5 phases are complete. Here's a summary of what was done on `feature/perf-test-limits-and-async-grounding` (8 commits):

**Phase 1 (Investigation)** — Completed in prior session, findings committed:
- 5 sync `.invoke()` call sites identified
- All 207 tests run in ~4.5s (mocked, no live APIs)
- MedGemma cold-start root cause: `min_replica_count=0`, Endpoint reinstantiated per call
- Agentic retry rate: 0-3.3%, no threshold change needed

**Phase 2 (Test Limits)** — `PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA=3` and `PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=5` in conftest.py, `@pytest.mark.slow` marker added

**Phase 3 (Async Fix)** — All 5 sync `.invoke()` calls converted to `await .ainvoke()` across medgemma_decider, field_mapper, structure_builder, ordinal_resolver. Test mocks updated in 5 test files.

**Phase 4 (Tracing)** — Ground node span now includes `avg_entity_ms`, `max_entity_ms`, `retry_count`. All 7 nodes confirmed tagging with `protocol_id`.

**Phase 5 (Endpoint Fixes)** — `aiplatform.Endpoint` moved to constructor for gRPC channel reuse. Pre-flight warmup call added before parallel entity batch.

All **207 tests pass** with no regressions across all phases. Branch is ready for PR into `feature/major-refactor-langgraph`.

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive research report.

## End-to-End Smoketest Research Report

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's what I found:

---

### 1. **Pipeline Triggering Mechanism**

The protocol processing pipeline is triggered via a **transactional outbox event pattern**:

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/trigger.py`

**How it works:**
- When a protocol upload is confirmed via `POST /protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload`, an outbox event is published (`PROTOCOL_UPLOADED`)
- The API service (`main.py`) runs an `OutboxProcessor` in the background that continuously polls for events
- When a `protocol_uploaded` event is found, it calls `handle_protocol_uploaded()` function
- This function:
  - Constructs initial pipeline state from the event payload
  - Generates a unique thread_id for LangGraph checkpoint management
  - Invokes the async 7-node LangGraph workflow via `asyncio.run()`
  - Stores the thread_id in protocol metadata for retry_from_checkpoint lookups

**Key entry point**: `handle_protocol_uploaded(payload)` synchronously bridges to the async graph

---

### 2. **Environment Variables & Services Required**

**Critical environment variables** (from `.env.example` and `docker-compose.yml`):

```
DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@host:5432/app
  → PostgreSQL for protocol storage, checkpoints, and outbox events
  
GOOGLE_API_KEY=<your-api-key>
  → Gemini API for extraction, structuring, ordinal resolution
  
UMLS_API_KEY=<your-api-key>
  → UMLS terminology API for entity grounding (required by ground node)
  
MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://localhost:5001
  → Optional but recommended for experiment tracking and observability
  
MODEL_BACKEND=vertex | gemini
GCP_PROJECT_ID=<your-project>
VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID=<endpoint-id>
GCP_REGION=europe-west4
  → Required only if using Vertex AI (MedGemma); otherwise use Gemini API
  
JWT_SECRET_KEY=<secret>
  → For API authentication (defaults to dev-secret-key-change-in-production)
  
USE_LOCAL_STORAGE=1
  → For local development; uses local file system instead of GCS
  → Upload URL points to /local-upload/{blob_path} endpoint
```

**Required external services:**
- PostgreSQL (port 5432) — database + checkpoint storage
- Gemini API or Vertex AI — LLM for extraction/grounding
- UMLS API — terminology grounding service
- Optional: MLflow (port 5001) — experiment tracking
- Optional: Google Cloud Storage — for production file storage

---

### 3. **Docker Compose Setup**

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/infra/docker-compose.yml`

**Command to start full stack:**
```bash
docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build
```

**Services included:**
- `api` (port 8000) — FastAPI service with OutboxProcessor
- `db` (PostgreSQL, port 5432) — application database
- `mlflow` (port 5001) — experiment tracking UI
- `pubsub-emulator` (port 8432) — Google Pub/Sub emulator
- `omop-vocab` (port 5433) — Optional OMOP vocabulary database (requires `--profile omop`)
- `ui` (port 3000) — React frontend

**Key features:**
- Database health checks before API startup
- MLflow configured to auto-log LangChain traces (see `mlflow.langchain.autolog(run_tracer_inline=True)`)
- Checkpointer (AsyncPostgresSaver) automatically creates checkpoint tables on first graph invocation
- LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR mounted to `/app/uploads` for file storage in dev mode

---

### 4. **Existing E2E Test Infrastructure**

**Location**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/`

**Files:**
- `conftest.py` — Fixtures for API client, database session, PDF upload factory
- `test_pipeline_full.py` — 4 comprehensive E2E tests (E2E-01, E2E-02, E2E-03, E2E-06)
- `test_infrastructure_smoke.py` — 3 infrastructure smoke tests
- `baseline.py` — Regression thresholds for test PDFs

**How to run existing tests:**
```bash
# Full stack must be running first:
docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d

# Then run E2E tests:
uv run pytest tests/e2e/ -v -m e2e

# Or run only infrastructure smoke tests:
uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_infrastructure_smoke.py -v -m e2e
```

**Test infrastructure:**
- Auto-skips if Docker Compose stack unavailable (checks `/health` at localhost:8000 and PostgreSQL at localhost:5432)
- Provides authenticated httpx client (JWT with synthetic test user)
- Factory pattern for PDF uploads (mimics browser 3-step flow):
  1. `POST /protocols/upload` → get signed/local upload URL
  2. `PUT /local-upload/{blob_path}` → upload PDF bytes
  3. `POST /protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload` → trigger pipeline
- Auto-cleanup fixture that deletes all test-created protocols and related data (respects foreign key constraints)
- `wait_for_pipeline()` helper polls protocol status until terminal state (`pending_review`, `extraction_failed`, `dead_letter`, etc.)

**Test datasets:**
- Tests reference `data/protocols/clinicaltrials/Prot_000-*.pdf` PDFs (actual CRC/ClinicalTrials protocol PDFs)
- Default test PDF: `data/protocols/clinicaltrials/Prot_000-2d2f25ab.pdf` (~90K, small for fast testing)

**Regression baselines** (from `baseline.py`):
- Only one baseline currently defined (for the default PDF)
- Thresholds are intentionally conservative:
  - min_criteria: 3
  - min_inclusion: 1
  - min_exclusion: 1
  - min_entities: 2
  - min_grounded_entities: 1

---

### 5. **API Endpoints for Pipeline Triggering**

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/protocols.py`

**Key endpoints:**

```
POST /protocols/upload
  → Request signed/local upload URL
  → Creates Protocol record with "uploaded" status
  → Returns protocol_id, upload_url, gcs_path

PUT /local-upload/{blob_path}
  → Direct file upload (local storage only)
  → Used in dev mode via USE_LOCAL_STORAGE=1

POST /protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload
  → Confirm upload and trigger pipeline
  → Optionally accepts pdf_bytes_base64 for quality scoring
  → Publishes PROTOCOL_UPLOADED outbox event
  → Returns protocol record with status

POST /protocols/{protocol_id}/retry
  → Retry failed protocol from last LangGraph checkpoint
  → Only works for: extraction_failed, grounding_failed, dead_letter states
  → Updates status to "processing" and resumes from checkpoint
  → Does NOT create new outbox event (uses saved checkpoint directly)

GET /protocols/{protocol_id}
  → Fetch protocol detail (status, error_reason, quality_score, etc.)

GET /protocols/{protocol_id}/batches
  → Get all CriteriaBatch records (archived + non-archived)
  → Shows re-extraction history in timeline view

GET /reviews/batches/{batch_id}/criteria
  → Get criteria for a batch (includes entities with grounding confidence)
```

---

### 6. **Running the Pipeline Directly via Python Script**

The pipeline can be invoked directly without the API/outbox:

**Approach 1: Direct LangGraph invocation**
```python
from protocol_processor.graph import get_graph
from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
import asyncio

async def run_pipeline():
    graph = await get_graph()
    
    initial_state = {
        "protocol_id": "test-proto-123",
        "file_uri": "gs://bucket/path/file.pdf",  # or "local://path/to/file.pdf"
        "title": "Trial Protocol",
        "batch_id": None,
        "pdf_bytes": None,
        "extraction_json": None,
        "entities_json": None,
        "grounded_entities_json": None,
        "archived_reviewed_criteria": None,
        "status": "processing",
        "error": None,
        "errors": [],
    }
    
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test-thread-1"}}
    result = await graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)
    return result

asyncio.run(run_pipeline())
```

**Approach 2: Simulate the outbox handler directly**
```python
from protocol_processor.trigger import handle_protocol_uploaded
import asyncio

payload = {
    "protocol_id": "test-proto-456",
    "file_uri": "local://data/protocols/clinicaltrials/Prot_000-2d2f25ab.pdf",
    "title": "Sample Protocol",
}

handle_protocol_uploaded(payload)  # Synchronous; internally runs asyncio.run()
```

**Important note**: The `retry_from_checkpoint()` function requires the protocol to already exist in the database with a saved checkpoint. It reads the thread_id from `protocol.metadata_["pipeline_thread_id"]` and resumes from there.

---

### 7. **Simplest Way to Run a Full Smoketest**

**Recommended approach:** Use the existing E2E test infrastructure + a new simple smoketest script

**Steps:**

```bash
# 1. Ensure .env is configured
cp .env.example .env
# Then manually set: GOOGLE_API_KEY, UMLS_API_KEY, GCLOUD_PROFILE (if using Vertex)

# 2. Start full Docker Compose stack
docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d

# 3. Wait for all services to be healthy (especially db and api)
# You can check: curl http://localhost:8000/health

# 4. Run existing E2E tests (they cover extraction-grounding loop)
uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_pipeline_full.py::TestFullPipeline::test_pipeline_completes_successfully -v

# 5. Or run the infrastructure smoke test (faster, just checks upload works)
uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_infrastructure_smoke.py -v
```

**To test with a specific PDF:**
```bash
# Use custom PDF via conftest.py fixture
uv run pytest tests/e2e/ -v --pdf-path="data/protocols/clinicaltrials/Prot_000-92094ef3.pdf"
# (Note: fixture doesn't yet support CLI arg, would need conftest modification)
```

**Minimal smoke test (no Docker):**

If you only want to test the Python pipeline code without running the full stack:

```bash
# Run unit tests for individual nodes (if they exist)
uv run pytest services/protocol-processor-service/tests -v -k "not slow"

# Run pytest with integration markers
uv run pytest -v -m "not slow"  # Skips slow extraction/grounding tests
```

---

### 8. **Pipeline Architecture (7-Node LangGraph)**

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/graph.py`

**Flow:**
```
START -> ingest -> extract -> parse -> ground -> persist -> structure -> ordinal_resolve -> END
         |          |        |
         +-----ERROR ROUTING (conditional edges)
                     |
                     v
                    END (on fatal error)
```

**Nodes:**

| Node | Purpose | Error Handling |
|------|---------|---|
| **ingest** | Fetch PDF bytes from GCS/local storage | Fatal error routes to END |
| **extract** | Gemini extraction of inclusion/exclusion criteria | Fatal error routes to END |
| **parse** | Parse extracted JSON into Criteria records; create CriteriaBatch | Fatal error routes to END |
| **ground** | UMLS grounding + entity confidence scoring | Error accumulation (partial failures preserved) |
| **persist** | Save Criteria + Entity records to database | Handles partial/total failures internally |
| **structure** | Build expression trees from criteria free-text | Error accumulation |
| **ordinal_resolve** | Detect & map ordinal scales (NYHA, ECOG, etc.) | Error accumulation |

**State management:**
- Minimal flat state using JSON strings for complex data
- Fields populated on-demand as pipeline progresses
- Checkpointing: AsyncPostgresSaver persists state after each node
- Retry support: Resume from last checkpoint by thread_id

---

```
data/protocols/clinicaltrials/
  ├── Prot_000-2d2f25ab.pdf    (default, ~90K - fastest)
  ├── Prot_000-23b31694.pdf
  ├── Prot_000-598bd24d.pdf

### 10. **Key Configuration Files**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|

---

### Summary

**Quickest smoketest path:**

1. Configure `.env` with API keys (GOOGLE_API_KEY, UMLS_API_KEY)
2. Start Docker Compose: `docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d`
3. Wait for services to be healthy (~30s)
4. Run E2E test: `uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_infrastructure_smoke.py::TestInfrastructureSmoke::test_upload_creates_protocol -v`
5. For full extraction-grounding test: `uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_pipeline_full.py::TestFullPipeline::test_pipeline_completes_successfully -v -s` (180s timeout)

**Key insight**: The pipeline is **event-driven and asynchronous**. Once you confirm upload, the OutboxProcessor automatically triggers the LangGraph workflow in the background. The E2E tests poll the protocol status endpoint until the pipeline reaches a terminal state.
</output>

     3→Provides Docker Compose stack detection, automatic skip when the stack is

     5→PDF upload factory, database cleanup) for end-to-end tests against the real

     7→
     8→Usage:

    10→    is not running, all e2e-marked tests are automatically skipped (exit 0).

    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import base64

    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    40→
    41→def _check_stack() -> bool:
    42→    """Check whether the Docker Compose stack (API + PostgreSQL) is reachable."""
    43→    api_url = os.getenv("E2E_API_URL", _DEFAULT_API_URL)
    44→    try:
    45→        resp = httpx.get(f"{api_url}/health", timeout=3.0)
    46→        if resp.status_code != 200:
    47→            return False
    48→    except (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.TimeoutException, Exception):
    49→        return False
    50→
    51→    # Also verify PostgreSQL is reachable
    52→    try:
    53→        from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    54→
    55→        db_url = os.getenv("E2E_DATABASE_URL", _DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL)
    56→        engine = create_engine(db_url, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 3})
    57→        with engine.connect() as conn:
    58→            conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    59→        engine.dispose()
    60→    except Exception:
    61→        return False
    62→
    63→    return True
    64→
    65→
    66→def _wants_e2e(config: pytest.Config) -> bool:
    67→    """Return True only if the user explicitly requested e2e tests.
    68→
    69→    This guard prevents the health-check from adding latency to regular
    70→    ``uv run pytest`` invocations that do not target E2E tests.
    71→    """
    72→    # Check -m flag for e2e marker expression
    73→    markexpr: str = config.getoption("-m", default="")
    74→    if markexpr and "e2e" in markexpr:
    75→        return True
    76→
    77→    # Check if any explicit test path is under tests/e2e/
    78→    args: list[str] = config.getoption("file_or_dir", default=[]) or []
    79→    for arg in args:
    80→        if "tests/e2e" in str(arg) or "tests\\e2e" in str(arg):
    81→            return True
    82→
    83→    return False
    84→
    85→

    87→# Pytest hooks
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→
    90→
    91→def pytest_configure(config: pytest.Config) -> None:
    92→    """Detect Docker Compose availability once per session (if e2e requested)."""
    93→    global _STACK_AVAILABLE
    94→    if _wants_e2e(config):
    95→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = _check_stack()
    96→    else:
    97→        # Don't waste time checking -- no e2e tests requested
    98→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = False

   283→    """Delete all test-created protocols and related data after each test.
   284→
   285→    Deletion order respects foreign key constraints:
   286→    entities -> reviews -> audit_log -> criteria -> criteria_batches ->
   287→    outbox_events -> protocol
   288→    """
   289→    yield  # Run the test
   290→
   291→    if not _created_protocol_ids:
   292→        return
   293→
   294→    from sqlmodel import col, delete, select
   295→
   296→    from shared.models import (
   297→        AuditLog,
   298→        Criteria,
   299→        CriteriaBatch,
   300→        Entity,
   301→        OutboxEvent,
   302→        Protocol,
   303→        Review,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    session = e2e_db_session
   307→
   308→    for protocol_id in _created_protocol_ids:
   309→        # Gather batch IDs for this protocol
   310→        batch_ids: list[str] = [
   311→            row
   312→            for row in session.exec(
   313→                select(CriteriaBatch.id).where(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id == protocol_id)
   314→            ).all()
   315→        ]
   316→
   317→        # Gather criteria IDs across all batches
   318→        criteria_ids: list[str] = []
   319→        if batch_ids:
   320→            criteria_ids = [
   321→                row
   322→                for row in session.exec(
   323→                    select(Criteria.id).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))
   324→                ).all()
   325→            ]
   326→
   327→        # 1. Delete entities for these criteria
   328→        if criteria_ids:
   329→            session.exec(
   330→                delete(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   331→            )
   332→
   333→        # 2. Delete reviews for these criteria
   334→        if criteria_ids:
   335→            session.exec(
   336→                delete(Review).where(col(Review.target_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   337→            )
   338→
   339→        # 3. Delete audit log entries related to this protocol or its criteria
   340→        target_ids = [protocol_id, *criteria_ids]
   341→        session.exec(
   342→            delete(AuditLog).where(col(AuditLog.target_id).in_(target_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   343→        )
   344→
   345→        # 4. Delete criteria
   346→        if batch_ids:
   347→            session.exec(
   348→                delete(Criteria).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   349→            )
   350→
   351→        # 5. Delete criteria batches
   352→        if batch_ids:
   353→            session.exec(
   354→                delete(CriteriaBatch).where(col(CriteriaBatch.id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   355→            )
   356→
   357→        # 6. Delete outbox events for this protocol
   358→        session.exec(
   359→            delete(OutboxEvent).where(
   360→                OutboxEvent.aggregate_id == protocol_id  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   361→            )
   362→        )
   363→
   364→        # 7. Delete the protocol itself
   365→        session.exec(
   366→            delete(Protocol).where(Protocol.id == protocol_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   367→        )
   368→
   369→    session.commit()
   370→    logger.info(
   371→        "E2E cleanup: deleted %d protocol(s) and related data",
   372→        len(_created_protocol_ids),
   373→    )
   374→
   375→
   376→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   377→# Pipeline wait utility
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→
   380→_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset(
   381→    {
   382→        "pending_review",
   383→        "complete",
   384→        "extraction_failed",
   385→        "grounding_failed",
   386→        "pipeline_failed",
   387→        "dead_letter",
   388→    }
   389→)
   390→
   391→
   392→def wait_for_pipeline(
   393→    api_client: httpx.Client,
   394→    protocol_id: str,
   395→    timeout: int = 180,
   396→    poll_interval: int = 5,
   397→) -> dict:
   398→    """Poll protocol status until it reaches a terminal state.
   399→
   400→    Args:
   401→        api_client: Authenticated httpx client.
   402→        protocol_id: Protocol to monitor.
   403→        timeout: Maximum seconds to wait.
   404→        poll_interval: Seconds between polls.
   405→
   406→    Returns:
   407→        The final protocol response dict.
   408→
   409→    Raises:
   410→        TimeoutError: If timeout is exceeded before reaching terminal state.
   411→    """
   412→    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   413→    while True:
   414→        resp = api_client.get(f"/protocols/{protocol_id}")
   415→        resp.raise_for_status()
   416→        data = resp.json()
   417→
   418→        status = data.get("status", "")
   419→        if status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES:
   420→            logger.info(
   421→                "Pipeline reached terminal status '%s' for %s",
   422→                status,
   423→                protocol_id,
   424→            )
   425→            return data
   426→
   427→        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
   428→            raise TimeoutError(
   429→                f"Protocol {protocol_id} still in '{status}' after {timeout}s"
   430→            )
   431→
   432→        time.sleep(poll_interval)
   433→
```

     1→"""E2E test configuration and fixtures.
     2→
     3→Provides Docker Compose stack detection, automatic skip when the stack is
     4→unavailable, and core fixtures (authenticated HTTP client, direct DB session,
     5→PDF upload factory, database cleanup) for end-to-end tests against the real
     6→running services.
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    Mark any E2E test with ``@pytest.mark.e2e``. When the Docker Compose stack
    10→    is not running, all e2e-marked tests are automatically skipped (exit 0).
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import base64
    16→import logging
    17→import os
    18→import time
    19→from pathlib import Path
    20→from typing import Callable, Generator
    21→
    22→import httpx
    23→import jwt
    24→import pytest
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
